========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/MainActivity.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge

import android.os.Bundle
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
import androidx.activity.viewModels
import androidx.core.splashscreen.SplashScreen.Companion.installSplashScreen
import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
import androidx.core.view.WindowInsetsCompat
import androidx.core.view.WindowInsetsControllerCompat
import com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.AppViewModel
import com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.KeyForgeApp

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    private val viewModel: AppViewModel by viewModels()

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        val splash = installSplashScreen()
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        enableEdgeToEdge()
        hideSystemNavigationBar()
        splash.setKeepOnScreenCondition { viewModel.state.value == null }
        setContent { KeyForgeApp(viewModel) }
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        hideSystemNavigationBar()
    }

    override fun onWindowFocusChanged(hasFocus: Boolean) {
        super.onWindowFocusChanged(hasFocus)
        if (hasFocus) hideSystemNavigationBar()
    }

    private fun hideSystemNavigationBar() {
        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
            systemBarsBehavior =
                WindowInsetsControllerCompat.BEHAVIOR_SHOW_TRANSIENT_BARS_BY_SWIPE
            hide(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.navigationBars())
        }
    }
}


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/data/AppPrefs.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge.data

import android.content.Context
import androidx.datastore.preferences.SharedPreferencesMigration
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.Preferences
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.booleanPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.edit
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.emptyPreferences
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.stringPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.preferencesDataStore
import java.io.IOException
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.catch
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map

private val Context.settingsStore by preferencesDataStore(
    name = "keyforge_settings",
    produceMigrations = { context -> listOf(SharedPreferencesMigration(context, "keyforge_prefs")) }
)

class AppPrefs(context: Context) {
    private val store = context.applicationContext.settingsStore

    private val data: Flow<Preferences> = store.data.catch {
        if (it is IOException) emit(emptyPreferences()) else throw it
    }

    val onboardingDone: Flow<Boolean> = data.map { it[KEY_DONE] ?: false }
    val language: Flow<String> = data.map { it[KEY_LANGUAGE] ?: LANGUAGE_SYSTEM }
    val theme: Flow<String> = data.map { it[KEY_THEME] ?: THEME_SYSTEM }
    val accentColor: Flow<String> = data.map { it[KEY_ACCENT_COLOR] ?: ACCENT_DEFAULT }
    val exportFolderUri: Flow<String?> = data.map { it[KEY_EXPORT_FOLDER_URI] }
    val exportNamePattern: Flow<String> = data.map { it[KEY_EXPORT_NAME_PATTERN] ?: EXPORT_NAME_PATTERN_DEFAULT }
    val exportSaveMode: Flow<String> = data.map { it[KEY_EXPORT_SAVE_MODE] ?: EXPORT_SAVE_ASK }
    val signingDefaultV1: Flow<Boolean> = data.map { it[KEY_SIGNING_DEFAULT_V1] ?: true }
    val signingDefaultV2: Flow<Boolean> = data.map { it[KEY_SIGNING_DEFAULT_V2] ?: true }
    val signingDefaultV3: Flow<Boolean> = data.map { it[KEY_SIGNING_DEFAULT_V3] ?: true }

    suspend fun setOnboardingDone(done: Boolean) {
        store.edit { it[KEY_DONE] = done }
    }

    suspend fun setLanguage(tag: String) {
        store.edit { it[KEY_LANGUAGE] = tag }
    }

    suspend fun setTheme(mode: String) {
        store.edit { it[KEY_THEME] = mode }
    }

    suspend fun setAccentColor(color: String) {
        store.edit { it[KEY_ACCENT_COLOR] = color }
    }

    suspend fun setExportFolderUri(uri: String?) {
        store.edit {
            if (uri.isNullOrBlank()) it.remove(KEY_EXPORT_FOLDER_URI) else it[KEY_EXPORT_FOLDER_URI] = uri
        }
    }

    suspend fun setExportNamePattern(pattern: String) {
        store.edit { it[KEY_EXPORT_NAME_PATTERN] = pattern }
    }

    suspend fun setExportSaveMode(mode: String) {
        store.edit { it[KEY_EXPORT_SAVE_MODE] = mode }
    }

    suspend fun setSigningDefaults(v1: Boolean, v2: Boolean, v3: Boolean) {
        require(v2 || v3) { "At least V2 or V3 must remain enabled" }
        store.edit {
            it[KEY_SIGNING_DEFAULT_V1] = v1
            it[KEY_SIGNING_DEFAULT_V2] = v2
            it[KEY_SIGNING_DEFAULT_V3] = v3
        }
    }

    companion object {
        const val LANGUAGE_SYSTEM = "system"
        const val THEME_SYSTEM = "system"
        const val THEME_LIGHT = "light"
        const val THEME_DARK = "dark"
        const val ACCENT_DEFAULT = "default"
        const val ACCENT_PURPLE = "purple"
        const val ACCENT_BLUE = "blue"
        const val ACCENT_MINT = "mint"
        const val ACCENT_TEAL = "teal"
        const val ACCENT_ORANGE = "orange"
        const val ACCENT_ROSE = "rose"
        const val ACCENT_RED = "red"
        const val EXPORT_SAVE_ASK = "ask"
        const val EXPORT_SAVE_DIRECT = "direct"
        const val EXPORT_NAME_PATTERN_DEFAULT = "{name}_{date}"

        private val KEY_DONE = booleanPreferencesKey("onboarding_done")
        private val KEY_LANGUAGE = stringPreferencesKey("language")
        private val KEY_THEME = stringPreferencesKey("theme")
        private val KEY_ACCENT_COLOR = stringPreferencesKey("accent_color")
        private val KEY_EXPORT_FOLDER_URI = stringPreferencesKey("export_folder_uri")
        private val KEY_EXPORT_NAME_PATTERN = stringPreferencesKey("export_name_pattern")
        private val KEY_EXPORT_SAVE_MODE = stringPreferencesKey("export_save_mode")
        private val KEY_SIGNING_DEFAULT_V1 = booleanPreferencesKey("signing_default_v1")
        private val KEY_SIGNING_DEFAULT_V2 = booleanPreferencesKey("signing_default_v2")
        private val KEY_SIGNING_DEFAULT_V3 = booleanPreferencesKey("signing_default_v3")
    }
}


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/data/EncryptedBackupManager.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge.data

import android.content.Context
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import com.forgehub.keyforge.data.keystore.KeyStoreRepository
import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileInputStream
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.security.SecureRandom
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.zip.ZipEntry
import java.util.zip.ZipInputStream
import java.util.zip.ZipOutputStream
import javax.crypto.Cipher
import javax.crypto.SecretKeyFactory
import javax.crypto.spec.GCMParameterSpec
import javax.crypto.spec.PBEKeySpec
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec

object EncryptedBackupManager {
    data class RestoreResult(
        val restoredKeystores: Int,
        val duplicateKeystores: Int,
        val restoredReports: Int
    )

    private val magic = "KFBK1".toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII)
    private const val ITERATIONS = 210_000
    private const val MAX_BACKUP_BYTES = 256L * 1024L * 1024L
    private const val MAX_RESTORED_BYTES = 512L * 1024L * 1024L
    private val preferenceFiles = listOf(
        "keyforge_key_usage",
        "keyforge_apk_reports",
        "keyforge_signing_presets"
    )

    fun create(context: Context, password: CharArray): ByteArray {
        require(password.size >= 8) { "Backup password must contain at least 8 characters" }
        KeyStoreRepository(context)
        val payload = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        ZipOutputStream(payload).use { zip ->
            val vault = File(context.noBackupFilesDir, "KeyVault")
            var sourceBytes = addDirectory(zip, vault, "keystores", 0L)
            val reports = File(context.filesDir, "apk-reports")
            sourceBytes = addDirectory(zip, reports, "reports", sourceBytes)
            preferenceFiles.forEach { name ->
                val snapshot = encodePreferences(context.getSharedPreferences(name, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)).toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
                sourceBytes += snapshot.size
                require(sourceBytes <= MAX_BACKUP_BYTES) { "Backup is too large" }
                zip.putNextEntry(ZipEntry("preferences/$name.json"))
                zip.write(snapshot)
                zip.closeEntry()
            }
        }
        val plain = payload.toByteArray()
        if (plain.size.toLong() > MAX_BACKUP_BYTES) {
            plain.fill(0)
            throw IllegalArgumentException("Backup is too large")
        }
        val salt = ByteArray(16).also(SecureRandom()::nextBytes)
        val nonce = ByteArray(12).also(SecureRandom()::nextBytes)
        val key = deriveKey(password, salt)
        return try {
            val cipher = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding")
            cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, key, GCMParameterSpec(128, nonce))
            cipher.updateAAD(magic)
            val encrypted = cipher.doFinal(plain)
            ByteArrayOutputStream(magic.size + salt.size + nonce.size + encrypted.size).apply {
                write(magic)
                write(salt)
                write(nonce)
                write(encrypted)
            }.toByteArray()
        } finally {
            plain.fill(0)
        }
    }

    fun restore(context: Context, password: CharArray, encryptedBackup: ByteArray): RestoreResult {
        require(password.size >= 8) { "Enter the backup password" }
        require(encryptedBackup.size.toLong() <= MAX_BACKUP_BYTES + 64L) { "Backup file is too large" }
        val headerSize = magic.size + 16 + 12
        require(encryptedBackup.size > headerSize + 16) { "Invalid or incomplete KeyForge backup" }
        require(encryptedBackup.copyOfRange(0, magic.size).contentEquals(magic)) { "This is not a supported KeyForge backup" }
        val saltStart = magic.size
        val nonceStart = saltStart + 16
        val payloadStart = nonceStart + 12
        val salt = encryptedBackup.copyOfRange(saltStart, nonceStart)
        val nonce = encryptedBackup.copyOfRange(nonceStart, payloadStart)
        val ciphertext = encryptedBackup.copyOfRange(payloadStart, encryptedBackup.size)
        val key = deriveKey(password, salt)
        val plain = try {
            val cipher = Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding")
            cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, key, GCMParameterSpec(128, nonce))
            cipher.updateAAD(magic)
            cipher.doFinal(ciphertext)
        } catch (error: Exception) {
            throw IllegalArgumentException("Incorrect backup password or damaged backup file", error)
        }

        val vault = File(context.noBackupFilesDir, "KeyVault").apply { mkdirs() }
        val reports = File(context.filesDir, "apk-reports").apply { mkdirs() }
        val fileMappings = mutableListOf<Pair<String, String>>()
        val pendingPreferences = mutableListOf<Pair<String, JSONObject>>()
        var restoredKeys = 0
        var duplicateKeys = 0
        var restoredReports = 0
        var restoredBytes = 0L
        try {
            ZipInputStream(ByteArrayInputStream(plain)).use { zip ->
                var entry: ZipEntry? = zip.nextEntry
                var fileCount = 0
                while (entry != null) {
                    if (!entry.isDirectory) {
                        fileCount++
                        require(fileCount <= 10_000) { "Backup contains too many files" }
                        val path = entry.name
                        val content = readLimited(zip, MAX_RESTORED_BYTES - restoredBytes)
                        restoredBytes += content.size
                        require(restoredBytes <= MAX_RESTORED_BYTES) { "Backup contents exceed the allowed size" }
                        when {
                            path.startsWith("keystores/") -> {
                                val originalName = safeLeaf(path.removePrefix("keystores/"))
                                val extension = originalName.substringAfterLast('.', "").lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
                                if (extension in setOf("p12", "pfx") && content.isNotEmpty()) {
                                    val base = originalName.substringBeforeLast('.', originalName)
                                    val target = uniqueTarget(vault, base, extension, content)
                                    if (target == null) {
                                        duplicateKeys++
                                    } else {
                                        FileOutputStream(target).use { it.write(content) }
                                        fileMappings += base to target.nameWithoutExtension
                                        restoredKeys++
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            path.startsWith("reports/") -> {
                                val originalName = safeLeaf(path.removePrefix("reports/"))
                                if (originalName.endsWith(".json", true) && content.isNotEmpty()) {
                                    val target = uniqueFile(reports, originalName, content)
                                    if (target != null) {
                                        FileOutputStream(target).use { it.write(content) }
                                        restoredReports++
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            path.startsWith("preferences/") -> {
                                val prefFile = safeLeaf(path.removePrefix("preferences/"))
                                val prefName = prefFile.removeSuffix(".json")
                                if (prefName in preferenceFiles) {
                                    val json = runCatching { JSONObject(content.toString(Charsets.UTF_8)) }.getOrNull()
                                    if (json != null) pendingPreferences += prefName to json
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    zip.closeEntry()
                    entry = zip.nextEntry
                }
            }
            pendingPreferences.forEach { (name, json) -> restorePreferences(context, name, json) }
            fileMappings.forEach { (oldName, newName) ->
                if (oldName != newName) copyKeystoreMetadata(context, oldName, newName)
            }
        } finally {
            plain.fill(0)
        }
        return RestoreResult(restoredKeys, duplicateKeys, restoredReports)
    }

    private fun addDirectory(zip: ZipOutputStream, directory: File, prefix: String, currentBytes: Long): Long {
        var totalBytes = currentBytes
        directory.listFiles()?.filter { it.isFile }?.forEach { file ->
            val extension = file.extension.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
            val allowed = when (prefix) {
                "keystores" -> extension in setOf("p12", "pfx")
                "reports" -> extension == "json"
                else -> false
            }
            if (!allowed) return@forEach
            require(file.length() <= MAX_BACKUP_BYTES - totalBytes) { "Backup is too large" }
            totalBytes += file.length()
            zip.putNextEntry(ZipEntry("$prefix/${file.name}"))
            FileInputStream(file).use { input -> input.copyTo(zip) }
            zip.closeEntry()
        }
        return totalBytes
    }

    private fun encodePreferences(preferences: android.content.SharedPreferences): String {
        val root = JSONObject()
        preferences.all.forEach { (key, value) ->
            val item = JSONObject()
            when (value) {
                is String -> item.put("type", "string").put("value", value)
                is Boolean -> item.put("type", "boolean").put("value", value)
                is Int -> item.put("type", "int").put("value", value)
                is Long -> item.put("type", "long").put("value", value)
                is Float -> item.put("type", "float").put("value", value.toDouble())
                is Set<*> -> item.put("type", "set").put("value", JSONArray(value.filterIsInstance<String>()))
            }
            if (item.has("type")) root.put(key, item)
        }
        return root.toString()
    }

    private fun restorePreferences(context: Context, name: String, root: JSONObject) {
        val preferences = context.getSharedPreferences(name, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val editor = preferences.edit()
        val existing = preferences.all
        val keys = root.keys()
        while (keys.hasNext()) {
            val key = keys.next()
            val item = root.optJSONObject(key) ?: continue
            val type = item.optString("type")
            when {
                name == "keyforge_key_usage" && key.endsWith("_records") && type == "string" -> {
                    val merged = mergeSigningRecords(preferences.getString(key, null), item.optString("value"))
                    editor.putString(key, merged)
                }
                name == "keyforge_key_usage" && (key.endsWith("_success") || key.endsWith("_failure") || key.endsWith("_last_used")) && type in setOf("long", "int") -> {
                    val restored = item.optLong("value")
                    val current = (existing[key] as? Number)?.toLong() ?: 0L
                    editor.putLong(key, maxOf(current, restored))
                }
                name == "keyforge_signing_presets" && key == "presets" && type == "string" -> {
                    editor.putString(key, mergeSigningPresets(preferences.getString(key, null), item.optString("value")))
                }
                type == "set" -> {
                    val values = item.optJSONArray("value") ?: JSONArray()
                    val restored = buildSet { for (i in 0 until values.length()) add(values.optString(i)) }
                    val current = preferences.getStringSet(key, emptySet<String>()).orEmpty()
                    editor.putStringSet(key, current + restored)
                }
                key !in existing -> when (type) {
                    "string" -> editor.putString(key, item.optString("value"))
                    "boolean" -> editor.putBoolean(key, item.optBoolean("value"))
                    "int" -> editor.putInt(key, item.optInt("value"))
                    "long" -> editor.putLong(key, item.optLong("value"))
                    "float" -> editor.putFloat(key, item.optDouble("value").toFloat())
                }
            }
        }
        editor.apply()
    }

    private fun mergeSigningRecords(currentValue: String?, restoredValue: String): String {
        val records = linkedMapOf<String, JSONObject>()
        listOf(currentValue, restoredValue).forEach { value ->
            val array = runCatching { JSONArray(value ?: "[]") }.getOrDefault(JSONArray())
            for (index in 0 until array.length()) {
                val item = array.optJSONObject(index) ?: continue
                val identity = "${item.optLong("timestamp")}|${item.optString("apkName")}|${item.optBoolean("success")}"
                records[identity] = JSONObject(item.toString())
            }
        }
        return JSONArray().apply {
            records.values.sortedBy { it.optLong("timestamp") }.takeLast(100).forEach { put(it) }
        }.toString()
    }

    private fun mergeSigningPresets(currentValue: String?, restoredValue: String): String {
        val presets = linkedMapOf<String, JSONObject>()
        listOf(currentValue, restoredValue).forEach { value ->
            val array = runCatching { JSONArray(value ?: "[]") }.getOrDefault(JSONArray())
            for (index in 0 until array.length()) {
                val item = array.optJSONObject(index) ?: continue
                val name = item.optString("name").trim()
                if (name.isNotEmpty()) presets.putIfAbsent(name.lowercase(Locale.ROOT), JSONObject(item.toString()))
            }
        }
        return JSONArray().apply { presets.values.take(30).forEach { put(it) } }.toString()
    }

    private fun deriveKey(password: CharArray, salt: ByteArray): SecretKeySpec {
        val spec = PBEKeySpec(password, salt, ITERATIONS, 256)
        return try {
            val bytes = SecretKeyFactory.getInstance("PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256").generateSecret(spec).encoded
            try { SecretKeySpec(bytes, "AES") } finally { bytes.fill(0) }
        } finally {
            spec.clearPassword()
        }
    }

    private fun safeLeaf(value: String): String {
        require(value.isNotBlank() && value != "." && value != "..") { "Invalid backup entry" }
        require('/' !in value && '\\' !in value && value.none { it.code < 32 }) { "Invalid backup entry" }
        return value
    }

    private fun uniqueTarget(directory: File, base: String, extension: String, content: ByteArray): File? {
        var candidate = File(directory, "$base.$extension")
        if (!candidate.exists()) return candidate
        if (sha256(candidate.readBytes()).contentEquals(sha256(content))) return null
        var index = 2
        while (index < 10_000) {
            candidate = File(directory, "${base}_restored_$index.$extension")
            if (!candidate.exists()) return candidate
            if (sha256(candidate.readBytes()).contentEquals(sha256(content))) return null
            index++
        }
        throw IllegalStateException("Unable to find a safe filename for a restored keystore")
    }

    private fun uniqueFile(directory: File, name: String, content: ByteArray): File? {
        var candidate = File(directory, name)
        if (!candidate.exists()) return candidate
        if (sha256(candidate.readBytes()).contentEquals(sha256(content))) return null
        val base = name.substringBeforeLast('.', name)
        val extension = name.substringAfterLast('.', "")
        var index = 2
        while (index < 10_000) {
            candidate = File(directory, "${base}_restored_$index.$extension")
            if (!candidate.exists()) return candidate
            if (sha256(candidate.readBytes()).contentEquals(sha256(content))) return null
            index++
        }
        throw IllegalStateException("Unable to find a safe filename for a restored report")
    }

    private fun copyKeystoreMetadata(context: Context, sourceName: String, targetName: String) {
        val preferences = context.getSharedPreferences("keyforge_key_usage", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val sourceHash = metadataHash(sourceName)
        val targetHash = metadataHash(targetName)
        val editor = preferences.edit()
        listOf("display", "description").forEach { field ->
            preferences.getString("keystore_${sourceHash}_$field", null)?.let {
                editor.putString("keystore_${targetHash}_$field", it)
            }
        }
        editor.apply()
    }

    private fun metadataHash(name: String): String = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
        .digest(name.toByteArray())
        .take(12)
        .joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }

    private fun sha256(bytes: ByteArray): ByteArray = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(bytes)

    private fun readLimited(input: ZipInputStream, remaining: Long): ByteArray {
        require(remaining >= 0L) { "Backup contents exceed the allowed size" }
        val output = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        val buffer = ByteArray(8192)
        var total = 0L
        while (true) {
            val count = input.read(buffer)
            if (count < 0) break
            total += count
            require(total <= remaining && total <= MAX_RESTORED_BYTES) { "Backup contents exceed the allowed size" }
            output.write(buffer, 0, count)
        }
        return output.toByteArray()
    }
}


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/data/FileExportManager.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge.data

import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import android.provider.DocumentsContract
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

object FileExportManager {
    fun buildFileName(
        pattern: String,
        name: String,
        version: String = "",
        packageName: String = "",
        extension: String
    ): String {
        val now = Date()
        val baseName = removeKnownExtension(name)
        val values = mapOf(
            "{name}" to baseName,
            "{app}" to baseName,
            "{version}" to version,
            "{package}" to packageName,
            "{date}" to SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", Locale.US).format(now),
            "{time}" to SimpleDateFormat("HH-mm-ss", Locale.US).format(now)
        )
        var result = pattern.ifBlank { AppPrefs.EXPORT_NAME_PATTERN_DEFAULT }
        values.forEach { (token, value) -> result = result.replace(token, value, ignoreCase = true) }
        result = result
            .replace(Regex("[\\\\/:*?\"<>|\\u0000-\\u001F]"), "-")
            .replace(Regex("\\s+"), "_")
            .trim('.', ' ', '_', '-')
            .take(120)
            .ifBlank { "KeyForge_Export" }
        val suffix = extension.removePrefix(".").lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
        if (!result.endsWith(".$suffix", ignoreCase = true)) result = "$result.$suffix"
        return result
    }

    private fun removeKnownExtension(name: String): String {
        val extension = name.substringAfterLast('.', "").lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
        return if (extension in setOf("apk", "jks", "keystore", "p12", "pfx", "pdf", "html", "htm", "txt", "png", "jpg", "jpeg", "webp", "xml", "bin")) {
            name.substringBeforeLast('.', name)
        } else {
            name
        }
    }

    fun folderLabel(folderUri: Uri?): String {
        if (folderUri == null) return ""
        return runCatching {
            val documentId = DocumentsContract.getTreeDocumentId(folderUri)
            val lastPart = documentId.substringAfterLast('/').substringAfterLast(':')
            Uri.decode(lastPart).ifBlank { "Selected folder" }
        }.getOrDefault("Selected folder")
    }

    fun persistFolderPermission(context: Context, uri: Uri) {
        runCatching {
            context.contentResolver.takePersistableUriPermission(
                uri,
                android.content.Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or android.content.Intent.FLAG_GRANT_WRITE_URI_PERMISSION
            )
        }
    }

    fun createFileInFolder(
        context: Context,
        folderUri: Uri,
        mimeType: String,
        displayName: String
    ): Uri {
        val resolver = context.contentResolver
        val treeDocumentId = DocumentsContract.getTreeDocumentId(folderUri)
        val directoryUri = DocumentsContract.buildDocumentUriUsingTree(folderUri, treeDocumentId)
        val safeName = displayName.replace(Regex("[\\\\/:*?\"<>|\\u0000-\\u001F]"), "-").take(150)
        val availableName = uniqueName(context, folderUri, treeDocumentId, safeName)
        return DocumentsContract.createDocument(resolver, directoryUri, mimeType, availableName)
            ?: throw IllegalStateException("Unable to create a file in the selected folder")
    }

    private fun uniqueName(
        context: android.content.Context,
        treeUri: Uri,
        directoryId: String,
        requestedName: String
    ): String {
        val existing = runCatching {
            val childrenUri = DocumentsContract.buildChildDocumentsUriUsingTree(treeUri, directoryId)
            context.contentResolver.query(
                childrenUri,
                arrayOf(DocumentsContract.Document.COLUMN_DISPLAY_NAME),
                null,
                null,
                null
            )?.use { cursor ->
                buildSet {
                    val index = cursor.getColumnIndex(DocumentsContract.Document.COLUMN_DISPLAY_NAME)
                    if (index >= 0) while (cursor.moveToNext()) add(cursor.getString(index))
                }
            }.orEmpty()
        }.getOrDefault(emptySet())
        if (requestedName !in existing) return requestedName
        val dot = requestedName.lastIndexOf('.')
        val base = if (dot > 0) requestedName.substring(0, dot) else requestedName
        val extension = if (dot > 0) requestedName.substring(dot) else ""
        var index = 2
        while ("$base ($index)$extension" in existing) index++
        return "$base ($index)$extension"
    }
}


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/data/SaveDocumentContract.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge.data

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.provider.DocumentsContract
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContract

data class SaveDocumentRequest(
    val mimeType: String,
    val displayName: String,
    val initialFolder: Uri? = null
)

class SaveDocumentContract : ActivityResultContract<SaveDocumentRequest, Uri?>() {
    override fun createIntent(context: Context, input: SaveDocumentRequest): Intent {
        return Intent(Intent.ACTION_CREATE_DOCUMENT).apply {
            addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_OPENABLE)
            type = input.mimeType
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TITLE, input.displayName)
            input.initialFolder?.let { putExtra(DocumentsContract.EXTRA_INITIAL_URI, it) }
        }
    }

    override fun parseResult(resultCode: Int, intent: Intent?): Uri? {
        return if (resultCode == Activity.RESULT_OK) intent?.data else null
    }
}

    fun toHtml(report: ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport): String {

        val permissionGroups = permissionGroups(report.requestedPermissions)
        val date = date(report.inspectedAt)
        val status = if (report.verified) "Signature verified" else "Signature not verified"
        val permissionRows = permissionGroups.entries.joinToString("") { (name, count) ->
            val percent = if (report.requestedPermissions.isEmpty()) 0 else count * 100 / report.requestedPermissions.size
