    fun writePdf(report: ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport, output: OutputStream) {
        val document = PdfDocument()
        try {
            drawCoverPage(document, report)
            drawDetailsPage(document, report)
            if (report.requestedPermissions.isNotEmpty() || report.errors.isNotEmpty() || report.warnings.isNotEmpty()) {
                drawFindingsPages(document, report)
            }
            document.writeTo(output)
        } finally {
            document.close()
        }
    }

    private fun drawCoverPage(document: PdfDocument, report: ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport) {
        val page = document.startPage(PdfDocument.PageInfo.Builder(595, 842, 1).create())
        val canvas = page.canvas
        canvas.drawColor(parseColor("#F4F6FA"))
        text(canvas, "KEYFORGE", 42f, 48f, 12f, "#586782", true)
        text(canvas, "APPLICATION INSPECTION REPORT", 553f, 48f, 9f, "#7D8798", true, Paint.Align.RIGHT)
        card(canvas, 34f, 78f, 561f, 398f)
        val icon = runCatching { Base64.decode(report.iconBase64, Base64.DEFAULT) }.getOrNull()?.let { BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(it, 0, it.size) }
        if (icon != null) {
            val destination = RectF(58f, 105f, 150f, 197f)
            canvas.drawRoundRect(destination, 20f, 20f, paint("#EDF1F8", true))
            val save = canvas.save()
            val clip = Path().apply { addRoundRect(destination, 18f, 18f, Path.Direction.CW) }
            canvas.clipPath(clip)
            canvas.drawBitmap(icon, null, destination, Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG))
            canvas.restoreToCount(save)
            icon.recycle()
        } else {
            canvas.drawRoundRect(RectF(58f, 105f, 150f, 197f), 20f, 20f, paint("#E8EDF8", true))
            text(canvas, report.appName.take(1).uppercase(Locale.ROOT).ifBlank { "A" }, 104f, 163f, 32f, "#6B80B4", true, Paint.Align.CENTER)
        }
        text(canvas, "APPLICATION PROFILE", 172f, 121f, 9f, "#7D8798", true)
        textWrapped(canvas, report.appName.ifBlank { report.fileName }, 172f, 151f, 370f, 27f, "#242C3B", true, 2)
        textWrapped(canvas, report.packageName.ifBlank { "Package unavailable" }, 172f, 198f, 370f, 11f, "#778195", false, 2)
        text(canvas, "${report.versionName.ifBlank { "Unknown version" }}  ·  ${report.versionCode}", 172f, 247f, 11f, "#68758B", true)
        canvas.drawRoundRect(RectF(58f, 286f, 333f, 323f), 18f, 18f, paint("#EEF3FC", true))
        canvas.drawCircle(79f, 304f, 8f, paint("#6489D7", true))
        text(canvas, "✓", 79f, 308f, 11f, "#FFFFFF", true, Paint.Align.CENTER)
        text(canvas, "INSPECTED BY KEYFORGE", 96f, 308f, 10f, "#5476B5", true)
        text(canvas, "${date(report.inspectedAt)}   ·   ${report.operationType}", 58f, 355f, 10f, "#7D8798", false)
        text(canvas, "REPORT ID  ${report.reportId}", 58f, 377f, 9f, "#7D8798", true)
        val statY = 425f
        statCard(canvas, 34f, statY, 164f, "APK SIZE", formatBytes(report.fileSize))
        statCard(canvas, 215f, statY, 164f, "SIGNATURE", if (report.verified) "Verified" else "Not verified")
        statCard(canvas, 396f, statY, 165f, "PERMISSIONS", report.requestedPermissions.size.toString())
        card(canvas, 34f, 546f, 561f, 782f)
        text(canvas, "SIGNATURE SCHEMES", 56f, 579f, 12f, "#30394A", true)
        val schemes = listOf("V1" to report.v1, "V2" to report.v2, "V3" to report.v3)
        val count = schemes.count { it.second }
        donut(canvas, 137f, 682f, 48f, count)
        text(canvas, "$count / 3", 137f, 686f, 19f, "#30394A", true, Paint.Align.CENTER)
        schemes.forEachIndexed { index, item ->
            val y = 632f + index * 38f
            text(canvas, item.first, 226f, y, 12f, "#30394A", true)
            text(canvas, if (item.second) "Detected" else "Not detected", 512f, y, 11f, if (item.second) "#3D987F" else "#929AAA", false, Paint.Align.RIGHT)
            canvas.drawLine(226f, y + 12f, 512f, y + 12f, paint("#E9ECF2", false, 1f))
        }
        textWrapped(canvas, "The KeyForge mark identifies the report generator. Signature verification does not establish that an application is malware-free or endorsed by its publisher.", 56f, 751f, 510f, 10f, "#7D8798", false, 2)
        footer(canvas, 1)
        document.finishPage(page)
    }

    private fun drawDetailsPage(document: PdfDocument, report: ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport) {
        val page = document.startPage(PdfDocument.PageInfo.Builder(595, 842, 2).create())
        val canvas = page.canvas
        canvas.drawColor(parseColor("#F4F6FA"))
        text(canvas, "APPLICATION DETAILS", 40f, 52f, 20f, "#242C3B", true)
        text(canvas, report.appName.ifBlank { report.fileName }, 40f, 76f, 10f, "#778195", false)
        card(canvas, 34f, 98f, 561f, 434f)
        text(canvas, "PACKAGE INFORMATION", 56f, 130f, 12f, "#30394A", true)
        val details = listOf(
            "APK file" to report.fileName,
            "Package ID" to report.packageName,
            "Version name" to report.versionName,
            "Version code" to report.versionCode.toString(),
            "Minimum SDK" to report.minSdk.takeIf { it > 0 }?.toString().orEmpty().ifBlank { "Unknown" },
            "Target SDK" to report.targetSdk.takeIf { it > 0 }?.toString().orEmpty().ifBlank { "Unknown" },
            "Debuggable" to yesNo(report.debuggable),
            "Cleartext traffic" to yesNo(report.cleartextTrafficAllowed),
            "Backup allowed" to yesNo(report.backupAllowed),
            "Native ABIs" to report.nativeAbis.joinToString().ifBlank { "None" }
        )
        details.forEachIndexed { index, row ->
            val y = 160f + index * 25f
            text(canvas, row.first, 56f, y, 10f, "#778195", false)
            textWrapped(canvas, row.second, 220f, y, 330f, 10f, "#30394A", true, 2)
            canvas.drawLine(56f, y + 9f, 550f, y + 9f, paint("#EDF0F5", false, 1f))
        }
        card(canvas, 34f, 455f, 561f, 567f)
        text(canvas, "FILE FINGERPRINT · SHA-256", 56f, 488f, 11f, "#30394A", true)
        textWrapped(canvas, report.fileSha256, 56f, 520f, 500f, 10f, "#66738B", false, 2)
        card(canvas, 34f, 588f, 561f, 804f)
        text(canvas, "SIGNER CERTIFICATES", 56f, 620f, 12f, "#30394A", true)
        if (report.signers.isEmpty()) {
            text(canvas, "No signer certificate details available.", 56f, 653f, 11f, "#778195", false)
        } else {
            report.signers.take(2).forEachIndexed { index, signer ->
                val y = 653f + index * 72f
                text(canvas, "Signer ${index + 1} · ${signer.signatureAlgorithm}", 56f, y, 10f, "#536A9F", true)
                textWrapped(canvas, signer.subject, 56f, y + 18f, 500f, 9f, "#30394A", false, 2)
                textWrapped(canvas, "SHA-256  ${signer.sha256}", 56f, y + 39f, 500f, 8f, "#778195", false, 2)
            }
        }
        footer(canvas, 2)
        document.finishPage(page)
    }

    private fun drawFindingsPages(document: PdfDocument, report: ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport) {
        data class Finding(val title: String, val value: String = "", val heading: Boolean = false, val error: Boolean = false)
        val findings = buildList {
            if (report.requestedPermissions.isNotEmpty()) {
                add(Finding("REQUESTED PERMISSIONS (${report.requestedPermissions.size})", heading = true))
                report.requestedPermissions.forEach { add(Finding("", it)) }
            }
            if (report.errors.isNotEmpty()) {
                add(Finding("VERIFICATION ERRORS (${report.errors.size})", heading = true, error = true))
                report.errors.forEach { add(Finding("", it, error = true)) }
            }
            if (report.warnings.isNotEmpty()) {
                add(Finding("NOTICES (${report.warnings.size})", heading = true))
                report.warnings.forEach { add(Finding("", it)) }
            }
        }
        val linesPerPage = 22
        findings.chunked(linesPerPage).forEachIndexed { pageIndex, chunk ->
            val pageNumber = pageIndex + 3
            val page = document.startPage(PdfDocument.PageInfo.Builder(595, 842, pageNumber).create())
            val canvas = page.canvas
            canvas.drawColor(parseColor("#F4F6FA"))
            text(canvas, "PERMISSIONS & FINDINGS", 40f, 52f, 20f, "#242C3B", true)
            text(canvas, "Application declarations and verifier messages", 40f, 76f, 10f, "#778195", false)
            val cardBottom = (132f + (chunk.size - 1).coerceAtLeast(0) * 29f + 34f).coerceIn(190f, 788f)
            card(canvas, 34f, 98f, 561f, cardBottom)
            chunk.forEachIndexed { index, finding ->
                val y = 132f + index * 29f
                if (finding.heading) {
                    text(canvas, finding.title, 56f, y, 10f, if (finding.error) "#B45E62" else "#536A9F", true)
                } else {
                    val bullet = if (finding.error) "• " else "– "
                    textWrapped(canvas, bullet + finding.value, 56f, y, 500f, 8.5f, "#536078", false, 2)
                }
                if (index < chunk.lastIndex) canvas.drawLine(56f, y + 13f, 550f, y + 13f, paint("#EEF0F5", false, 0.7f))
            }
            footer(canvas, pageNumber)
            document.finishPage(page)
        }
    }

    private fun permissionGroups(permissions: List<String>): Map<String, Int> {
        val labels = linkedMapOf("Network" to 0, "Location" to 0, "Media" to 0, "Device" to 0, "Other" to 0)
        permissions.forEach { permission ->
            val value = permission.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)
            val key = when {
                "INTERNET" in value || "NETWORK" in value || "WIFI" in value -> "Network"
                "LOCATION" in value || "GPS" in value -> "Location"
                "CAMERA" in value || "AUDIO" in value || "MEDIA" in value || "STORAGE" in value || "PHOTO" in value || "VIDEO" in value -> "Media"
                "PHONE" in value || "CONTACT" in value || "SMS" in value || "BLUETOOTH" in value || "SENSOR" in value -> "Device"
                else -> "Other"
            }
            labels[key] = labels.getValue(key) + 1
        }
        return labels.filterValues { it > 0 }
    }

    private fun detail(label: String, value: String) = "<dt>${escape(label)}</dt><dd>${escape(value.ifBlank { "—" })}</dd>"
    private fun escape(value: String): String = value.replace("&", "&amp;").replace("<", "&lt;").replace(">", "&gt;").replace("\"", "&quot;").replace("'", "&#39;")
    private fun yesNo(value: Boolean) = if (value) "Yes" else "No"
    private fun date(value: Long) = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).format(Date(value))
    private fun formatBytes(value: Long): String = when {
        value >= 1_073_741_824L -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f GB", value / 1_073_741_824.0)
        value >= 1_048_576L -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f MB", value / 1_048_576.0)
        value >= 1024L -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f KB", value / 1024.0)
        else -> "$value B"
    }

    private fun card(canvas: Canvas, left: Float, top: Float, right: Float, bottom: Float) {
        canvas.drawRoundRect(RectF(left, top, right, bottom), 19f, 19f, paint("#FFFFFF", true))
        canvas.drawRoundRect(RectF(left, top, right, bottom), 19f, 19f, paint("#E6EAF1", false, 1f))
    }

    private fun statCard(canvas: Canvas, left: Float, top: Float, width: Float, label: String, value: String) {
        card(canvas, left, top, left + width, top + 92f)
        text(canvas, label, left + 16f, top + 28f, 9f, "#818A9B", true)
        textWrapped(canvas, value, left + 16f, top + 60f, width - 30f, 16f, "#30394A", true, 1)
    }

    private fun donut(canvas: Canvas, centerX: Float, centerY: Float, radius: Float, count: Int) {
        val bounds = RectF(centerX - radius, centerY - radius, centerX + radius, centerY + radius)
        canvas.drawArc(bounds, 0f, 360f, false, paint("#EDF0F5", false, 11f))
        if (count > 0) canvas.drawArc(bounds, -90f, 360f * count / 3f, false, paint("#7795D7", false, 11f).apply { strokeCap = Paint.Cap.ROUND })
    }

    private fun footer(canvas: Canvas, page: Int) {
        text(canvas, "KEYFORGE  ·  LOCALLY GENERATED REPORT", 38f, 821f, 8f, "#8992A2", true)
        text(canvas, page.toString(), 558f, 821f, 8f, "#8992A2", false, Paint.Align.RIGHT)
    }

    private fun textWrapped(canvas: Canvas, value: String, x: Float, y: Float, maxWidth: Float, size: Float, hex: String, bold: Boolean, maxLines: Int) {
        val p = paint(hex, true, size).apply { typeface = if (bold) Typeface.create("sans-serif", Typeface.BOLD) else Typeface.create("sans-serif", Typeface.NORMAL) }
        val words = value.split(Regex("\\s+"))
        var line = ""
        var lineNumber = 0
        words.forEach { word ->
            val next = if (line.isEmpty()) word else "$line $word"
            if (p.measureText(next) > maxWidth && line.isNotEmpty()) {
                canvas.drawText(line, x, y + lineNumber * (size + 4f), p)
                lineNumber++
                line = word
            } else line = next
        }
        if (line.isNotEmpty() && lineNumber < maxLines) canvas.drawText(line, x, y + lineNumber * (size + 4f), p)
    }

    private fun text(canvas: Canvas, value: String, x: Float, y: Float, size: Float, hex: String, bold: Boolean, align: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.LEFT) {
        val p = paint(hex, true, size).apply {
            typeface = if (bold) Typeface.create("sans-serif", Typeface.BOLD) else Typeface.create("sans-serif", Typeface.NORMAL)
            textAlign = align
        }
        canvas.drawText(value.take(180), x, y, p)
    }

    private fun paint(hex: String, fill: Boolean, strokeWidth: Float = 1f) = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = parseColor(hex)
        style = if (fill) Paint.Style.FILL else Paint.Style.STROKE
        this.strokeWidth = strokeWidth
    }

    private fun parseColor(hex: String): Int = android.graphics.Color.parseColor(hex)
}


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/data/keystore/ApkToolsRepository.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge.data.keystore

import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.os.Build
import android.util.Base64
import com.android.apksig.ApkSigner
import com.android.apksig.ApkVerifier
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileInputStream
import java.security.KeyStore
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.security.PrivateKey
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.zip.ZipFile

class ApkToolsRepository(private val context: Context) {
    data class SignerCertificate(
        val subject: String,
        val issuer: String,
        val serialNumber: String,
        val signatureAlgorithm: String,
        val validFrom: String,
        val validUntil: String,
        val sha256: String
    )

    data class ApkReport(
        val fileName: String,
        val fileSize: Long,
        val fileSha256: String,
        val appName: String,
        val packageName: String,
        val versionName: String,
        val versionCode: Long,
        val minSdk: Int,
        val targetSdk: Int,
        val debuggable: Boolean,
        val cleartextTrafficAllowed: Boolean,
        val backupAllowed: Boolean,
        val requestedPermissions: List<String>,
        val nativeAbis: List<String>,
        val verified: Boolean,
        val v1: Boolean,
        val v2: Boolean,
        val v3: Boolean,
        val signers: List<SignerCertificate>,
        val errors: List<String>,
        val warnings: List<String>,
        val iconBase64: String = "",
        val reportId: String = "",
        val inspectedAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
        val operationType: String = "INSPECTION"
    ) {
        fun toText(): String = buildString {
            appendLine("KEYFORGE APK REPORT")
            appendLine("Inspected by KeyForge")
            appendLine("Report ID: ${reportId.ifBlank { "KF-${fileSha256.take(12)}" }}")
            appendLine("Inspection date: ${SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).format(Date(inspectedAt))}")
            appendLine("Operation: $operationType")
            appendLine("File: $fileName")
            appendLine("Size: $fileSize bytes")
            appendLine("SHA-256: $fileSha256")
            appendLine("App: ${appName.ifBlank { "Unknown" }}")
            appendLine("Package: ${packageName.ifBlank { "Unknown" }}")
            appendLine("Version: ${versionName.ifBlank { "Unknown" }} ($versionCode)")
            appendLine("Min SDK: ${minSdk.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: "Unknown"}")
            appendLine("Target SDK: ${targetSdk.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: "Unknown"}")
            appendLine("Debuggable: $debuggable")
            appendLine("Cleartext traffic allowed: $cleartextTrafficAllowed")
            appendLine("Backup allowed: $backupAllowed")
            appendLine("Native ABIs: ${nativeAbis.joinToString().ifBlank { "None" }}")
            appendLine("Requested permissions:")
            requestedPermissions.forEach { appendLine("- $it") }
            appendLine("Signature verified: $verified")
            appendLine("V1: $v1 | V2: $v2 | V3: $v3")
            signers.forEachIndexed { index, signer ->
                appendLine("Signer ${index + 1}")
                appendLine("Subject: ${signer.subject}")
                appendLine("Issuer: ${signer.issuer}")
                appendLine("Serial: ${signer.serialNumber}")
                appendLine("Signature: ${signer.signatureAlgorithm}")
                appendLine("Valid: ${signer.validFrom} - ${signer.validUntil}")
                appendLine("Certificate SHA-256: ${signer.sha256}")
            }
            if (errors.isNotEmpty()) {
                appendLine("Errors")
                errors.forEach { appendLine("- $it") }
            }
            if (warnings.isNotEmpty()) {
                appendLine("Warnings")
                warnings.forEach { appendLine("- $it") }
            }
        }
    }

    data class SigningResult(
        val outputFile: File,
        val certificateSha256: String,
        val verification: ApkReport
    )

    fun sign(
        inputApk: File,
        keystoreFile: File,
        alias: String,
        password: CharArray,
        outputApk: File,
        v1: Boolean,
        v2: Boolean,
        v3: Boolean
    ): SigningResult {
        require(inputApk.isFile && inputApk.length() > 0L) { "Selected APK is empty or unavailable" }
        require(outputApk.canonicalFile != inputApk.canonicalFile) { "Output APK must be different from the source APK" }
        require(v1 || v2 || v3) { "Enable at least one signature scheme" }
        require(v2 || v3) { "Enable V2 or V3 for modern Android compatibility" }
        require(alias.isNotBlank()) { "Select a private-key alias" }

        val store = KeyStore.getInstance("PKCS12")
        FileInputStream(keystoreFile).use { store.load(it, password) }
        require(store.containsAlias(alias) && store.isKeyEntry(alias)) { "The selected alias does not contain a private key" }
        val privateKey = store.getKey(alias, password) as? PrivateKey
            ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("Private key could not be loaded")
        val chain = store.getCertificateChain(alias)?.mapNotNull { it as? X509Certificate }.orEmpty()
        require(chain.isNotEmpty()) { "The selected alias has no X.509 certificate chain" }
        val leaf = chain.first()

        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
        val signerConfig = ApkSigner.SignerConfig.Builder(alias, privateKey, chain).build()
        outputApk.parentFile?.mkdirs()
        outputApk.delete()
        try {
            ApkSigner.Builder(listOf(signerConfig))
                .setInputApk(inputApk)
                .setOutputApk(outputApk)
                .setOtherSignersSignaturesPreserved(false)
                .setV1SigningEnabled(v1)
                .setV2SigningEnabled(v2)
                .setV3SigningEnabled(v3)
                .setV4SigningEnabled(false)
                .build()
                .sign()

            require(outputApk.isFile && outputApk.length() > 0L) { "The signing process did not produce an APK" }
            val report = verify(outputApk)
            require(report.verified) {
                report.errors.firstOrNull()?.let { "Signed APK verification failed: $it" }
                    ?: "Signed APK verification failed"
            }
            val verifiedSigner = report.signers.firstOrNull()?.sha256
            require(!verifiedSigner.isNullOrBlank()) { "The signed APK has no verifiable signer certificate" }
            require(verifiedSigner.equals(fingerprint(leaf.encoded), ignoreCase = true)) {
                "The output APK signer does not match the selected keystore certificate"
            }
            return SigningResult(outputApk, verifiedSigner, report)
        } catch (error: Exception) {
            outputApk.delete()
            throw error
        }
    }

    fun verify(apk: File): ApkReport {
        require(apk.isFile && apk.length() > 0L) { "Selected APK is empty or unavailable" }
        val result = ApkVerifier.Builder(apk).build().verify()
        val packageInfo = runCatching {
            val signatureFlags = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 28) PackageManager.GET_SIGNING_CERTIFICATES else PackageManager.GET_SIGNATURES
            val flags = signatureFlags or PackageManager.GET_PERMISSIONS
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            context.packageManager.getPackageArchiveInfo(apk.absolutePath, flags)
        }.getOrNull()
        val appInfo = packageInfo?.applicationInfo
        if (appInfo != null) {
            appInfo.sourceDir = apk.absolutePath
            appInfo.publicSourceDir = apk.absolutePath
        }
        val signers = result.signerCertificates.map { certificateInfo(it) }
        val nativeAbis = readNativeAbis(apk)
        val fileSha256 = digest(apk)
        val inspectedAt = System.currentTimeMillis()
        return ApkReport(
            fileName = apk.name,
            fileSize = apk.length(),
            fileSha256 = fileSha256,
            appName = if (appInfo != null) runCatching { context.packageManager.getApplicationLabel(appInfo).toString() }.getOrDefault("") else "",
            packageName = packageInfo?.packageName.orEmpty(),
            versionName = packageInfo?.versionName.orEmpty(),
            versionCode = packageInfo?.let { versionCode(it) } ?: 0L,
            minSdk = appInfo?.minSdkVersion ?: 0,
            targetSdk = appInfo?.targetSdkVersion ?: 0,
            debuggable = appInfo?.let { it.flags and android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo.FLAG_DEBUGGABLE != 0 } ?: false,
            cleartextTrafficAllowed = readCleartextTraffic(apk, packageInfo?.applicationInfo?.targetSdkVersion ?: 0),
            backupAllowed = appInfo?.let { it.flags and android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo.FLAG_ALLOW_BACKUP != 0 } ?: false,
            requestedPermissions = packageInfo?.requestedPermissions?.toList().orEmpty(),
            nativeAbis = nativeAbis,
            verified = result.isVerified,
            v1 = result.isVerifiedUsingV1Scheme,
            v2 = result.isVerifiedUsingV2Scheme,
            v3 = result.isVerifiedUsingV3Scheme,
            signers = signers,
            errors = result.errors.map { it.toString() },
            warnings = result.warnings.map { it.toString() },
            iconBase64 = applicationIconBase64(appInfo),
            reportId = "KF-${SimpleDateFormat("yyyyMMdd-HHmmss-SSS", Locale.US).format(Date(inspectedAt))}-${fileSha256.take(6)}",
            inspectedAt = inspectedAt
        )
    }

    fun sha256(file: File): String = digest(file)



    private fun applicationIconBase64(appInfo: android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo?): String {
        if (appInfo == null) return ""
        return runCatching {
            val drawable = context.packageManager.getApplicationIcon(appInfo)
            val width = 192
            val height = 192
            val bitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(width, height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
            val canvas = Canvas(bitmap)
            drawable.setBounds(0, 0, width, height)
            drawable.draw(canvas)
            val output = java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream()
            bitmap.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.PNG, 100, output)
            bitmap.recycle()
            Base64.encodeToString(output.toByteArray(), Base64.NO_WRAP)
        }.getOrDefault("")
    }

    private fun readCleartextTraffic(apk: File, targetSdk: Int): Boolean {
        val explicitValue = runCatching {
            ZipFile(apk).use { zip ->
                val manifest = zip.getEntry("AndroidManifest.xml") ?: return@use null
                zip.getInputStream(manifest).use { input ->
                    readCleartextTrafficAttribute(input.readBytes())
                }
            }
        }.getOrNull()
        return explicitValue ?: (targetSdk <= 27)
    }

    private fun readCleartextTrafficAttribute(xml: ByteArray): Boolean? {
        if (xml.size < 8) return null

        fun u16(offset: Int): Int {
            require(offset >= 0 && offset + 2 <= xml.size)
            return (xml[offset].toInt() and 0xFF) or
                ((xml[offset + 1].toInt() and 0xFF) shl 8)
        }

        fun u32(offset: Int): Long {
            require(offset >= 0 && offset + 4 <= xml.size)
            return u16(offset).toLong() or (u16(offset + 2).toLong() shl 16)
        }

        fun readLength8(start: Int): Pair<Int, Int> {
            val first = xml[start].toInt() and 0xFF
            return if ((first and 0x80) != 0) {
                (((first and 0x7F) shl 8) or (xml[start + 1].toInt() and 0xFF)) to (start + 2)
            } else {
                first to (start + 1)
            }
        }

        fun readLength16(start: Int): Pair<Int, Int> {
            val first = u16(start)
            return if ((first and 0x8000) != 0) {
                (((first and 0x7FFF) shl 16) or u16(start + 2)) to (start + 4)
            } else {
                first to (start + 2)
            }
        }

        fun readStringPool(chunkOffset: Int, chunkSize: Int): List<String?> {
            require(chunkSize >= 28)
            val stringCount = u32(chunkOffset + 8).toInt()
            val flags = u32(chunkOffset + 16).toInt()
            val stringsStart = u32(chunkOffset + 20).toInt()
            require(stringCount in 0..200_000)
            require(chunkOffset.toLong() + 28L + stringCount.toLong() * 4L <= chunkOffset.toLong() + chunkSize.toLong())
            val stringDataStart = chunkOffset + stringsStart
            val isUtf8 = (flags and 0x100) != 0
            return List(stringCount) { index ->
                val relativeOffset = u32(chunkOffset + 28 + index * 4).toInt()
                val start = stringDataStart + relativeOffset
                require(start >= chunkOffset && start < chunkOffset + chunkSize)
                if (isUtf8) {
                    val (_, afterUtf16Length) = readLength8(start)
                    val (byteLength, dataStart) = readLength8(afterUtf16Length)
                    require(byteLength >= 0 && dataStart.toLong() + byteLength <= chunkOffset.toLong() + chunkSize)
                    String(xml, dataStart, byteLength, Charsets.UTF_8)
                } else {
                    val (charLength, dataStart) = readLength16(start)
                    val byteLength = charLength.toLong() * 2L
                    require(charLength >= 0 && dataStart.toLong() + byteLength <= chunkOffset.toLong() + chunkSize)
                    String(xml, dataStart, byteLength.toInt(), Charsets.UTF_16LE)
                }
            }
        }

        if (u16(0) != 0x0003 || u16(2) < 8 || u32(4) > xml.size.toLong()) return null

        var offset = u16(2)
        var strings: List<String?> = emptyList()
        while (offset + 8 <= xml.size) {
            val type = u16(offset)
            val headerSize = u16(offset + 2)
            val chunkSize = u32(offset + 4).toInt()
            require(headerSize >= 8 && chunkSize >= headerSize && offset.toLong() + chunkSize.toLong() <= xml.size.toLong())

            when (type) {
                0x0001 -> strings = readStringPool(offset, chunkSize)
                0x0102 -> {
                    require(headerSize >= 16 && offset + 36 <= xml.size)
                    val elementNameIndex = u32(offset + 20).toInt()
                    val elementName = strings.getOrNull(elementNameIndex)
                    if (elementName == "application") {
                        val attributeStart = u16(offset + 24)
                        val attributeSize = u16(offset + 26)
                        val attributeCount = u16(offset + 28)
                        require(attributeSize >= 20)
                        val attributesOffset = offset + 16 + attributeStart
                        require(attributesOffset.toLong() + attributeSize.toLong() * attributeCount <= offset.toLong() + chunkSize)
                        repeat(attributeCount) { index ->
                            val attributeOffset = attributesOffset + index * attributeSize
                            val nameIndex = u32(attributeOffset + 4).toInt()
                            if (strings.getOrNull(nameIndex) == "usesCleartextTraffic") {
                                val rawValueIndex = u32(attributeOffset + 8).toInt()
                                val valueType = xml[attributeOffset + 15].toInt() and 0xFF
                                val valueData = u32(attributeOffset + 16)
                                if (valueType == 0x12) return valueData != 0L
                                if (valueType == 0x10 || valueType == 0x11) return valueData != 0L
                                val rawValue = strings.getOrNull(rawValueIndex)
                                return when (rawValue?.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)) {
                                    "true", "1" -> true
                                    "false", "0" -> false
                                    else -> null
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        return null
                    }
                }
            }
            offset += chunkSize
        }
        return null
    }

    private fun readNativeAbis(apk: File): List<String> = runCatching {
        val abis = linkedSetOf<String>()
        ZipFile(apk).use { zip ->
            val entries = zip.entries()
            while (entries.hasMoreElements()) {
                val name = entries.nextElement().name
                if (name.startsWith("lib/")) {
                    val abi = name.removePrefix("lib/").substringBefore('/')
                    if (abi.isNotBlank() && abi != name) abis += abi
                }
            }
        }
        abis.toList().sorted()
    }.getOrDefault(emptyList())

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    private fun versionCode(info: android.content.pm.PackageInfo): Long =
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 28) info.longVersionCode else info.versionCode.toLong()

    private fun certificateInfo(certificate: X509Certificate): SignerCertificate = SignerCertificate(
        subject = certificate.subjectX500Principal.name,
        issuer = certificate.issuerX500Principal.name,
        serialNumber = certificate.serialNumber.toString(16).uppercase(Locale.ROOT),
        signatureAlgorithm = certificate.sigAlgName,
        validFrom = date(certificate.notBefore),
        validUntil = date(certificate.notAfter),
        sha256 = fingerprint(certificate.encoded)
    )

    private fun digest(file: File): String {
        val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
        file.inputStream().buffered().use { input ->
            val buffer = ByteArray(64 * 1024)
            while (true) {
                val count = input.read(buffer)
                if (count < 0) break
                digest.update(buffer, 0, count)
            }
        }
        return digest.digest().joinToString("") { "%02X".format(Locale.ROOT, it) }
    }

    private fun fingerprint(encoded: ByteArray): String = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
        .digest(encoded)
        .joinToString(":") { "%02X".format(Locale.ROOT, it) }

    private fun date(value: Date): String = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).format(value)
}


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/data/keystore/ApkWorkspaceRepository.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge.data.keystore

import android.content.Context
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.zip.ZipFile

class ApkReportHistoryStore(context: Context) {
    private val directory = File(context.filesDir, "apk-reports").apply { mkdirs() }
    private val preferences = context.getSharedPreferences("keyforge_apk_reports", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    private val favoritesKey = "favorites"

    @Synchronized
    fun save(report: ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport) {
        val safeId = report.reportId.ifBlank { "KF-${report.fileSha256.take(16)}-${report.inspectedAt}" }
            .replace(Regex("[^A-Za-z0-9._-]"), "_")
        val target = File(directory, "$safeId.json")
        val temporary = File(directory, "$safeId.tmp")
        temporary.writeText(encode(report).toString(), Charsets.UTF_8)
        if (target.exists()) target.delete()
        if (!temporary.renameTo(target)) {
            temporary.copyTo(target, overwrite = true)
            temporary.delete()
        }
        val stored = directory.listFiles()?.filter { it.isFile && it.extension == "json" }.orEmpty()
        val decoded = stored.mapNotNull { file -> runCatching { decode(JSONObject(file.readText(Charsets.UTF_8))) }.getOrNull()?.let { file to it } }
            .sortedByDescending { it.second.inspectedAt }
        decoded.drop(50).forEach { (file, reportItem) ->
            file.delete()
            setFavorite(reportItem.reportId, false)
        }
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun getReports(): List<ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport> = directory.listFiles()
        ?.filter { it.isFile && it.extension == "json" }
        ?.mapNotNull { file -> runCatching { decode(JSONObject(file.readText(Charsets.UTF_8))) }.getOrNull() }
        ?.sortedByDescending { it.inspectedAt }
        .orEmpty()

    fun remove(reportId: String) {
        val safeId = reportId.replace(Regex("[^A-Za-z0-9._-]"), "_")
        File(directory, "$safeId.json").delete()
        setFavorite(reportId, false)
    }

    fun clear() {
        directory.listFiles()?.forEach { it.delete() }
        preferences.edit().remove(favoritesKey).apply()
    }

    fun favoriteIds(): Set<String> = preferences.getStringSet(favoritesKey, emptySet())?.toMutableSet().orEmpty()

    fun setFavorite(reportId: String, favorite: Boolean) {
        val values = favoriteIds().toMutableSet()
        if (favorite) values += reportId else values -= reportId
        preferences.edit().putStringSet(favoritesKey, values).apply()
    }

    private fun encode(report: ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport) = JSONObject().apply {
        put("fileName", report.fileName)
        put("fileSize", report.fileSize)
        put("fileSha256", report.fileSha256)
        put("appName", report.appName)
        put("packageName", report.packageName)
        put("versionName", report.versionName)
        put("versionCode", report.versionCode)
        put("minSdk", report.minSdk)
        put("targetSdk", report.targetSdk)
        put("debuggable", report.debuggable)
        put("cleartextTrafficAllowed", report.cleartextTrafficAllowed)
        put("backupAllowed", report.backupAllowed)
        put("requestedPermissions", JSONArray(report.requestedPermissions))
        put("nativeAbis", JSONArray(report.nativeAbis))
        put("verified", report.verified)
        put("v1", report.v1)
        put("v2", report.v2)
        put("v3", report.v3)
        put("signers", JSONArray().apply {
            report.signers.forEach { signer ->
                put(JSONObject().apply {
                    put("subject", signer.subject)
                    put("issuer", signer.issuer)
                    put("serialNumber", signer.serialNumber)
                    put("signatureAlgorithm", signer.signatureAlgorithm)
                    put("validFrom", signer.validFrom)
                    put("validUntil", signer.validUntil)
                    put("sha256", signer.sha256)
                })
            }
        })
        put("errors", JSONArray(report.errors))
        put("warnings", JSONArray(report.warnings))
        put("iconBase64", report.iconBase64)
        put("reportId", report.reportId)
        put("inspectedAt", report.inspectedAt)
        put("operationType", report.operationType)
    }

    private fun decode(json: JSONObject): ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport {
        val signers = json.optJSONArray("signers") ?: JSONArray()
        val signerList = buildList {
            for (i in 0 until signers.length()) {
                val signer = signers.optJSONObject(i) ?: continue
                add(ApkToolsRepository.SignerCertificate(
                    signer.optString("subject"), signer.optString("issuer"), signer.optString("serialNumber"),
                    signer.optString("signatureAlgorithm"), signer.optString("validFrom"),
                    signer.optString("validUntil"), signer.optString("sha256")
                ))
            }
        }
        return ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport(
            fileName = json.optString("fileName"),
            fileSize = json.optLong("fileSize"),
            fileSha256 = json.optString("fileSha256"),
            appName = json.optString("appName"),
            packageName = json.optString("packageName"),
            versionName = json.optString("versionName"),
            versionCode = json.optLong("versionCode"),
            minSdk = json.optInt("minSdk"),
            targetSdk = json.optInt("targetSdk"),
            debuggable = json.optBoolean("debuggable"),
            cleartextTrafficAllowed = json.optBoolean("cleartextTrafficAllowed"),
            backupAllowed = json.optBoolean("backupAllowed"),
            requestedPermissions = json.optJSONArray("requestedPermissions").toStringList(),
            nativeAbis = json.optJSONArray("nativeAbis").toStringList(),
            verified = json.optBoolean("verified"),
            v1 = json.optBoolean("v1"),
            v2 = json.optBoolean("v2"),
            v3 = json.optBoolean("v3"),
            signers = signerList,
            errors = json.optJSONArray("errors").toStringList(),
            warnings = json.optJSONArray("warnings").toStringList(),
            iconBase64 = json.optString("iconBase64"),
            reportId = json.optString("reportId"),
            inspectedAt = json.optLong("inspectedAt"),
            operationType = json.optString("operationType", "INSPECTION")
        )
    }

    private fun JSONArray?.toStringList(): List<String> {
        if (this == null) return emptyList()
        return buildList { for (i in 0 until length()) add(optString(i)) }
    }
}

class ApkSigningPresetStore(context: Context) {
    data class Preset(val name: String, val v1: Boolean, val v2: Boolean, val v3: Boolean)

    private val preferences = context.getSharedPreferences("keyforge_signing_presets", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    fun all(): List<Preset> {
        val array = runCatching { JSONArray(preferences.getString("presets", "[]")) }.getOrNull() ?: return emptyList()
        return buildList {
            for (i in 0 until array.length()) {
                val item = array.optJSONObject(i) ?: continue
                add(Preset(item.optString("name"), item.optBoolean("v1"), item.optBoolean("v2"), item.optBoolean("v3")))
            }
        }
    }

    fun save(preset: Preset) {
        require(preset.name.isNotBlank()) { "Enter a preset name" }
        require(preset.v2 || preset.v3) { "Enable V2 or V3 for modern Android compatibility" }
        val updated = all().filterNot { it.name.equals(preset.name.trim(), true) } + preset.copy(name = preset.name.trim())
        preferences.edit().putString("presets", JSONArray().apply {
            updated.take(30).forEach { item ->
                put(JSONObject().apply {
                    put("name", item.name)
                    put("v1", item.v1)
                    put("v2", item.v2)
                    put("v3", item.v3)
                })
            }
        }.toString()).apply()
    }

    fun delete(name: String) {
        val updated = all().filterNot { it.name == name }
        preferences.edit().putString("presets", JSONArray().apply {
            updated.forEach { item -> put(JSONObject().apply {
                put("name", item.name); put("v1", item.v1); put("v2", item.v2); put("v3", item.v3)
            }) }
        }.toString()).apply()
    }
}

class ApkArchiveRepository {
    data class ArchiveEntry(val path: String, val size: Long, val compressedSize: Long, val directory: Boolean, val category: String) {
        val isPreviewableImage: Boolean get() = !directory && category == "Images" && size in 1L..67_108_864L
    }

    fun listEntries(apk: File): List<ArchiveEntry> {
        require(apk.isFile && apk.length() > 0L) { "Selected APK is empty or unavailable" }
        val entries = mutableListOf<ArchiveEntry>()
        ZipFile(apk).use { zip ->
            val iterator = zip.entries()
            while (iterator.hasMoreElements()) {
                val entry = iterator.nextElement()
                val path = entry.name
                if (path.startsWith("/") || path.split('/').any { it == ".." }) continue
                if (entries.size >= 50_000) break
                if (path.length > 1024) continue
                entries += ArchiveEntry(path, entry.size.coerceAtLeast(0), entry.compressedSize.coerceAtLeast(0), entry.isDirectory, category(path, entry.isDirectory))
            }
        }
        return entries.sortedWith(compareBy<ArchiveEntry> { !it.directory }.thenBy { it.path.lowercase(Locale.ROOT) })
    }

    fun readTextPreview(apk: File, entryPath: String, maxBytes: Int = 65_536): String? {
        val extension = entryPath.substringAfterLast('.', "").lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
        if (extension !in setOf("xml", "json", "txt", "properties", "yml", "yaml", "html", "css")) return null
        return runCatching {
            ZipFile(apk).use zipUse@ { zip ->
                val entry = zip.getEntry(entryPath) ?: return@zipUse null
                if (entry.isDirectory || entry.size !in 1L..maxBytes.toLong()) return@zipUse null
                val bytes = zip.getInputStream(entry).use inputUse@ { input ->
                    val output = java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream()
                    val buffer = ByteArray(8192)
                    var total = 0
                    while (true) {
                        val count = input.read(buffer)
                        if (count < 0) break
                        total += count
                        if (total > maxBytes) return@inputUse null
                        output.write(buffer, 0, count)
                    }
                    output.toByteArray()
                } ?: return@zipUse null
                if (bytes.any { it == 0.toByte() }) return@zipUse null
                val decoded = String(bytes, Charsets.UTF_8)
                val controlCount = decoded.count { it.code < 0x20 && it != '\n' && it != '\r' && it != '\t' }
                if (controlCount > decoded.length / 50) null else decoded
            }
        }.getOrNull()
    }

    fun extract(apk: File, entryPath: String, target: File) {
        require(entryPath.isNotBlank() && !entryPath.startsWith("/") && entryPath.split('/').none { it == ".." }) { "Invalid archive path" }
        ZipFile(apk).use { zip ->
            val entry = zip.getEntry(entryPath) ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("File was not found inside APK")
            require(!entry.isDirectory && (entry.size < 0L || entry.size <= 64L * 1024 * 1024)) { "Selected item is too large or is a directory" }
            target.parentFile?.mkdirs()
            zip.getInputStream(entry).use { input ->
                FileOutputStream(target).use { output ->
                    val buffer = ByteArray(8192)
                    var total = 0L
                    while (true) {
                        val count = input.read(buffer)
                        if (count < 0) break
                        total += count
                        require(total <= 64L * 1024 * 1024) { "Extracted item exceeds the 64 MB safety limit" }
                        output.write(buffer, 0, count)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun category(path: String, directory: Boolean): String {
        if (directory) return "Folders"
        val lower = path.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
        return when {
            lower == "androidmanifest.xml" -> "Manifest"
            lower.endsWith(".png") || lower.endsWith(".jpg") || lower.endsWith(".jpeg") || lower.endsWith(".webp") || lower.endsWith(".gif") -> "Images"
            lower.endsWith(".dex") -> "Bytecode"
            lower.startsWith("res/") || lower == "resources.arsc" -> "Resources"
            lower.startsWith("assets/") -> "Assets"
            lower.startsWith("lib/") && lower.endsWith(".so") -> "Native libraries"
            lower.startsWith("meta-inf/") -> "Signing metadata"
            lower.endsWith(".xml") -> "XML"
            lower.endsWith(".ttf") || lower.endsWith(".otf") -> "Fonts"
            else -> "Other"
        }
    }
}


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/data/keystore/KeyStoreRepository.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge.data.keystore

import android.content.Context
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x500.style.BCStyle
import org.bouncycastle.asn1.x500.X500NameBuilder
import org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509CertificateConverter
import org.bouncycastle.cert.jcajce.JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder
import org.bouncycastle.operator.jcajce.JcaContentSignerBuilder
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileInputStream
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.math.BigInteger
import java.security.KeyPairGenerator
import java.security.KeyStore
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.security.SecureRandom
import java.security.interfaces.ECKey
import java.security.interfaces.RSAKey
import java.security.spec.ECGenParameterSpec
import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

class KeyStoreRepository(private val context: Context) {
    private val directory = File(context.noBackupFilesDir, "KeyVault").apply { mkdirs() }
    private val legacyDirectory = File(context.filesDir, "keystores")
    private val usagePreferences = context.getSharedPreferences("keyforge_key_usage", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    data class CreateOptions(
        val name: String,
        val alias: String,
        val commonName: String,
        val organization: String,
        val organizationalUnit: String,
        val countryCode: String,
        val locality: String,
        val stateOrProvince: String,
        val emailAddress: String,
        val keyAlgorithm: String,
        val keySize: Int,
        val validityYears: Int
    )

    data class CertificateDetails(
        val alias: String,
        val entryType: String,
        val subject: String,
        val issuer: String,
        val serialNumber: String,
        val version: String,
        val signatureAlgorithm: String,
        val publicKeyAlgorithm: String,
        val publicKeySize: String,
        val notBefore: String,
        val notAfter: String,
        val sha1: String,
        val sha256: String,
        val md5: String,
        val isValidNow: Boolean,
        val subjectAlternativeNames: String,
        val keyUsage: String,
        val extendedKeyUsage: String,
        val basicConstraints: String,
        val isSelfSigned: Boolean,
        val chainLength: Int,
        val chainSubjects: String
    )

    data class Entry(
        val file: File,
        val name: String,
        val type: String,
        val aliases: List<String>,
        val size: Long,
        val modified: Long,
        val certificates: List<CertificateDetails> = emptyList(),
        val privateKeyCount: Int = 0,
        val trustedCertificateCount: Int = 0,
        val provider: String = "",
        val displayName: String = name,
        val description: String = ""
    )

    data class SigningUsageRecord(
        val apkName: String,
        val timestamp: Long,
        val success: Boolean
    )

    data class SigningUsageStats(
        val successCount: Long,
        val failureCount: Long,
        val lastUsed: Long,
        val records: List<SigningUsageRecord>
    )

    data class SigningActivityItem(
        val certificateFingerprint: String,
        val record: SigningUsageRecord
    )

    init {
        migrateLegacyFiles()
    }

    fun create(options: CreateOptions, password: CharArray): Entry {
        require(options.name.isNotBlank()) { "Enter a file name" }
        require(options.alias.isNotBlank()) { "Enter an alias" }
        require(password.size >= 8) { "Password must contain at least 8 characters" }
        require(options.validityYears in 1..30) { "Certificate validity must be between 1 and 30 years" }

        val algorithm = options.keyAlgorithm.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)
        val validKeySize = when (algorithm) {
            "RSA" -> options.keySize in setOf(2048, 3072, 4096)
            "EC" -> options.keySize in setOf(256, 384, 521)
            else -> false
        }
        require(validKeySize) { "Unsupported key algorithm or size" }

        val countryCode = options.countryCode.trim().uppercase(Locale.ROOT)
        require(countryCode.isEmpty() || countryCode in Locale.getISOCountries()) {
            "Country code must be a valid two-letter ISO 3166-1 code"
        }
        require(options.emailAddress.isBlank() || android.util.Patterns.EMAIL_ADDRESS.matcher(options.emailAddress.trim()).matches()) {
            "Enter a valid email address"
        }

        val safeName = sanitizeName(options.name)
        require(safeName.isNotBlank()) { "Enter a valid file name" }
        val target = File(directory, "$safeName.p12")
        require(!target.exists()) { "A keystore with this name already exists" }

        val generator = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance(algorithm)
        if (algorithm == "RSA") {
            generator.initialize(options.keySize, SecureRandom())
        } else {
            val curve = when (options.keySize) {
                256 -> "secp256r1"
                384 -> "secp384r1"
                else -> "secp521r1"
            }
            generator.initialize(ECGenParameterSpec(curve), SecureRandom())
        }
        val pair = generator.generateKeyPair()
        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val subjectBuilder = X500NameBuilder(BCStyle.INSTANCE)
        var hasSubject = false

        fun addSubjectField(attribute: org.bouncycastle.asn1.ASN1ObjectIdentifier, value: String) {
            val trimmed = value.trim()
            if (trimmed.isNotEmpty()) {
                subjectBuilder.addRDN(attribute, trimmed)
                hasSubject = true
            }
        }

        addSubjectField(BCStyle.CN, options.commonName)
        addSubjectField(BCStyle.O, options.organization)
        addSubjectField(BCStyle.OU, options.organizationalUnit)
        addSubjectField(BCStyle.C, countryCode)
        addSubjectField(BCStyle.L, options.locality)
        addSubjectField(BCStyle.ST, options.stateOrProvince)
        addSubjectField(BCStyle.EmailAddress, options.emailAddress)
        if (!hasSubject) subjectBuilder.addRDN(BCStyle.CN, options.alias.trim())
        val subject = subjectBuilder.build()

        val serial = BigInteger(159, SecureRandom()).add(BigInteger.ONE)
        val signatureAlgorithm = when (algorithm) {
            "RSA" -> when (options.keySize) {
                2048 -> "SHA256withRSA"
                3072 -> "SHA384withRSA"
                else -> "SHA512withRSA"
            }
            else -> when (options.keySize) {
                256 -> "SHA256withECDSA"
                384 -> "SHA384withECDSA"
                else -> "SHA512withECDSA"
            }
        }
        val builder = JcaX509v3CertificateBuilder(
            subject,
            serial,
            Date(now - TimeUnit.MINUTES.toMillis(5)),
            Date(now + TimeUnit.DAYS.toMillis(365L * options.validityYears)),
            subject,
            pair.public
        )
        val certificate = JcaX509CertificateConverter().getCertificate(
            builder.build(JcaContentSignerBuilder(signatureAlgorithm).build(pair.private))
        )
        certificate.verify(pair.public)

        val keyStore = KeyStore.getInstance("PKCS12")
        keyStore.load(null, password)
        keyStore.setKeyEntry(options.alias.trim(), pair.private, password, arrayOf(certificate))
        storeAtomically(keyStore, target, password)
        return inspect(target, password)
    }

    fun import(sourceName: String, bytes: ByteArray, password: CharArray): Entry {
        require(bytes.isNotEmpty()) { "Selected file is empty" }
        val store = KeyStore.getInstance("PKCS12")
        try {
            store.load(ByteArrayInputStream(bytes), password)
        } catch (error: Exception) {
            throw IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported keystore format or incorrect password. Choose a PKCS12 file (.p12 or .pfx).", error)
        }
        val base = sanitizeName(sourceName.substringBeforeLast('.', sourceName)).ifBlank { "Imported_Keystore" }
        var target = File(directory, "$base.p12")
        var index = 1
        while (target.exists()) {
            target = File(directory, "${base}_$index.p12")
            index++
        }
        storeAtomically(store, target, password)
        return inspect(target, password)
    }

    fun inspect(file: File, password: CharArray): Entry {
        require(file.exists() && file.isFile) { "Keystore file was not found" }
        val store = FileInputStream(file).use { loadKeyStore(it, password) }
        val aliases = store.aliases().toList().sorted()
        val details = aliases.mapNotNull { alias ->
            val cert = store.getCertificate(alias) as? X509Certificate ?: return@mapNotNull null
            val key = runCatching { store.getKey(alias, password) }.getOrNull()
            val chain = runCatching { store.getCertificateChain(alias)?.filterIsInstance<X509Certificate>().orEmpty() }.getOrDefault(emptyList())
            certificateDetails(alias, store.isKeyEntry(alias), key?.algorithm, cert, chain.ifEmpty { listOf(cert) })
        }
        return Entry(
            file = file,
            name = file.nameWithoutExtension,
            type = "PKCS12",
            aliases = aliases,
            size = file.length(),
            modified = file.lastModified(),
            certificates = details,
            privateKeyCount = aliases.count { store.isKeyEntry(it) },
            trustedCertificateCount = aliases.count { store.isCertificateEntry(it) },
            provider = store.provider.name,
            displayName = usagePreferences.getString(metadataKey(file.nameWithoutExtension, "display"), file.nameWithoutExtension).orEmpty().ifBlank { file.nameWithoutExtension },
            description = usagePreferences.getString(metadataKey(file.nameWithoutExtension, "description"), "").orEmpty()
        )
    }

    fun list(): List<Entry> = directory.listFiles()
        ?.filter { it.isFile && (it.extension.equals("p12", true) || it.extension.equals("pfx", true)) }
        ?.sortedByDescending { it.lastModified() }
        ?.map { file ->
            Entry(
                file = file,
                name = file.nameWithoutExtension,
                type = "PKCS12",
                aliases = emptyList(),
                size = file.length(),
                modified = file.lastModified(),
                displayName = usagePreferences.getString(metadataKey(file.nameWithoutExtension, "display"), file.nameWithoutExtension).orEmpty().ifBlank { file.nameWithoutExtension },
                description = usagePreferences.getString(metadataKey(file.nameWithoutExtension, "description"), "").orEmpty()
            )
        } ?: emptyList()

    fun updateKeystore(
        entry: Entry,
        newFileName: String,
        oldAlias: String,
        newAlias: String,
        currentPassword: CharArray,
        newPassword: CharArray? = null,
        displayName: String = entry.displayName,
        description: String = entry.description
    ): Entry {
        require(entry.file.exists() && entry.file.isFile) { "Keystore file was not found" }
        require(newFileName.isNotBlank()) { "Enter a file name" }
        require(oldAlias in entry.aliases) { "Select an existing alias" }
        require(newAlias.isNotBlank()) { "Enter a new alias" }
        if (newPassword != null) require(newPassword.size >= 8) { "New password must contain at least 8 characters" }

        val sourceStore = FileInputStream(entry.file).use { loadKeyStore(it, currentPassword) }
        val finalPassword = newPassword ?: currentPassword
        val renamedAlias = newAlias.trim()
        if (oldAlias != renamedAlias) {
            require(!sourceStore.containsAlias(renamedAlias)) { "An alias with this name already exists" }
        }

        val updatedStore = KeyStore.getInstance("PKCS12")
        updatedStore.load(null, finalPassword)
        val sourceAliases = sourceStore.aliases().toList().sorted()
        sourceAliases.forEach { sourceAlias ->
            val targetAlias = if (sourceAlias == oldAlias) renamedAlias else sourceAlias
            if (sourceStore.isKeyEntry(sourceAlias)) {
                val key = sourceStore.getKey(sourceAlias, currentPassword)
                    ?: throw IllegalStateException("Key could not be read: $sourceAlias")
                val chain = sourceStore.getCertificateChain(sourceAlias)
                require(chain != null && chain.isNotEmpty()) { "Certificate chain is missing: $sourceAlias" }
                updatedStore.setKeyEntry(targetAlias, key, finalPassword, chain)
            } else {
                val certificate = sourceStore.getCertificate(sourceAlias)
                    ?: throw IllegalStateException("Certificate could not be read: $sourceAlias")
                updatedStore.setCertificateEntry(targetAlias, certificate)
            }
        }

        val normalizedName = newFileName.trim().replace(Regex("\\.(p12|pfx)$", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), "")
        val safeName = sanitizeName(normalizedName)
        require(safeName.isNotBlank()) { "Enter a valid file name" }
        val target = File(directory, "$safeName.p12")
        require(target == entry.file || !target.exists()) { "A keystore with this name already exists" }

        val temporary = File(directory, ".${entry.file.name}.${System.nanoTime()}.edit.tmp")
        val backup = File(directory, ".${entry.file.name}.${System.nanoTime()}.backup")
        var originalMoved = false
        var updatedMoved = false
        try {
            FileOutputStream(temporary).use {
                updatedStore.store(it, finalPassword)
                it.fd.sync()
            }
            val verified = FileInputStream(temporary).use { loadKeyStore(it, finalPassword) }
            val expectedAliases = sourceAliases.map { if (it == oldAlias) renamedAlias else it }.sorted()
            require(verified.aliases().toList().sorted() == expectedAliases) { "Keystore entries failed validation" }
            sourceAliases.forEach { sourceAlias ->
                val targetAlias = if (sourceAlias == oldAlias) renamedAlias else sourceAlias
                val beforeCertificate = sourceStore.getCertificate(sourceAlias)
                val afterCertificate = verified.getCertificate(targetAlias)
                require(beforeCertificate?.encoded?.contentEquals(afterCertificate?.encoded ?: byteArrayOf()) == true) {
                    "Certificate validation failed: $sourceAlias"
                }
                if (sourceStore.isKeyEntry(sourceAlias)) {
                    val beforeKey = sourceStore.getKey(sourceAlias, currentPassword)
                    val afterKey = verified.getKey(targetAlias, finalPassword)
                    val beforeEncoded = beforeKey?.encoded
                    val afterEncoded = afterKey?.encoded
                    require(beforeEncoded != null && afterEncoded != null && beforeEncoded.contentEquals(afterEncoded)) {
                        "Private key could not be verified after update: $sourceAlias"
                    }
                    val beforeChain = sourceStore.getCertificateChain(sourceAlias).orEmpty().map { it.encoded }
                    val afterChain = verified.getCertificateChain(targetAlias).orEmpty().map { it.encoded }
                    require(beforeChain.size == afterChain.size && beforeChain.indices.all { beforeChain[it].contentEquals(afterChain[it]) }) {
                        "Certificate chain validation failed: $sourceAlias"
                    }
                }
            }

            require(entry.file.renameTo(backup)) { "Unable to prepare keystore update" }
            originalMoved = true
            require(temporary.renameTo(target)) { "Unable to save updated keystore" }
            updatedMoved = true
            val result = inspect(target, finalPassword)
            require(renamedAlias in result.aliases) { "Saved keystore validation failed" }
            if (entry.name != target.nameWithoutExtension) {
                usagePreferences.edit()
                    .remove(metadataKey(entry.name, "display"))
                    .remove(metadataKey(entry.name, "description"))
                    .apply()
            }
            usagePreferences.edit()
                .putString(metadataKey(target.nameWithoutExtension, "display"), displayName.trim().ifBlank { target.nameWithoutExtension })
                .putString(metadataKey(target.nameWithoutExtension, "description"), description.trim())
                .apply()
            backup.delete()
            return result.copy(
                displayName = displayName.trim().ifBlank { target.nameWithoutExtension },
                description = description.trim()
            )
        } catch (error: Exception) {
            if (updatedMoved && target.exists()) target.delete()
            if (originalMoved && backup.exists()) backup.renameTo(entry.file)
            throw error
        } finally {
            temporary.delete()
            if (backup.exists() && entry.file.exists()) backup.delete()
        }
    }

    fun exportBytes(entry: Entry, password: CharArray): ByteArray {
        FileInputStream(entry.file).use { loadKeyStore(it, password) }
        return entry.file.readBytes()
    }

    fun recordSigningResult(certificateSha256: String, apkName: String, success: Boolean, timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()) {
        require(certificateSha256.isNotBlank()) { "Certificate fingerprint is required" }
        val key = usageKey(certificateSha256)
        val oldRecords = runCatching { JSONArray(usagePreferences.getString("${key}_records", "[]") ?: "[]") }.getOrDefault(JSONArray())
        val records = JSONArray()
        for (index in (oldRecords.length() - 99).coerceAtLeast(0) until oldRecords.length()) {
            records.put(oldRecords.getJSONObject(index))
        }
        records.put(
            JSONObject()
                .put("apkName", apkName)
                .put("timestamp", timestamp)
                .put("success", success)
        )
        val editor = usagePreferences.edit()
            .putString("${key}_records", records.toString())
            .putLong("${key}_last_used", timestamp)
        val counter = if (success) "${key}_success" else "${key}_failure"
        editor.putLong(counter, usagePreferences.getLong(counter, 0L) + 1L).apply()
    }

    fun allSigningActivity(): List<SigningActivityItem> {
        val records = mutableListOf<SigningActivityItem>()
        usagePreferences.all.keys
            .filter { it.startsWith("usage_") && it.endsWith("_records") }
            .forEach { preferenceKey ->
                val fingerprint = preferenceKey.removePrefix("usage_").removeSuffix("_records")
                val json = runCatching {
                    JSONArray(usagePreferences.getString(preferenceKey, "[]") ?: "[]")
                }.getOrDefault(JSONArray())
                for (index in 0 until json.length()) {
                    val item = json.optJSONObject(index) ?: continue
                    records += SigningActivityItem(
                        certificateFingerprint = fingerprint,
                        record = SigningUsageRecord(
                            apkName = item.optString("apkName"),
                            timestamp = item.optLong("timestamp"),
                            success = item.optBoolean("success")
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        return records.sortedByDescending { it.record.timestamp }.take(100)
    }

    fun signingUsage(certificateSha256: String): SigningUsageStats {
        val key = usageKey(certificateSha256)
        val json = runCatching { JSONArray(usagePreferences.getString("${key}_records", "[]") ?: "[]") }.getOrDefault(JSONArray())
        val records = buildList {
            for (index in 0 until json.length()) {
                val item = json.optJSONObject(index) ?: continue
                add(
                    SigningUsageRecord(
                        apkName = item.optString("apkName"),
                        timestamp = item.optLong("timestamp"),
                        success = item.optBoolean("success")
                    )
                )
            }
        }.asReversed()
        return SigningUsageStats(
            successCount = usagePreferences.getLong("${key}_success", 0L),
            failureCount = usagePreferences.getLong("${key}_failure", 0L),
            lastUsed = usagePreferences.getLong("${key}_last_used", 0L),
            records = records
        )
    }

    private fun usageKey(fingerprint: String): String =
        "usage_${fingerprint.filter { it.isLetterOrDigit() }.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)}"

    private fun metadataKey(fileName: String, field: String): String =
        "keystore_${MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(fileName.toByteArray()).take(12).joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }}_$field"

    private fun certificateDetails(
        alias: String,
        isKeyEntry: Boolean,
        keyAlgorithm: String?,
        cert: X509Certificate,
        chain: List<X509Certificate>
    ): CertificateDetails {
        val publicKey = cert.publicKey
        val keySize = when (publicKey) {
            is RSAKey -> publicKey.modulus.bitLength().toString()
            is ECKey -> publicKey.params.curve.field.fieldSize.toString()
            else -> "—"
        }
        val alternativeNames = runCatching {
            cert.subjectAlternativeNames.orEmpty().mapNotNull { item ->
                val type = (item.firstOrNull() as? Number)?.toInt()
                val value = item.getOrNull(1)?.toString()
                if (value.isNullOrBlank()) null else "${sanType(type)}: $value"
            }.joinToString("\n").ifBlank { "—" }
        }.getOrDefault("—")
        val keyUsageNames = listOf(
            "Digital signature", "Non-repudiation", "Key encipherment", "Data encipherment",
            "Key agreement", "Certificate signing", "CRL signing", "Encipher only", "Decipher only"
        )
        val usageFlags = cert.keyUsage
        val keyUsages = if (usageFlags == null) "—" else usageFlags.indices
            .filter { usageFlags[it] }
            .mapNotNull { keyUsageNames.getOrNull(it) }
            .joinToString(", ").ifBlank { "—" }
        val extendedUsages = runCatching {
            cert.extendedKeyUsage?.joinToString(", ") { oid ->
                when (oid) {
                    "1.3.6.1.5.5.7.3.1" -> "TLS server authentication"
                    "1.3.6.1.5.5.7.3.2" -> "TLS client authentication"
                    "1.3.6.1.5.5.7.3.3" -> "Code signing"
                    "1.3.6.1.5.5.7.3.4" -> "Email protection"
                    "1.3.6.1.5.5.7.3.8" -> "Time stamping"
                    "2.5.29.37.0" -> "Any purpose"
                    else -> oid
                }
            } ?: "—"
        }.getOrDefault("—")
        val constraints = cert.basicConstraints
        val basicConstraints = if (constraints < 0) "End-entity certificate" else if (constraints == Int.MAX_VALUE) "Certificate authority · unlimited path" else "Certificate authority · path length $constraints"
        val selfSigned = cert.subjectX500Principal == cert.issuerX500Principal &&
            runCatching { cert.verify(cert.publicKey); true }.getOrDefault(false)
        return CertificateDetails(
            alias = alias,
            entryType = if (isKeyEntry) "Private key entry" else "Trusted certificate",
            subject = cert.subjectX500Principal.name,
            issuer = cert.issuerX500Principal.name,
            serialNumber = cert.serialNumber.toString(16).uppercase(Locale.ROOT),
            version = cert.version.toString(),
            signatureAlgorithm = cert.sigAlgName,
            publicKeyAlgorithm = keyAlgorithm ?: publicKey.algorithm,
            publicKeySize = keySize,
            notBefore = formatDate(cert.notBefore),
            notAfter = formatDate(cert.notAfter),
            sha1 = fingerprint(cert, "SHA-1"),
            sha256 = fingerprint(cert, "SHA-256"),
            md5 = fingerprint(cert, "MD5"),
            isValidNow = runCatching { cert.checkValidity(); true }.getOrDefault(false),
            subjectAlternativeNames = alternativeNames,
            keyUsage = keyUsages,
            extendedKeyUsage = extendedUsages,
            basicConstraints = basicConstraints,
            isSelfSigned = selfSigned,
            chainLength = chain.size,
            chainSubjects = chain.joinToString("\n") { it.subjectX500Principal.name }.ifBlank { "—" }
        )
    }

    private fun sanType(type: Int?): String = when (type) {
        1 -> "Email"
        2 -> "DNS"
        4 -> "X.400"
        6 -> "URI"
        7 -> "IP"
        8 -> "Registered ID"
        else -> "Other"
    }

    private fun fingerprint(cert: X509Certificate, algorithm: String): String =
        MessageDigest.getInstance(algorithm).digest(cert.encoded)
            .joinToString(":") { "%02X".format(Locale.ROOT, it) }

    private fun formatDate(date: Date): String =
        SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).format(date)

    private fun storeAtomically(store: KeyStore, target: File, password: CharArray) {
        val temporary = File(directory, ".${target.name}.${System.nanoTime()}.tmp")
        try {
            FileOutputStream(temporary).use {
                store.store(it, password)
                it.fd.sync()
            }
            if (!temporary.renameTo(target)) {
                throw IllegalStateException("Unable to save keystore in private storage")
            }
        } finally {
            temporary.delete()
        }
    }

    private fun loadKeyStore(input: java.io.InputStream, password: CharArray): KeyStore {
        val store = KeyStore.getInstance("PKCS12")
        store.load(input, password)
        return store
    }

    private fun sanitizeName(value: String): String =
        value.trim().replace(Regex("[^A-Za-z0-9._-]"), "_").trim('.', '_')

    private fun migrateLegacyFiles() {
        if (!legacyDirectory.exists() || !legacyDirectory.isDirectory) return
        legacyDirectory.listFiles()?.filter {
            it.isFile && (it.extension.equals("p12", true) || it.extension.equals("pfx", true))
        }?.forEach { old ->
            val target = File(directory, old.name)
            if (!target.exists()) runCatching { old.copyTo(target, overwrite = false) }
        }
    }
}

package com.forgehub.keyforge.ui

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.forgehub.keyforge.data.AppPrefs
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.combine
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

data class AppState(val onboardingDone: Boolean, val language: String, val theme: String, val accentColor: String)

class AppViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {
    private val prefs = AppPrefs(application)

    val state: StateFlow<AppState?> = combine(prefs.onboardingDone, prefs.language, prefs.theme, prefs.accentColor) { done, language, theme, accentColor ->
        AppState(done, language, theme, accentColor)
    }.stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, null)

    fun completeOnboarding() {
        viewModelScope.launch { prefs.setOnboardingDone(true) }
    }


    fun setLanguage(tag: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch { prefs.setLanguage(tag) }
    }

    fun setTheme(mode: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch { prefs.setTheme(mode) }
    }

    fun setAccentColor(color: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch { prefs.setAccentColor(color) }
    }
}


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/ui/KeyForgeApp.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge.ui

import androidx.compose.animation.Crossfade
import androidx.compose.animation.core.tween
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.Surface
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.CompositionLocalProvider
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.home.HomeScreen
import com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.locale.LocaleProvider
import com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.motion.LocalReduceMotion
import com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.motion.rememberReduceMotion
import com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.onboarding.OnboardingScreen
import com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.theme.KeyForgeTheme

@Composable
fun KeyForgeApp(viewModel: AppViewModel) {
    val state by viewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val current = state ?: return
    val reduceMotion = rememberReduceMotion()

    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalReduceMotion provides reduceMotion) {
        LocaleProvider(current.language) {
            KeyForgeTheme(themeMode = current.theme, accentColor = current.accentColor) {
                Surface(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.background
                ) {
                    Crossfade(
                        targetState = current.onboardingDone,
                        animationSpec = tween(if (reduceMotion) 0 else 350),
                        label = "root"
                    ) { done ->
                        if (done) {
                            HomeScreen(
                                themeMode = current.theme,
                                language = current.language,
                                accentColor = current.accentColor,
                                onThemeChange = viewModel::setTheme,
                                onAccentColorChange = viewModel::setAccentColor,
                                onLanguageChange = viewModel::setLanguage
                            )
                        } else {
                            OnboardingScreen(
                                onFinished = viewModel::completeOnboarding,
                                onLanguageChange = viewModel::setLanguage
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
