@Composable
internal fun ApkCompareScreen(onBack: () -> Unit, repository: ApkToolsRepository) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    var first by remember { mutableStateOf<File?>(null) }
    var second by remember { mutableStateOf<File?>(null) }
    var pickTarget by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    var busy by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var error by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var result by remember { mutableStateOf<Pair<ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport, ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport>?>(null) }
    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        onDispose { first?.delete(); second?.delete() }
    }
    val picker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocument()) { uri ->
        if (uri != null) scope.launch {
            runCatching { withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { copySelectedApk(context, uri) } }
                .onSuccess {
                    if (pickTarget == 0) { first?.delete(); first = it } else { second?.delete(); second = it }
                    result = null
                    error = null
                }
                .onFailure { error = it.message ?: "Unable to read APK" }
        }
    }
    ScreenHeader("APK Comparison", "Compare two builds side by side", onBack)
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(13.dp)) {
        FileChoiceCard("BASELINE APK", first, "Choose the earlier version") { pickTarget = 0; picker.launch(arrayOf("application/vnd.android.package-archive", "application/octet-stream", "*/*")) }
        FileChoiceCard("NEW APK", second, "Choose the newer version") { pickTarget = 1; picker.launch(arrayOf("application/vnd.android.package-archive", "application/octet-stream", "*/*")) }
        Button(onClick = {
            val a = first ?: return@Button
            val b = second ?: return@Button
            scope.launch {
                busy = true
                error = null
                result = null
                runCatching { withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { repository.verify(a) to repository.verify(b) } }
                    .onSuccess { result = it }
                    .onFailure { error = it.message ?: "Unable to compare selected APKs" }
                busy = false
            }
        }, enabled = first != null && second != null && !busy, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
            if (busy) CircularProgressIndicator(Modifier.size(18.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp) else Icon(Icons.Filled.CompareArrows, null)
            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
            Text("Compare builds")
        }
        error?.let { InlineNotice(it, true) }
        result?.let { (before, after) ->
            ComparisonSummary(before, after)
            if (before.packageName.isNotBlank() && after.packageName.isNotBlank() && before.packageName != after.packageName) {
                InlineNotice("These APKs have different package IDs. Review the package identity before treating this as a version-to-version comparison.", true)
            }
            SectionLabel("PACKAGE CHANGES")
            val rows = listOf(
                Triple("Application name", before.appName, after.appName),
                Triple("Package ID", before.packageName, after.packageName),
                Triple("Version", "${before.versionName} (${before.versionCode})", "${after.versionName} (${after.versionCode})"),
                Triple("APK size", readableBytes(before.fileSize), readableBytes(after.fileSize)),
                Triple("Minimum SDK", before.minSdk.toString(), after.minSdk.toString()),
                Triple("Target SDK", before.targetSdk.toString(), after.targetSdk.toString()),
                Triple("Debuggable", before.debuggable.toString(), after.debuggable.toString()),
                Triple("Cleartext traffic", before.cleartextTrafficAllowed.toString(), after.cleartextTrafficAllowed.toString()),
                Triple("Backup allowed", before.backupAllowed.toString(), after.backupAllowed.toString()),
                Triple("Signer SHA-256", before.signers.joinToString(" · ") { it.sha256 }, after.signers.joinToString(" · ") { it.sha256 })
            )
            rows.forEach { row ->
                val changed = row.second != row.third
                CardBlock {
                    Text(row.first, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                    ComparisonValue("Previous", row.second.ifBlank { "—" })
                    ComparisonValue("Current", row.third.ifBlank { "—" }, changed)
                }
            }
            val added = after.requestedPermissions.toSet() - before.requestedPermissions.toSet()
            val removed = before.requestedPermissions.toSet() - after.requestedPermissions.toSet()
            SectionLabel("PERMISSION CHANGES")
            CardBlock {
                Text("${added.size} added · ${removed.size} removed", fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                added.forEach { Text("+  $it", color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary, fontSize = 11.sp) }
                removed.forEach { Text("−  $it", color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error, fontSize = 11.sp) }
                if (added.isEmpty() && removed.isEmpty()) Text("No permission changes", color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 12.sp)
            }
            SectionLabel("SIGNATURE SCHEMES")
            CardBlock {
                listOf("V1" to (before.v1 to after.v1), "V2" to (before.v2 to after.v2), "V3" to (before.v3 to after.v3)).forEach { (name, values) ->
                    ComparisonValue(name, "${values.first}  →  ${values.second}", values.first != values.second)
                }
            }
            if (before.fileSha256 == after.fileSha256) InlineNotice("Both selections have the same SHA-256 fingerprint.", false)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun ApkInspectorScreen(onBack: () -> Unit, archiveRepository: ApkArchiveRepository) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    var apk by remember { mutableStateOf<File?>(null) }
    var entries by remember { mutableStateOf<List<ApkArchiveRepository.ArchiveEntry>>(emptyList()) }
    var query by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var category by remember { mutableStateOf("All") }
    var preview by remember { mutableStateOf<android.graphics.Bitmap?>(null) }
    var showImagePreview by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var textPreview by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var selectedEntry by remember { mutableStateOf<ApkArchiveRepository.ArchiveEntry?>(null) }
    var message by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var busy by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        onDispose { apk?.delete() }
    }
    val picker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocument()) { uri ->
        if (uri != null) scope.launch {
            busy = true
            runCatching {
                val copied = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { copySelectedApk(context, uri) }
                val listing = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { archiveRepository.listEntries(copied) }
                copied to listing
            }.onSuccess { (file, listing) ->
                apk?.delete()
                apk = file; entries = listing; query = ""; category = "All"; preview = null; showImagePreview = false; textPreview = null; selectedEntry = null; message = null
            }.onFailure { message = it.message ?: "Unable to inspect APK archive" }
            busy = false
        }
    }
    val appPrefs = remember(context) { AppPrefs(context) }
    val exportFolderValue by appPrefs.exportFolderUri.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = null)
    val exportNamePattern by appPrefs.exportNamePattern.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = AppPrefs.EXPORT_NAME_PATTERN_DEFAULT)
    val exportSaveMode by appPrefs.exportSaveMode.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = AppPrefs.EXPORT_SAVE_ASK)
    var pendingExtraction by remember { mutableStateOf<File?>(null) }
    var pendingExtractionName by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var pendingExtractionMime by remember { mutableStateOf("application/octet-stream") }
    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        onDispose { pendingExtraction?.delete() }
    }

    fun clearPendingExtraction() {
        pendingExtraction?.delete()
        pendingExtraction = null
        pendingExtractionName = ""
        pendingExtractionMime = "application/octet-stream"
    }

    fun writeExtractionToUri(uri: Uri, extracted: File) {
        context.contentResolver.openOutputStream(uri, "wt")?.use { output ->
            extracted.inputStream().buffered().use { it.copyTo(output, 64 * 1024) }
        } ?: throw IllegalStateException("Unable to open destination")
    }

    fun writeExtractionToFolder(folderUri: Uri, extracted: File, displayName: String, mimeType: String) {
        val outputUri = FileExportManager.createFileInFolder(context, folderUri, mimeType, displayName)
        try {
            writeExtractionToUri(outputUri, extracted)
        } catch (error: Exception) {
            runCatching { android.provider.DocumentsContract.deleteDocument(context.contentResolver, outputUri) }
            throw error
        }
    }

    val saveLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(SaveDocumentContract()) { uri ->
        val extracted = pendingExtraction
        if (uri != null && extracted != null) scope.launch {
            busy = true
            runCatching { withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { writeExtractionToUri(uri, extracted) } }
                .onSuccess { message = "File extracted successfully" }
                .onFailure { message = it.message ?: "Unable to extract file" }
            clearPendingExtraction()
            busy = false
        } else {
            clearPendingExtraction()
            busy = false
        }
    }

    val exportFolderPicker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocumentTree()) { uri ->
        val extracted = pendingExtraction
        if (uri != null && extracted != null) {
            FileExportManager.persistFolderPermission(context, uri)
            scope.launch {
                busy = true
                runCatching {
                    appPrefs.setExportFolderUri(uri.toString())
                    withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        writeExtractionToFolder(uri, extracted, pendingExtractionName, pendingExtractionMime)
                    }
                }.onSuccess { message = "File extracted successfully" }
                    .onFailure { message = it.message ?: "Unable to extract file" }
                clearPendingExtraction()
                busy = false
            }
        } else {
            clearPendingExtraction()
            busy = false
        }
    }

    val requestExtraction: (ApkArchiveRepository.ArchiveEntry) -> Unit = { entry ->
        val source = apk
        if (source != null && !entry.directory) scope.launch {
            busy = true
            runCatching {
                withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                    val temporary = File(context.cacheDir, "extract-${System.currentTimeMillis()}-${File(entry.path).name}")
                    try {
                        archiveRepository.extract(source, entry.path, temporary)
                        temporary
                    } catch (error: Exception) {
                        temporary.delete()
                        throw error
                    }
                }
            }.onSuccess { extracted ->
                pendingExtraction?.delete()
                pendingExtraction = extracted
                val extension = File(entry.path).extension.ifBlank { "bin" }
                pendingExtractionName = FileExportManager.buildFileName(
                    pattern = exportNamePattern,
                    name = File(entry.path).nameWithoutExtension,
                    extension = extension
                )
                pendingExtractionMime = android.webkit.MimeTypeMap.getSingleton().getMimeTypeFromExtension(extension.lowercase(Locale.ROOT))
                    ?: "application/octet-stream"
                if (exportSaveMode == AppPrefs.EXPORT_SAVE_DIRECT) {
                    val folderUri = exportFolderValue?.let { Uri.parse(it) }
                    if (folderUri == null) {
                        exportFolderPicker.launch(null)
                    } else {
                        val displayName = pendingExtractionName
                        val mimeType = pendingExtractionMime
                        runCatching {
                            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                                writeExtractionToFolder(folderUri, extracted, displayName, mimeType)
                            }
                        }.onSuccess { message = "File extracted successfully" }
                            .onFailure { message = it.message ?: "Unable to extract file" }
                        clearPendingExtraction()
                        busy = false
                    }
                } else {
                    saveLauncher.launch(
                        SaveDocumentRequest(
                            mimeType = pendingExtractionMime,
                            displayName = pendingExtractionName,
                            initialFolder = exportFolderValue?.let { Uri.parse(it) }
                        )
                    )
                    busy = false
                }
            }.onFailure {
                message = it.message ?: "Unable to extract file"
                busy = false
            }
        }
    }
    ScreenHeader("APK Inspector", "Browse archive contents and resources", onBack)
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
        FileChoiceCard("APK ARCHIVE", apk, "Choose an APK to explore") { picker.launch(arrayOf("application/vnd.android.package-archive", "application/octet-stream", "*/*")) }
        if (busy) LinearLoading()
        apk?.let {
            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                MetricPill("${entries.size} entries")
                MetricPill(readableBytes(it.length()))
                MetricPill("ZIP archive")
            }
            OutlinedTextField(value = query, onValueChange = { query = it }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), singleLine = true, leadingIcon = { Icon(Icons.Filled.Search, null) }, placeholder = { Text("Search files and paths") })
            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp), modifier = Modifier.horizontalScroll(rememberScrollState())) {
                listOf("All", "Images", "Resources", "XML", "Assets", "Native libraries", "Manifest", "Fonts", "Bytecode").forEach { item ->
                    androidx.compose.material3.FilterChip(selected = category == item, onClick = { category = item }, label = { Text(item, fontSize = 10.sp) })
                }
            }
            selectedEntry?.let { selected ->
                if (textPreview != null) {
                    CardBlock {
                        Text(selected.path, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, fontSize = 12.sp)
                        textPreview?.let { Text(it.take(5000), fontSize = 10.sp, lineHeight = 14.sp, fontFamily = androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontFamily.Monospace, maxLines = 24, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis) }
                    }
                }
            }
            val filtered = entries.filter { entry ->
                (category == "All" || entry.category == category) && (query.isBlank() || entry.path.contains(query, true))
            }
            Text("${filtered.size} items", color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 11.sp)
            filtered.take(500).forEach { entry ->
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)).background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.44f)).clickable {
                        selectedEntry = entry
                        preview = null
                        showImagePreview = false
                        textPreview = null
                        message = null
                        val sourceApk = apk
                        if (sourceApk != null && entry.category in setOf("XML", "Resources", "Assets", "Manifest")) scope.launch {
                            val result = runCatching { withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { archiveRepository.readTextPreview(sourceApk, entry.path) } }
                            if (selectedEntry == entry && apk == sourceApk) {
                                textPreview = result.getOrNull()
                                result.exceptionOrNull()?.let { message = it.message ?: "Unable to preview this item" }
                                if (entry.category == "Manifest" && textPreview == null) message = "AndroidManifest.xml is stored as binary Android XML. Extract the file to inspect it with an XML decoder."
                            }
                        }
                        if (entry.isPreviewableImage && sourceApk != null) scope.launch {
                            val image = runCatching {
                                withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                                    val temp = File(context.cacheDir, "preview-${System.currentTimeMillis()}.img")
                                    try {
                                        archiveRepository.extract(sourceApk, entry.path, temp)
                                        decodeImagePreview(temp)
                                    } finally {
                                        temp.delete()
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            if (selectedEntry == entry && apk == sourceApk) {
                                preview = image.getOrNull()
                                showImagePreview = preview != null
                                image.exceptionOrNull()?.let { message = it.message ?: "Unable to preview this image" }
                                if (image.isSuccess && preview == null) message = "This image format cannot be previewed. You can still extract it."
                            }
                        }
                    }.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Icon(if (entry.directory) Icons.Filled.FolderOpen else Icons.Filled.InsertDriveFile, null, tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary)
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(horizontal = 10.dp)) {
                        Text(entry.path.substringAfterLast('/').ifBlank { entry.path }, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
                        Text("${entry.category} · ${readableBytes(entry.size)}", fontSize = 10.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
                    }
                    if (!entry.directory) IconButton(onClick = { requestExtraction(entry) }) { Icon(Icons.Filled.Download, null) }
                }
            }
            if (filtered.size > 500) Text("Showing the first 500 items. Refine your search to find more.", color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 11.sp)
        }
        message?.let { InlineNotice(it, false) }
    }
    val image = preview
    val imageEntry = selectedEntry
    if (showImagePreview && image != null && imageEntry != null) {
        Dialog(onDismissRequest = { showImagePreview = false }) {
            Surface(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface,
                tonalElevation = 8.dp
            ) {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(18.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(13.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(imageEntry.path.substringAfterLast('/'), fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
                            Text(imageEntry.path, fontSize = 10.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
                        }
                        IconButton(onClick = { showImagePreview = false }) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.Close, contentDescription = "Close image preview", tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
                        }
                    }
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(310.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                            .background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.58f)),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        androidx.compose.foundation.Image(
                            image.asImageBitmap(),
                            contentDescription = imageEntry.path,
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(10.dp),
                            contentScale = ContentScale.Fit
                        )
                    }
                    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text("${image.width} × ${image.height}", fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface)
                            Text(readableBytes(imageEntry.size), fontSize = 10.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
                        }
                        TextButton(onClick = { showImagePreview = false }) { Text("Close") }
                        Button(onClick = {
                            showImagePreview = false
                            requestExtraction(imageEntry)
                        }) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.Download, null, modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp))
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text("Save image")
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

private fun decodeImagePreview(file: File): android.graphics.Bitmap? {
    val bounds = BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inJustDecodeBounds = true }
    BitmapFactory.decodeFile(file.absolutePath, bounds)
    if (bounds.outWidth <= 0 || bounds.outHeight <= 0) return null
    var sampleSize = 1
    while (bounds.outWidth / sampleSize > 1600 || bounds.outHeight / sampleSize > 1600) {
        sampleSize *= 2
    }
    val options = BitmapFactory.Options().apply {
        inSampleSize = sampleSize
        inPreferredConfig = android.graphics.Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888
    }
    return BitmapFactory.decodeFile(file.absolutePath, options)
}

@Composable
internal fun CertificateIntelligenceScreen(onBack: () -> Unit, keyRepository: KeyStoreRepository, entries: List<KeyStoreRepository.Entry>) {
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    var selected by remember { mutableStateOf<KeyStoreRepository.Entry?>(null) }
    var password by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var certificates by remember { mutableStateOf<List<KeyStoreRepository.CertificateDetails>>(emptyList()) }
    var compareAliases by remember { mutableStateOf<List<String>>(emptyList()) }
    var busy by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var error by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    ScreenHeader("Certificate Intelligence", "Validity, identity and fingerprint review", onBack)
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
        if (entries.isEmpty()) InlineNotice("No PKCS12 keystores are available in the local vault.", false)
        entries.forEach { entry ->
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)).background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = if (selected == entry) 0.9f else 0.4f)).clickable { selected = entry; certificates = emptyList(); error = null }.padding(14.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Icon(Icons.Filled.FolderOpen, null, tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary)
                Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(start = 10.dp)) {
                    Text(entry.displayName, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, fontSize = 13.sp)
                    Text(entry.file.name, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 10.sp)
                }
                if (selected == entry) Icon(Icons.Filled.CheckCircle, null, tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary)
            }
        }
        if (selected != null) {
            OutlinedTextField(value = password, onValueChange = { password = it; certificates = emptyList() }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), label = { Text("Keystore password") }, visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation(), keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Password), singleLine = true)
            Button(onClick = {
                val item = selected ?: return@Button
                val secret = password.toCharArray()
                scope.launch {
                    busy = true; error = null
                    try {
                        runCatching { withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { keyRepository.inspect(item.file, secret).certificates } }
                            .onSuccess { certificates = it; compareAliases = emptyList() }
                            .onFailure { error = it.message ?: "Unable to inspect certificates" }
                    } finally {
                        secret.fill('\u0000')
                        password = ""
                        busy = false
                    }
                }
            }, enabled = password.isNotEmpty() && !busy, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                if (busy) CircularProgressIndicator(Modifier.size(18.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp) else Icon(Icons.Filled.Verified, null)
                Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp)); Text("Analyze certificates")
            }
        }
        error?.let { InlineNotice(it, true) }
        certificates.forEach { certificate ->
            val dateFormat = remember { SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.US) }
            val startDate = runCatching { dateFormat.parse(certificate.notBefore) }.getOrNull()
            val endDate = runCatching { dateFormat.parse(certificate.notAfter) }.getOrNull()
            val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
            val expired = endDate?.time?.let { it < now } == true
            val days = endDate?.let { TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toDays((it.time - now).coerceAtLeast(0L)) }
            val validityLabel = when {
                startDate != null && now < startDate.time -> "Not yet valid"
                expired -> "Expired"
                days == null -> "Date unknown"
                days <= 30 -> "$days days left"
                certificate.isValidNow -> "Valid"
                else -> "Not valid"
            }
            CardBlock {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(certificate.alias, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 14.sp)
                        Text(certificate.entryType, fontSize = 10.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
                    }
                    MetricPill(validityLabel)
                }
                ComparisonValue("Subject", certificate.subject)
                ComparisonValue("Issuer", certificate.issuer)
                ComparisonValue("Valid from", certificate.notBefore)
                ComparisonValue("Valid until", certificate.notAfter)
                ComparisonValue("Public key", "${certificate.publicKeyAlgorithm} · ${certificate.publicKeySize} bits")
                ComparisonValue("Chain length", certificate.chainLength.toString())
                ComparisonValue("Self-signed", if (certificate.isSelfSigned) "Yes" else "No")
                ComparisonValue("SHA-256", certificate.sha256)
                OutlinedButton(onClick = {
                    compareAliases = if (certificate.alias in compareAliases) compareAliases - certificate.alias else (compareAliases + certificate.alias).takeLast(2)
                }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                    Text(if (certificate.alias in compareAliases) "Selected for comparison (${compareAliases.indexOf(certificate.alias) + 1}/2)" else "Select to compare")
                }
                if (validityLabel == "Expired" || validityLabel == "Not yet valid" || validityLabel == "Not valid" || (days != null && days <= 30)) {
                    InlineNotice(when { expired -> "This certificate is past its notAfter date."; validityLabel == "Not yet valid" -> "The certificate's notBefore date is in the future."; else -> "This certificate is not currently valid or expires within 30 days." }, true)
                }
            }
        }
        if (compareAliases.size == 2) {
            val left = certificates.firstOrNull { it.alias == compareAliases[0] }
            val right = certificates.firstOrNull { it.alias == compareAliases[1] }
            if (left != null && right != null) {
                SectionLabel("CERTIFICATE COMPARISON")
                CardBlock {
                    ComparisonValue("Aliases", "${left.alias}  ↔  ${right.alias}")
                    ComparisonValue("Same certificate", if (left.sha256.equals(right.sha256, true)) "Yes · identical SHA-256" else "No · different fingerprints", left.sha256 != right.sha256)
                    ComparisonValue("Same issuer", if (left.issuer == right.issuer) "Yes" else "No", left.issuer != right.issuer)
                    ComparisonValue("Same public key type", if (left.publicKeyAlgorithm == right.publicKeyAlgorithm) "Yes" else "No", left.publicKeyAlgorithm != right.publicKeyAlgorithm)
                    ComparisonValue("Validity dates", "${left.notBefore} – ${left.notAfter}\n${right.notBefore} – ${right.notAfter}")
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun ReportHistoryScreen(
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    store: ApkReportHistoryStore,
    onOpen: (ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport) -> Unit
) {
    var reports by remember { mutableStateOf(store.getReports()) }
    var favorites by remember { mutableStateOf(store.favoriteIds()) }
    var favoritesOnly by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var selectedToDelete by remember { mutableStateOf<ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport?>(null) }
    ScreenHeader("Report Library", "Saved inspections and recent APK activity", onBack)
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Text("${reports.size} reports", color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 12.sp)
            Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
            androidx.compose.material3.FilterChip(selected = favoritesOnly, onClick = { favoritesOnly = !favoritesOnly }, label = { Text("Favorites") }, leadingIcon = { Icon(Icons.Filled.Bookmark, null) })
        }
        if (reports.isEmpty()) {
            CardBlock {
                Icon(Icons.Filled.InsertDriveFile, null, tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp))
                Text("Your report library is empty", fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                Text("Every APK inspection or successful signing report will appear here.", fontSize = 12.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
            }
        }
        reports.filter { !favoritesOnly || it.reportId in favorites }.forEach { report ->
            CardBlock {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    if (report.iconBase64.isNotBlank()) {
                        val bitmap = remember(report.reportId) { runCatching { val bytes = android.util.Base64.decode(report.iconBase64, android.util.Base64.DEFAULT); BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(bytes, 0, bytes.size) }.getOrNull() }
                        if (bitmap != null) androidx.compose.foundation.Image(bitmap.asImageBitmap(), null, modifier = Modifier.size(48.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(13.dp)))
                        else DefaultAppIcon()
                    } else DefaultAppIcon()
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(horizontal = 10.dp)) {
                        Text(report.appName.ifBlank { report.fileName }, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
                        Text(report.packageName.ifBlank { report.fileName }, fontSize = 10.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
                        Text("${report.versionName} · ${report.reportId}", fontSize = 10.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
                    }
                    IconButton(onClick = { store.setFavorite(report.reportId, report.reportId !in favorites); favorites = store.favoriteIds() }) {
                        Icon(if (report.reportId in favorites) Icons.Filled.Bookmark else Icons.Filled.BookmarkBorder, null, tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary)
                    }
                    IconButton(onClick = { selectedToDelete = report }) { Icon(Icons.Filled.DeleteOutline, null, tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error) }
                }
                HorizontalDivider(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Text(formatDate(report.inspectedAt), fontSize = 10.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
                    Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                    MetricPill(if (report.verified) "Signature verified" else "Not verified")
                    TextButton(onClick = { onOpen(report) }) { Text("Open report") }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    selectedToDelete?.let { report ->
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { selectedToDelete = null },
            title = { Text("Delete report?") },
            text = { Text("${report.appName.ifBlank { report.fileName }} will be removed from the local report library.") },
            confirmButton = { TextButton(onClick = { store.remove(report.reportId); reports = store.getReports(); favorites = store.favoriteIds(); selectedToDelete = null }) { Text("Delete") } },
            dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = { selectedToDelete = null }) { Text("Cancel") } }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ScreenHeader(title: String, subtitle: String, onBack: () -> Unit) {
    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(bottom = 20.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
        IconButton(onClick = onBack) { Icon(Icons.Filled.ArrowBack, null) }
        Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(start = 8.dp)) {
            Text(title, fontSize = 24.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground)
            Text(subtitle, fontSize = 11.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun FileChoiceCard(label: String, file: File?, hint: String, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    Card(onClick = onClick, colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.52f)), shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)) {
        Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(15.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Icon(Icons.Filled.FolderOpen, null, tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(25.dp))
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(horizontal = 12.dp)) {
                Text(label, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 9.sp, letterSpacing = 1.sp)
                Text(file?.name ?: hint, fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
                file?.let { Text(readableBytes(it.length()), color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 10.sp) }
            }
            Text("Browse", color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary, fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ComparisonSummary(before: ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport, after: ApkToolsRepository.ApkReport) {
    CardBlock {
        Text("Build comparison", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 16.sp)
        Text("${before.appName.ifBlank { before.fileName }}  →  ${after.appName.ifBlank { after.fileName }}", fontSize = 12.sp)
        Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
            MetricPill("${after.requestedPermissions.size - before.requestedPermissions.size} permission net change")
            MetricPill(if (after.verified) "New signature verified" else "New signature not verified")
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ComparisonValue(label: String, value: String, changed: Boolean = false) {
    Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 3.dp)) {
        Text(label, fontSize = 10.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
        Text(value, fontSize = 11.sp, color = if (changed) MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface, lineHeight = 16.sp)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun CardBlock(content: @Composable () -> Unit) {
    Card(colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.38f)), shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)) {
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(15.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(7.dp)) {
            content()
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SectionLabel(text: String) {
    Text(text, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, letterSpacing = 1.1.sp, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 7.dp))
}

@Composable
private fun MetricPill(text: String) {
    Box(Modifier.clip(CircleShape).background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary.copy(alpha = 0.10f)).padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 6.dp)) {
        Text(text, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary, fontSize = 9.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun InlineNotice(message: String, error: Boolean) {
    Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(13.dp)).background((if (error) MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary).copy(alpha = 0.10f)).padding(12.dp)) {
        Text(message, color = if (error) MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface, fontSize = 11.sp)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun DefaultAppIcon() {
    Box(Modifier.size(48.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(13.dp)).background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary.copy(alpha = 0.13f)), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
        Icon(Icons.Filled.InsertDriveFile, null, tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun LinearLoading() {
    androidx.compose.material3.LinearProgressIndicator(Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
}

private fun copySelectedApk(context: Context, uri: Uri): File {
    val sourceName = context.contentResolver.query(uri, arrayOf(android.provider.OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME), null, null, null)?.use { cursor ->
        if (cursor.moveToFirst()) cursor.getString(0) else null
    }?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "selected.apk"
    val safeName = sourceName.replace(Regex("[^A-Za-z0-9._-]"), "_").take(100).ifBlank { "selected.apk" }
    val target = File(context.cacheDir, "${System.currentTimeMillis()}-$safeName")
    try {
        context.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use { input -> target.outputStream().use { output -> input.copyTo(output) } }
            ?: throw IllegalStateException("Unable to read selected file")
        require(target.length() > 0L) { "Selected file is empty" }
        return target
    } catch (error: Exception) {
        target.delete()
        throw error
    }
}

private fun readableBytes(value: Long): String = when {
    value >= 1_073_741_824L -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f GB", value / 1_073_741_824.0)
    value >= 1_048_576L -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f MB", value / 1_048_576.0)
    value >= 1024L -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f KB", value / 1024.0)
    else -> "$value B"
}

private fun formatDate(value: Long) = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm", Locale.US).format(Date(value))

package com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.apk

import android.content.Context
