    var selectedEntry by remember { mutableStateOf<KeyStoreRepository.Entry?>(null) }
    var inspectPassword by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var editFileName by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var editOldAlias by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var editNewAlias by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var editNewPassword by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var editConfirmPassword by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var editDisplayName by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var editDescription by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var pendingKeystoreExport by remember { mutableStateOf<PendingKeystoreExport?>(null) }

    fun refresh() {
        entries.clear()
        entries.addAll(repository.list())
    }

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) { refresh() }
    LaunchedEffect(page) { onFullScreenChanged(page != "overview") }
    androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler(enabled = page != "overview") {
        if (page == "edit") {
            editNewPassword = ""
            editConfirmPassword = ""
            page = "details"
        } else {
            page = "overview"
            importPassword = ""
            inspectPassword = ""
        }
        message = null
    }

    val exportLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(SaveDocumentContract()) { uri ->
        val pending = pendingKeystoreExport
        if (uri != null && pending != null) {
            scope.launch {
                busy = true
                runCatching {
                    withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        context.contentResolver.openOutputStream(uri, "wt")?.use { output ->
                            output.write(pending.bytes)
                            output.flush()
                        } ?: error("Unable to write destination")
                    }
                }.onSuccess {
                    message = if (arabic) "تم تصدير نسخة من الملف" else "Keystore exported successfully"
                    messageError = false
                }.onFailure {
                    message = it.message ?: if (arabic) "تعذر تصدير الملف" else "Unable to export keystore"
                    messageError = true
                }
                pending.bytes.fill(0)
                pendingKeystoreExport = null
                busy = false
            }
        } else {
            pending?.bytes?.fill(0)
            pendingKeystoreExport = null
        }
    }

    val exportFolderPicker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocumentTree()) { uri ->
        val pending = pendingKeystoreExport
        if (uri != null && pending != null) {
            FileExportManager.persistFolderPermission(context, uri)
            scope.launch {
                busy = true
                runCatching {
                    appPrefs.setExportFolderUri(uri.toString())
                    withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        val outputUri = FileExportManager.createFileInFolder(context, uri, "application/octet-stream", pending.fileName)
                        try {
                            context.contentResolver.openOutputStream(outputUri, "wt")?.use { output ->
                                output.write(pending.bytes)
                                output.flush()
                            } ?: error("Unable to write destination")
                        } catch (error: Exception) {
                            runCatching { android.provider.DocumentsContract.deleteDocument(context.contentResolver, outputUri) }
                            throw error
                        }
                    }
                }.onSuccess {
                    message = if (arabic) "تم تصدير نسخة من الملف" else "Keystore exported successfully"
                    messageError = false
                }.onFailure {
                    message = it.message ?: if (arabic) "تعذر تصدير الملف" else "Unable to export keystore"
                    messageError = true
                }
                pending.bytes.fill(0)
                pendingKeystoreExport = null
                busy = false
            }
        } else {
            pending?.bytes?.fill(0)
            pendingKeystoreExport = null
        }
    }

    val startKeystoreExport: (KeyStoreRepository.Entry) -> Unit = { entry ->
        scope.launch {
            busy = true
            message = null
            runCatching {
                val secret = inspectPassword.toCharArray()
                try {
                    withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { repository.exportBytes(entry, secret) }
                } finally {
                    secret.fill('\u0000')
                }
            }.onSuccess { bytes ->
                val fileName = FileExportManager.buildFileName(
                    pattern = exportNamePattern,
                    name = entry.name,
                    extension = "p12"
                )
                val pending = PendingKeystoreExport(fileName, bytes)
                pendingKeystoreExport = pending
                if (exportSaveMode == AppPrefs.EXPORT_SAVE_DIRECT) {
                    val folderUri = exportFolderValue?.let { Uri.parse(it) }
                    if (folderUri == null) {
                        exportFolderPicker.launch(null)
                    } else {
                        runCatching {
                            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                                val outputUri = FileExportManager.createFileInFolder(context, folderUri, "application/octet-stream", fileName)
                                try {
                                    context.contentResolver.openOutputStream(outputUri, "wt")?.use { output ->
                                        output.write(bytes)
                                        output.flush()
                                    } ?: error("Unable to write destination")
                                } catch (error: Exception) {
                                    runCatching { android.provider.DocumentsContract.deleteDocument(context.contentResolver, outputUri) }
                                    throw error
                                }
                            }
                        }.onSuccess {
                            message = if (arabic) "تم تصدير نسخة من الملف" else "Keystore exported successfully"
                            messageError = false
                            bytes.fill(0)
                            pendingKeystoreExport = null
                        }.onFailure {
                            message = it.message ?: if (arabic) "تعذر تصدير الملف" else "Unable to export keystore"
                            messageError = true
                            bytes.fill(0)
                            pendingKeystoreExport = null
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    exportLauncher.launch(
                        SaveDocumentRequest(
                            mimeType = "application/octet-stream",
                            displayName = fileName,
                            initialFolder = exportFolderValue?.let { Uri.parse(it) }
                        )
                    )
                }
            }.onFailure {
                message = it.message ?: if (arabic) "تعذر تصدير الملف" else "Unable to export keystore"
                messageError = true
            }
            busy = false
        }
    }

    val importLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocument()) { uri ->
        if (uri != null) {
            scope.launch {
                busy = true
                message = null
                runCatching {
                    runCatching {
                        context.contentResolver.takePersistableUriPermission(uri, Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                    }
                    val name = context.contentResolver.query(uri, arrayOf(android.provider.OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME), null, null, null)?.use { cursor ->
                        if (cursor.moveToFirst()) cursor.getString(0) else "Imported_Keystore"
                    } ?: "Imported_Keystore"
                    val bytes = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        context.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use { it.readBytes() } ?: error("Unable to read file")
                    }
                    withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        val secret = importPassword.toCharArray()
                        try {
                            repository.import(name, bytes, secret)
                        } finally {
                            secret.fill('\u0000')
                        }
                    }
                }.onSuccess {
                    refresh()
                    importPassword = ""
                    page = "overview"
                    message = if (arabic) "تم استيراد ملف Keystore بنجاح" else "Keystore imported successfully"
                    messageError = false
                }.onFailure {
                    message = it.message ?: if (arabic) "تعذر استيراد الملف" else "Unable to import keystore"
                    messageError = true
                }
                busy = false
            }
        }
    }

    if (page == "overview") {
        TabPage(
            title = if (arabic) "المفاتيح" else "Keys",
            subtitle = if (arabic) "أنشئ ملفات Keystore وأدر مفاتيح التوقيع بأمان" else "Create keystores and manage your signing keys securely"
        ) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(22.dp)) {
                KeysVaultHero(arabic, entries.size) {
                    selectedEntry = null
                    message = null
                    page = "create"
                }
                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(2.dp)) {
                    ToolRow(
                        title = if (arabic) "إنشاء Keystore" else "Create keystore",
                        subtitle = if (arabic) "إنشاء ملف PKCS12 جديد ومفتاح توقيع" else "Generate a PKCS12 file and signing key",
                        icon = Icons.Filled.Add,
                        onClick = { selectedEntry = null; message = null; page = "create" }
                    )
                    RowDivider()
                    ToolRow(
                        title = if (arabic) "استيراد ملف" else "Import keystore",
                        subtitle = if (arabic) "استيراد ملف P12 أو PFX من جهازك" else "Import a P12 or PFX file from your device",
                        icon = Icons.Filled.FileDownload,
                        onClick = { selectedEntry = null; importPassword = ""; message = null; page = "import" }
                    )
                }
                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(11.dp)) {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(if (arabic) "ملفات Keystore" else "Your keystores", fontSize = 19.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground)
                            Text(if (arabic) "${entries.size} ملفات في المخزن الداخلي الخاص" else "${entries.size} files in private internal storage", fontSize = 12.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
                        }
                        androidx.compose.material3.FilterChip(
                            selected = favoritesOnly,
                            onClick = { favoritesOnly = !favoritesOnly },
                            label = { Text(if (arabic) "المفضلة" else "Favorites") },
                            leadingIcon = { Icon(Icons.Filled.Star, null, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)) }
                        )
                    }
                    if (entries.isEmpty()) EmptyKeystores(arabic)
                    else {
                        val visibleEntries = entries.filter { !favoritesOnly || it.file.name in favoriteKeystores }
                        if (visibleEntries.isEmpty()) {
                            Text(if (arabic) "لا توجد ملفات مفضلة بعد." else "No favorite keystores yet.", color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 12.sp)
                        }
                        visibleEntries.forEach { entry ->
                            KeystoreItem(
                                entry = entry,
                                arabic = arabic,
                                favorite = entry.file.name in favoriteKeystores,
                                onFavorite = {
                                    val next = favoriteKeystores.toMutableSet()
                                    if (entry.file.name in next) next.remove(entry.file.name) else next.add(entry.file.name)
                                    favoriteKeystores = next
                                    favoritesPreferences.edit().putStringSet("favorite_keystores", next).apply()
                                },
                                onClick = {
                                    selectedEntry = entry
                                    inspectPassword = ""
                                    message = null
                                    page = "details"
                                }
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
                message?.let { StatusMessage(it, messageError) }
            }
        }
    } else {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.background)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(horizontal = 24.dp)
                .padding(top = 22.dp, bottom = 34.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)
        ) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                androidx.compose.material3.IconButton(onClick = {
                    if (page == "edit") {
                        editNewPassword = ""
                        editConfirmPassword = ""
                        page = "details"
                    } else {
                        page = "overview"
                        importPassword = ""
                        inspectPassword = ""
                    }
                    message = null
                }) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.ArrowBack, contentDescription = null, tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground)
                }
                Column(Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp)) {
                    Text(
                        when (page) {
                            "create" -> if (arabic) "إنشاء مفتاح" else "Create keystore"
                            "import" -> if (arabic) "استيراد مفتاح" else "Import keystore"
                            "edit" -> if (arabic) "إدارة المخزن" else "Manage keystore"
                            else -> if (arabic) "تفاصيل المفتاح" else "Keystore details"
                        },
                        fontSize = 25.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground
                    )
                    Text(
                        when (page) {
                            "create" -> if (arabic) "أنشئ هوية توقيع آمنة بصيغة PKCS12" else "Create a secure PKCS12 signing identity"
                            "import" -> if (arabic) "أضف ملف توقيع موجود إلى خزنتك" else "Add an existing signing file to your vault"
                            "edit" -> if (arabic) "إدارة الاسم وAlias وكلمة المرور" else "Manage name, alias and password"
                            else -> selectedEntry?.displayName.orEmpty()
                        },
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant
                    )
                }
            }
            when (page) {
                "create" -> CreateKeystoreForm(
                    arabic = arabic,
                    busy = busy,
                    onCancel = { page = "overview" },
                    onCreate = { options, password ->
                        scope.launch {
                            busy = true
                            message = null
                            runCatching {
                                withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                                    val secret = password.toCharArray()
                                    try {
                                        repository.create(options, secret)
                                    } finally {
                                        secret.fill('\u0000')
                                    }
                                }
                            }.onSuccess {
                                refresh()
                                page = "overview"
                                message = if (arabic) "تم إنشاء Keystore وحفظه في المخزن الداخلي الخاص" else "Keystore created in private internal storage"
                                messageError = false
                            }.onFailure {
                                message = it.message ?: if (arabic) "فشل إنشاء Keystore" else "Keystore creation failed"
                                messageError = true
                            }
                            busy = false
                        }
                    }
                )
                "import" -> {
                    ImportKeystoreForm(
                        arabic = arabic,
                        password = importPassword,
                        onPasswordChange = { importPassword = it },
                        busy = busy,
                        onCancel = { page = "overview"; importPassword = "" },
                        onChoose = { importLauncher.launch(arrayOf("application/x-pkcs12", "application/octet-stream")) }
                    )
                }
                "edit" -> selectedEntry?.let { entry ->
                    EditKeystoreForm(
                        arabic = arabic,
                        fileName = editFileName,
                        onFileNameChange = { editFileName = it },
                        displayName = editDisplayName,
                        onDisplayNameChange = { editDisplayName = it },
                        description = editDescription,
                        onDescriptionChange = { editDescription = it },
                        entry = entry,
                        aliases = entry.aliases,
                        currentPassword = inspectPassword,
                        onCurrentPasswordChange = { inspectPassword = it },
                        oldAlias = editOldAlias,
                        onOldAliasChange = { editOldAlias = it; editNewAlias = it },
                        newAlias = editNewAlias,
                        onNewAliasChange = { editNewAlias = it },
                        newPassword = editNewPassword,
                        onNewPasswordChange = { editNewPassword = it },
                        confirmPassword = editConfirmPassword,
                        onConfirmPasswordChange = { editConfirmPassword = it },
                        busy = busy,
                        onCancel = {
                            editNewPassword = ""
                            editConfirmPassword = ""
                            page = "details"
                        },
                        onSave = {
                            scope.launch {
                                busy = true
                                message = null
                                runCatching {
                                    withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                                        val currentSecret = inspectPassword.toCharArray()
                                        val nextSecret = editNewPassword.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.toCharArray()
                                        try {
                                            repository.updateKeystore(entry, editFileName, editOldAlias, editNewAlias, currentSecret, nextSecret, editDisplayName, editDescription)
                                        } finally {
                                            currentSecret.fill('\u0000')
                                            nextSecret?.fill('\u0000')
                                        }
                                    }
                                }.onSuccess { updated ->
                                    if (entry.file.name in favoriteKeystores && entry.file.name != updated.file.name) {
                                        val nextFavorites = favoriteKeystores.toMutableSet().apply { remove(entry.file.name); add(updated.file.name) }
                                        favoriteKeystores = nextFavorites
                                        favoritesPreferences.edit().putStringSet("favorite_keystores", nextFavorites).apply()
                                    }
                                    refresh()
                                    selectedEntry = updated
                                    if (editNewPassword.isNotEmpty()) inspectPassword = editNewPassword
                                    editNewPassword = ""
                                    editConfirmPassword = ""
                                    page = "details"
                                    message = if (arabic) "تم تعديل ملف Keystore بنجاح" else "Keystore updated successfully"
                                    messageError = false
                                }.onFailure {
                                    message = it.message ?: if (arabic) "تعذر تعديل الملف" else "Unable to update keystore"
                                    messageError = true
                                }
                                busy = false
                            }
                        }
                    )
                }
                else -> {
                    selectedEntry?.let { entry ->
                        KeystoreDetailsPanel(
                            entry = entry,
                            repository = repository,
                            arabic = arabic,
                            password = inspectPassword,
                            onPasswordChange = { inspectPassword = it },
                            busy = busy,
                            onUnlock = {
                                scope.launch {
                                    busy = true
                                    runCatching {
                                        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                                            val secret = inspectPassword.toCharArray()
                                            try {
                                                repository.inspect(entry.file, secret)
                                            } finally {
                                                secret.fill('\u0000')
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }.onSuccess { verified ->
                                            selectedEntry = verified
                                            message = if (arabic) "كلمة المرور صحيحة · ${verified.aliases.size} Aliases" else "Password verified · ${verified.aliases.size} aliases"
                                            messageError = false
                                        }
                                        .onFailure {
                                            message = if (arabic) "كلمة المرور غير صحيحة أو الملف تالف" else "Incorrect password or damaged keystore"
                                            messageError = true
                                        }
                                    busy = false
                                }
                            },
                            onExport = { startKeystoreExport(entry) },
                            onEdit = {
                                editFileName = entry.name
                                editDisplayName = entry.displayName
                                editDescription = entry.description
                                editOldAlias = entry.aliases.firstOrNull().orEmpty()
                                editNewAlias = entry.aliases.firstOrNull().orEmpty()
                                editNewPassword = ""
                                editConfirmPassword = ""
                                page = "edit"
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
            message?.let { StatusMessage(it, messageError) }
        }
    }
}


@Composable
private fun EditKeystoreForm(
    arabic: Boolean,
    entry: KeyStoreRepository.Entry,
    fileName: String,
    onFileNameChange: (String) -> Unit,
    displayName: String,
    onDisplayNameChange: (String) -> Unit,
    description: String,
    onDescriptionChange: (String) -> Unit,
    aliases: List<String>,
    currentPassword: String,
    onCurrentPasswordChange: (String) -> Unit,
    oldAlias: String,
    onOldAliasChange: (String) -> Unit,
    newAlias: String,
    onNewAliasChange: (String) -> Unit,
    newPassword: String,
    onNewPasswordChange: (String) -> Unit,
    confirmPassword: String,
    onConfirmPasswordChange: (String) -> Unit,
    busy: Boolean,
    onCancel: () -> Unit,
    onSave: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    var aliasMenuExpanded by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val passwordValid = if (newPassword.isEmpty()) confirmPassword.isEmpty() else newPassword.length >= 8 && newPassword == confirmPassword
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().background(colors.surface, RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
            .border(1.dp, colors.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.55f), RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)).padding(18.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
    ) {
        Text(if (arabic) "إدارة ملف Keystore" else "Manage Keystore", fontSize = 19.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = colors.onSurface)
        Text(if (arabic) "تعديلات آمنة على المخزن الحالي دون توليد مفتاح جديد." else "Safely update this keystore without generating a new key.", color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 12.sp)
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().background(colors.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.42f), RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)).padding(14.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
        ) {
            Text(if (arabic) "معلومات المخزن" else "KEYSTORE OVERVIEW", fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = colors.primary, letterSpacing = 1.sp)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "النوع" else "Format", entry.type)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "الحجم" else "File size", "${(entry.size / 1024f).let { String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", it) }} KB")
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "عدد المدخلات" else "Entries", entry.aliases.size.toString())
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "مفاتيح خاصة" else "Private keys", entry.privateKeyCount.toString())
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "شهادات موثوقة" else "Trusted certificates", entry.trustedCertificateCount.toString())
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "المزوّد" else "Provider", entry.provider.ifBlank { "—" })
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "آخر تعديل" else "Last modified", DateFormat.getDateTimeInstance().format(Date(entry.modified)))
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "المسار" else "Location", entry.file.absolutePath)
            entry.certificates.forEach { certificate ->
                HorizontalDivider(color = colors.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "Alias" else "Alias", certificate.alias)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "Subject" else "Subject", certificate.subject)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "Issuer" else "Issuer", certificate.issuer)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "نوع الإدخال" else "Entry type", certificate.entryType)
                DetailLine("Subject", certificate.subject)
                DetailLine("Issuer", certificate.issuer)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "الرقم التسلسلي" else "Serial number", certificate.serialNumber)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "إصدار X.509" else "X.509 version", certificate.version)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "خوارزمية التوقيع" else "Signature algorithm", certificate.signatureAlgorithm)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "الخوارزمية" else "Key algorithm", certificate.publicKeyAlgorithm)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "حجم المفتاح" else "Key size", "${certificate.publicKeySize} bit")
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "صالح من" else "Valid from", certificate.notBefore)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "ينتهي في" else "Valid until", certificate.notAfter)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "نوع الشهادة" else "Certificate profile", if (certificate.isSelfSigned) "Self-signed" else "Not self-signed")
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "قيود CA" else "Basic constraints", certificate.basicConstraints)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "طول السلسلة" else "Chain length", certificate.chainLength.toString())
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "سلسلة الشهادات" else "Certificate chain", certificate.chainSubjects)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "استخدامات المفتاح" else "Key usage", certificate.keyUsage)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "الاستخدامات الممتدة" else "Extended key usage", certificate.extendedKeyUsage)
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "أسماء بديلة SAN" else "Subject alternative names", certificate.subjectAlternativeNames)
                DetailLine("SHA-1", certificate.sha1)
                DetailLine("SHA-256", certificate.sha256)
                DetailLine("MD5", certificate.md5)
            }
        }
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "كلمة المرور الحالية" else "Current password")
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = currentPassword,
            onValueChange = onCurrentPasswordChange,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation(),
            label = { Text(if (arabic) "تحقق من المخزن" else "Verify this keystore") }
        )
        HorizontalDivider(color = colors.outlineVariant)
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "اسم الملف" else "File name")
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = fileName,
            onValueChange = onFileNameChange,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            label = { Text(if (arabic) "اسم ملف Keystore" else "Keystore file name") }
        )
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "اسم العرض" else "Display name")
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = displayName,
            onValueChange = onDisplayNameChange,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            label = { Text(if (arabic) "الاسم الظاهر في قائمة المفاتيح" else "Name shown in the key list") }
        )
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "الوصف · اختياري" else "Description · optional")
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = description,
            onValueChange = onDescriptionChange,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            minLines = 2,
            maxLines = 3,
            label = { Text(if (arabic) "ملاحظة عن هذا المخزن" else "A note about this keystore") }
        )
        Box {
            OutlinedButton(onClick = { aliasMenuExpanded = true }, enabled = aliases.isNotEmpty() && !busy, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                Text(if (arabic) "Alias المحدد: $oldAlias" else "Selected alias: $oldAlias")
            }
            DropdownMenu(expanded = aliasMenuExpanded, onDismissRequest = { aliasMenuExpanded = false }) {
                aliases.forEach { alias ->
                    DropdownMenuItem(text = { Text(alias) }, onClick = {
                        onOldAliasChange(alias)
                        aliasMenuExpanded = false
                    })
                }
            }
        }
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = newAlias,
            onValueChange = onNewAliasChange,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            label = { Text(if (arabic) "اسم Alias الجديد" else "New alias")
            }
        )
        HorizontalDivider(color = colors.outlineVariant)
        Text(if (arabic) "تغيير كلمة المرور" else "CHANGE PASSWORD", fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = colors.primary, letterSpacing = 1.sp)
        Text(if (arabic) "اترك الحقلين فارغين للإبقاء على كلمة المرور الحالية." else "Leave both fields empty to keep the current password.", color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 11.sp)
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = newPassword,
            onValueChange = onNewPasswordChange,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation(),
            label = { Text(if (arabic) "كلمة المرور الجديدة" else "New password") },
            supportingText = { Text(if (arabic) "8 أحرف على الأقل" else "At least 8 characters") },
            isError = newPassword.isNotEmpty() && newPassword.length < 8
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = confirmPassword,
            onValueChange = onConfirmPasswordChange,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation(),
            label = { Text(if (arabic) "تأكيد كلمة المرور الجديدة" else "Confirm new password") },
            isError = confirmPassword.isNotEmpty() && newPassword != confirmPassword
        )
        Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
            OutlinedButton(onClick = onCancel, enabled = !busy, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(if (arabic) "إلغاء" else "Cancel")
            }
            Button(
                onClick = onSave,
                enabled = !busy && currentPassword.isNotEmpty() && fileName.isNotBlank() && oldAlias.isNotBlank() && newAlias.isNotBlank() && passwordValid && (newPassword.isEmpty() || confirmPassword.isNotEmpty()),
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1.3f)
            ) {
                if (busy) CircularProgressIndicator(Modifier.size(17.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp)
                Text(if (arabic) "حفظ التعديلات" else "Save changes", modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp))
            }
        }
    }
}


@Composable
private fun ImportKeystoreForm(
    arabic: Boolean,
    password: String,
    onPasswordChange: (String) -> Unit,
    busy: Boolean,
    onCancel: () -> Unit,
    onChoose: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().background(colors.surface, RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
            .border(1.dp, colors.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.55f), RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)).padding(20.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(15.dp)
    ) {
        Box(Modifier.size(54.dp).background(colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.12f), CircleShape), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            Icon(Icons.Filled.FileDownload, null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(26.dp))
        }
        Text(if (arabic) "أضف ملفك إلى الخزنة" else "Add a keystore to your vault", fontSize = 19.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = colors.onSurface)
        Text(
            if (arabic) "اختر ملف PKCS12 بصيغة P12 أو PFX. سيتم حفظ نسخة داخل مساحة KeyForge الخاصة." else "Choose a PKCS12 file in P12 or PFX format. A copy will be stored in KeyForge's private space.",
            fontSize = 12.sp, lineHeight = 19.sp, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = password,
            onValueChange = onPasswordChange,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            label = { Text(if (arabic) "كلمة مرور الملف" else "Keystore password") },
            visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation(),
            singleLine = true
        )
        Button(onClick = onChoose, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), enabled = password.isNotEmpty() && !busy) {
            if (busy) CircularProgressIndicator(Modifier.size(18.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp)
            else Icon(Icons.Filled.FolderOpen, null, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
            Text(if (arabic) "اختيار ملف P12 / PFX" else "Choose P12 / PFX file", modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 9.dp))
        }
        OutlinedButton(onClick = onCancel, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), enabled = !busy) {
            Text(if (arabic) "إلغاء" else "Cancel")
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun KeystoreDetailsPanel(
    entry: KeyStoreRepository.Entry,
    repository: KeyStoreRepository,
    arabic: Boolean,
    password: String,
    onPasswordChange: (String) -> Unit,
    busy: Boolean,
    onUnlock: () -> Unit,
    onExport: () -> Unit,
    onEdit: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().background(colors.surface, RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
            .border(1.dp, colors.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.55f), RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)).padding(18.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(13.dp)
    ) {
        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Box(Modifier.size(44.dp).background(colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.12f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                Icon(Icons.Filled.VpnKey, null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
            }
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(start = 12.dp)) {
                Text(entry.displayName, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 16.sp, color = colors.onSurface)
                Text("PKCS12  ·  ${entry.name}.p12", fontSize = 10.sp, color = colors.primary)
                if (entry.description.isNotBlank()) Text(entry.description, fontSize = 11.sp, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant)
            }
        }
        HorizontalDivider(color = colors.outlineVariant)
        DetailLine(if (arabic) "النوع" else "Format", entry.type)
        DetailLine(if (arabic) "الحجم" else "Size", "${(entry.size / 1024f).let { String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", it) }} KB")
        DetailLine(if (arabic) "عدد المدخلات" else "Entries", entry.aliases.size.toString())
        DetailLine(if (arabic) "المفاتيح الخاصة" else "Private key entries", entry.privateKeyCount.toString())
        DetailLine(if (arabic) "الشهادات الموثوقة" else "Trusted certificates", entry.trustedCertificateCount.toString())
        DetailLine(if (arabic) "المزوّد" else "Provider", entry.provider.ifBlank { "—" })
        DetailLine(if (arabic) "آخر تعديل" else "Modified", DateFormat.getDateTimeInstance().format(Date(entry.modified)))
        DetailLine(if (arabic) "المسار" else "File location", entry.file.absolutePath)
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = password,
            onValueChange = onPasswordChange,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            label = { Text(if (arabic) "كلمة مرور Keystore" else "Keystore password") },
            visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation(),
            singleLine = true,
            supportingText = { Text(if (arabic) "افتح الملف لعرض Aliases ومعلومات الشهادة." else "Unlock the file to view aliases and certificate information.") }
        )
        Button(onClick = onUnlock, enabled = password.isNotEmpty() && !busy, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
            if (busy) CircularProgressIndicator(Modifier.size(17.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp)
            Text(if (arabic) "فتح وعرض التفاصيل" else "Unlock and inspect", modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp))
        }
        OutlinedButton(onClick = onExport, enabled = !busy && password.isNotEmpty(), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
            Icon(Icons.Filled.FileUpload, null, modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp))
            Text(if (arabic) "تصدير نسخة احتياطية" else "Export backup", modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp))
        }
        OutlinedButton(onClick = onEdit, enabled = !busy && password.isNotEmpty() && entry.aliases.isNotEmpty(), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
            Icon(Icons.Filled.Security, null, modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp))
            Text(if (arabic) "تعديل ملف Keystore" else "Edit Keystore", modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp))
        }
        if (entry.aliases.isNotEmpty()) {
            HorizontalDivider(color = colors.outlineVariant)
            Text("ALIASES", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = colors.primary, fontSize = 10.sp, letterSpacing = 1.sp)
            entry.aliases.forEach { DetailLine("•", it) }
        }
        entry.certificates.forEach { certificate ->
            HorizontalDivider(color = colors.outlineVariant)
            Text(if (arabic) "تفاصيل الشهادة" else "Certificate details", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 15.sp, color = colors.onSurface)
            Surface(
                color = if (certificate.isValidNow) colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.10f) else colors.errorContainer,
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    if (certificate.isValidNow) {
                        if (arabic) "الشهادة صالحة حاليًا" else "Certificate is currently valid"
                    } else {
                        if (arabic) "الشهادة منتهية أو غير صالحة زمنيًا" else "Certificate is expired or not currently valid"
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 11.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = colors.onSurface
                )
            }
            DetailLine("Alias", certificate.alias)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "نوع الإدخال" else "Entry type", if (arabic) if (certificate.entryType == "Private key entry") "مفتاح خاص" else "شهادة موثوقة" else certificate.entryType)
            DetailLine("Subject", certificate.subject)
            DetailLine("Issuer", certificate.issuer)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "الرقم التسلسلي" else "Serial number", certificate.serialNumber)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "إصدار X.509" else "X.509 version", certificate.version)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "خوارزمية التوقيع" else "Signature algorithm", certificate.signatureAlgorithm)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "خوارزمية المفتاح" else "Public key algorithm", certificate.publicKeyAlgorithm)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "حجم المفتاح" else "Public key size", "${certificate.publicKeySize} bit")
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "صالح من" else "Valid from", certificate.notBefore)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "ينتهي في" else "Expires", certificate.notAfter)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "نوع الشهادة" else "Certificate profile", if (certificate.isSelfSigned) "Self-signed" else "Not self-signed")
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "قيود CA" else "Basic constraints", certificate.basicConstraints)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "طول السلسلة" else "Chain length", certificate.chainLength.toString())
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "سلسلة الشهادات" else "Certificate chain", certificate.chainSubjects)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "استخدامات المفتاح" else "Key usage", certificate.keyUsage)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "الاستخدامات الممتدة" else "Extended key usage", certificate.extendedKeyUsage)
            DetailLine(if (arabic) "أسماء بديلة SAN" else "Subject alternative names", certificate.subjectAlternativeNames)
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().background(colors.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.55f), RoundedCornerShape(15.dp)).padding(12.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(9.dp)
            ) {
                Text(if (arabic) "بصمات الشهادة" else "CERTIFICATE FINGERPRINTS", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 10.sp, letterSpacing = 1.sp, color = colors.primary)
                DetailLine("SHA-1", certificate.sha1)
                DetailLine("SHA-256", certificate.sha256)
                DetailLine("MD5", certificate.md5)
            }
            val usage = repository.signingUsage(certificate.sha256)
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().background(colors.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.42f), RoundedCornerShape(15.dp)).padding(12.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
            ) {
                Text(if (arabic) "إحصائيات الاستخدام داخل KeyForge" else "KEYFORGE USAGE STATISTICS", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 10.sp, letterSpacing = 1.sp, color = colors.primary)
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(usage.successCount.toString(), fontSize = 22.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = colors.primary)
                        Text(if (arabic) "توقيع ناجح" else "Successful", fontSize = 10.sp, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant)
                    }
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(usage.failureCount.toString(), fontSize = 22.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = colors.onSurface)
                        Text(if (arabic) "محاولة فاشلة" else "Failed", fontSize = 10.sp, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant)
                    }
                }
                DetailLine(if (arabic) "آخر استخدام" else "Last used", if (usage.lastUsed == 0L) (if (arabic) "لم يُسجل بعد" else "Not recorded yet") else DateFormat.getDateTimeInstance().format(Date(usage.lastUsed)))
                if (usage.records.isEmpty()) {
                    Text(if (arabic) "سيبدأ التسجيل عند تنفيذ عمليات توقيع APK من داخل KeyForge." else "Tracking starts when APK signing is performed inside KeyForge.", color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 11.sp, lineHeight = 16.sp)
                } else {
                    usage.records.take(8).forEach { record ->
                        Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Column(Modifier.weight(1f), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(2.dp)) {
                                Text(record.apkName, fontSize = 11.sp, color = colors.onSurface, maxLines = 1)
                                Text(DateFormat.getDateTimeInstance().format(Date(record.timestamp)), fontSize = 9.sp, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant)
                            }
                            Text(if (record.success) (if (arabic) "نجح" else "Success") else (if (arabic) "فشل" else "Failed"), color = if (record.success) colors.primary else colors.error, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun StatusMessage(message: String, error: Boolean) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    Surface(
        color = if (error) colors.errorContainer else colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.10f),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
    ) {
        Row(Modifier.padding(14.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Icon(if (error) Icons.Filled.Security else Icons.Filled.CheckCircle, null, tint = if (error) colors.error else colors.primary)
            Text(message, modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 10.dp), color = colors.onSurface, fontSize = 13.sp)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ActionCard(title: String, subtitle: String, icon: androidx.compose.ui.graphics.vector.ImageVector, modifier: Modifier = Modifier, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(21.dp)
    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .pressable(0.985f, onClick)
            .background(Brush.linearGradient(listOf(colors.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.78f), colors.surface)), shape)
            .border(1.dp, colors.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.62f), shape)
            .padding(15.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(13.dp)
    ) {
        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Box(Modifier.size(42.dp).background(colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.13f), RoundedCornerShape(13.dp)), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                Icon(icon, null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(21.dp))
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
            Icon(Icons.Filled.ChevronRight, null, tint = colors.onSurfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.7f), modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
        }
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
            Text(title, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 13.sp, color = colors.onSurface)
            Text(subtitle, fontSize = 10.sp, lineHeight = 15.sp, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant)
        }
        Text(if (isArabicLocale()) "ابدأ الآن" else "GET STARTED", fontSize = 9.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, letterSpacing = 1.1.sp, color = colors.primary)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun KeysVaultHero(arabic: Boolean, count: Int, onCreate: () -> Unit) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
        com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.components.HeroCard(
            eyebrow = if (arabic) "خزنة المفاتيح الآمنة" else "SECURE KEY VAULT",
            title = if (arabic) "مفاتيحك تحت سيطرتك." else "Your signing keys. Your control.",
            subtitle = if (arabic) "إدارة آمنة لهويات التوقيع بصيغة PKCS12" else "Secure management for PKCS12 signing identities",
            icon = Icons.Filled.Security,
            onClick = onCreate
        )
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 3.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(9.dp)
        ) {
            Surface(color = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.12f), shape = RoundedCornerShape(30.dp)) {
                Row(Modifier.padding(horizontal = 11.dp, vertical = 7.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.Lock, null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(13.dp))
                    Text("PKCS12", modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 6.dp), color = colors.primary, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                }
            }
            Text(if (arabic) "$count ملفات محفوظة" else "$count stored keystores", color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 11.sp)
            Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
            Text(if (arabic) "تخزين داخلي خاص" else "PRIVATE VAULT", color = colors.onSurfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.7f), fontSize = 9.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, letterSpacing = 0.8.sp)
        }
    }
}

private data class CountryChoice(val code: String, val name: String, val englishName: String)

@Composable
private fun CreateKeystoreForm(
    arabic: Boolean,
    busy: Boolean,
    onCancel: () -> Unit,
    onCreate: (KeyStoreRepository.CreateOptions, String) -> Unit
) {
    var name by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var alias by remember { mutableStateOf("release") }
    var commonName by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var organization by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var organizationalUnit by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var countryCode by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var locality by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var stateOrProvince by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var emailAddress by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var password by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var confirmation by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var keyAlgorithm by remember { mutableStateOf("RSA") }
    var keySize by remember { mutableStateOf(2048) }
    var validityYears by remember { mutableStateOf(25) }
    var countrySheetOpen by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var validitySheetOpen by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var countrySearch by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    val countries = remember(arabic) {
        val displayLocale = if (arabic) Locale("ar") else Locale.US
        Locale.getISOCountries().map { code ->
            val countryName = runCatching { Locale("", code).getDisplayCountry(displayLocale) }.getOrDefault(code)
            val englishName = runCatching { Locale("", code).getDisplayCountry(Locale.US) }.getOrDefault(code)
            CountryChoice(code, countryName.ifBlank { code }, englishName.ifBlank { code })
        }.sortedBy { it.name }
    }
    val countryName = countries.firstOrNull { it.code == countryCode }?.let { "${it.name} (${it.code})" }.orEmpty()
    val emailValid = emailAddress.isBlank() || android.util.Patterns.EMAIL_ADDRESS.matcher(emailAddress.trim()).matches()
    val countryValid = countryCode.isBlank() || countryCode.uppercase(Locale.ROOT) in Locale.getISOCountries()
    val valid = name.isNotBlank() && alias.isNotBlank() && password.length >= 8 &&
        password == confirmation && emailValid && countryValid
    val sizes = if (keyAlgorithm == "RSA") listOf(2048, 3072, 4096) else listOf(256, 384, 521)
    val signatureAlgorithm = when (keyAlgorithm) {
        "RSA" -> when (keySize) {
            2048 -> "SHA256withRSA"
            3072 -> "SHA384withRSA"
            else -> "SHA512withRSA"
        }
        else -> when (keySize) {
            256 -> "SHA256withECDSA"
            384 -> "SHA384withECDSA"
            else -> "SHA512withECDSA"
        }
    }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .background(colors.surface, RoundedCornerShape(22.dp))
            .border(1.dp, colors.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.65f), RoundedCornerShape(22.dp))
            .padding(18.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(13.dp)
    ) {
        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(
                    if (arabic) "إنشاء Keystore جديد" else "Create a keystore",
                    fontSize = 19.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = colors.onSurface
                )
                Text(
                    if (arabic) "أنشئ مفتاح توقيع وشهادة ذاتية محليًا" else "Generate a signing key and a local self-signed certificate",
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    color = colors.onSurfaceVariant,
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 4.dp)
                )
            }
            Icon(Icons.Filled.VpnKey, null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp))
        }

        HorizontalDivider(color = colors.outlineVariant)

        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "اسم الملف" else "Keystore name")
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = name,
            onValueChange = { name = it },
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            placeholder = { Text("MyReleaseKey") }
        )

        Surface(
            color = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.09f),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().border(1.dp, colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.22f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
        ) {
            Row(Modifier.padding(13.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Box(Modifier.size(34.dp).background(colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.14f), RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.Security, null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                }
                Column(Modifier.padding(start = 11.dp).weight(1f), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(2.dp)) {
                    Text("PKCS12", color = colors.onSurface, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 13.sp)
                    Text(
                        if (arabic) "الصيغة الأساسية · تخزين داخلي خاص" else "Primary format · private internal storage",
                        color = colors.onSurfaceVariant,
                        fontSize = 11.sp
                    )
                }
                Text(".p12", color = colors.primary, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 12.sp)
            }
        }

        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "اسم Alias" else "Key alias")
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = alias,
            onValueChange = { alias = it },
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            placeholder = { Text("release") }
        )

        HorizontalDivider(color = colors.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.75f))
        Text(
            if (arabic) "إعدادات المفتاح" else "KEY CONFIGURATION",
            color = colors.primary,
            fontSize = 10.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            letterSpacing = 1.2.sp
        )
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "خوارزمية المفتاح" else "Key algorithm")
        Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(9.dp)) {
            ChoicePill("RSA", keyAlgorithm == "RSA", Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                keyAlgorithm = "RSA"
                keySize = 2048
            }
            ChoicePill("EC", keyAlgorithm == "EC", Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                keyAlgorithm = "EC"
                keySize = 256
            }
        }
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "حجم المفتاح" else "Key size")
        Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
            sizes.forEach { size ->
                ChoicePill(
                    "$size bit",
                    keySize == size,
                    Modifier.weight(1f)
                ) { keySize = size }
            }
        }
        Text(
            (if (arabic) "خوارزمية توقيع الشهادة تلقائية: " else "Certificate signature algorithm · automatic: ") + signatureAlgorithm,
            color = colors.onSurfaceVariant,
            fontSize = 10.sp
        )
        SelectionField(
            label = if (arabic) "مدة صلاحية الشهادة" else "Certificate validity",
            value = if (arabic) "$validityYears سنة" else "$validityYears years",
            placeholder = if (arabic) "اختر مدة الصلاحية" else "Choose validity period",
            onClick = { validitySheetOpen = true }
        )

        HorizontalDivider(color = colors.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.75f))
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
            Text(
                if (arabic) "هوية الشهادة" else "CERTIFICATE IDENTITY",
                color = colors.primary,
                fontSize = 10.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                letterSpacing = 1.2.sp
            )
            Text(
                if (arabic) "كل هذه البيانات اختيارية. إذا تركتها جميعًا فارغة، سيُستخدم Alias تلقائيًا كاسم CN." else "All fields are optional. When all are blank, the Alias is used as the CN fallback.",
                color = colors.onSurfaceVariant,
                fontSize = 11.sp,
                lineHeight = 17.sp
            )
        }
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "الاسم الشائع CN · اختياري" else "Common Name (CN) · optional")
        OutlinedTextField(commonName, { commonName = it }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), singleLine = true, placeholder = { Text(if (arabic) "اسم التطبيق أو صاحب الشهادة" else "App or certificate owner") })
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "اسم الشركة O · اختياري" else "Organization (O) · optional")
        OutlinedTextField(organization, { organization = it }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), singleLine = true, placeholder = { Text(if (arabic) "اسم الشركة أو المؤسسة" else "Company or organization") })
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "القسم OU · اختياري" else "Organizational Unit (OU) · optional")
        OutlinedTextField(organizationalUnit, { organizationalUnit = it }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), singleLine = true, placeholder = { Text(if (arabic) "القسم أو الفريق" else "Department or team") })

        SelectionField(
            label = if (arabic) "الدولة · اختياري" else "Country · optional",
            value = countryName.ifBlank { countryCode },
            placeholder = if (arabic) "اختر الدولة" else "Select a country",
            onClick = {
                countrySearch = ""
                countrySheetOpen = true
            }
        )
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "رمز الدولة ISO · اختياري" else "Country code (ISO) · optional")
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = countryCode,
            onValueChange = { countryCode = it.filter { char -> char in 'A'..'Z' || char in 'a'..'z' }.take(2).uppercase(Locale.ROOT) },
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            placeholder = { Text("IQ") },
            isError = !countryValid
        )
        if (!countryValid) {
            Text(
                if (arabic) "استخدم رمز دولة صحيحًا من حرفين." else "Enter a valid two-letter country code.",
                color = colors.error,
                fontSize = 10.sp
            )
        }
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "المدينة L · اختياري" else "Locality (L) · optional")
        OutlinedTextField(locality, { locality = it }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), singleLine = true)
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "المحافظة / المنطقة ST · اختياري" else "State / Province (ST) · optional")
        OutlinedTextField(stateOrProvince, { stateOrProvince = it }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), singleLine = true)
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "البريد الإلكتروني · اختياري" else "Email address · optional")
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = emailAddress,
            onValueChange = { emailAddress = it },
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Email),
            isError = !emailValid
        )
        if (!emailValid) {
            Text(
                if (arabic) "تحقق من عنوان البريد الإلكتروني." else "Check the email address.",
                color = colors.error,
                fontSize = 10.sp
            )
        }

        HorizontalDivider(color = colors.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.75f))
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "كلمة المرور" else "Keystore password")
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = password,
            onValueChange = { password = it },
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation(),
            supportingText = { Text(if (arabic) "8 أحرف على الأقل. لن يتم حفظ كلمة المرور." else "At least 8 characters. The password is never stored.") }
        )
        FieldLabel(if (arabic) "تأكيد كلمة المرور" else "Confirm password")
        OutlinedTextField(
            value = confirmation,
            onValueChange = { confirmation = it },
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation(),
            isError = confirmation.isNotEmpty() && password != confirmation
        )

        Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
            OutlinedButton(onClick = onCancel, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f), enabled = !busy) {
                Text(if (arabic) "إلغاء" else "Cancel")
            }
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    onCreate(
                        KeyStoreRepository.CreateOptions(
                            name = name.trim(),
                            alias = alias.trim(),
                            commonName = commonName.trim(),
                            organization = organization.trim(),
                            organizationalUnit = organizationalUnit.trim(),
                            countryCode = countryCode.trim().uppercase(Locale.ROOT),
                            locality = locality.trim(),
                            stateOrProvince = stateOrProvince.trim(),
                            emailAddress = emailAddress.trim(),
                            keyAlgorithm = keyAlgorithm,
                            keySize = keySize,
                            validityYears = validityYears
                        ),
                        password
                    )
                },
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1.4f),
                enabled = valid && !busy
            ) {
                if (busy) CircularProgressIndicator(Modifier.size(17.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp)
                else Icon(Icons.Filled.Lock, null, modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp))
                Text(if (arabic) "إنشاء وحفظ" else "Create & save", modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp))
            }
        }
    }

    if (validitySheetOpen) {
        SettingsChoiceSheet(
            title = if (arabic) "مدة صلاحية الشهادة" else "Certificate validity",
            selected = validityYears.toString(),
            options = listOf(1, 3, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30).map {
                it.toString() to if (arabic) "$it سنة" else "$it years"
            },
            onSelect = {
                validityYears = it.toInt()
                validitySheetOpen = false
            },
            onDismiss = { validitySheetOpen = false }
        )
    }

    if (countrySheetOpen) {
        CountrySelectionSheet(
            arabic = arabic,
            countries = countries,
            selectedCode = countryCode,
            search = countrySearch,
            onSearchChange = { countrySearch = it },
            onSelect = {
                countryCode = it
                countrySheetOpen = false
            },
            onDismiss = { countrySheetOpen = false }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ChoicePill(
    label: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
    Surface(
        modifier = modifier
            .border(1.dp, if (selected) colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.65f) else colors.outlineVariant, shape)
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                indication = null,
                onClick = onClick
            ),
        color = if (selected) colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.12f) else colors.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.35f),
        shape = shape
    ) {
        Box(Modifier.padding(horizontal = 9.dp, vertical = 11.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            Text(
                label,
                color = if (selected) colors.primary else colors.onSurfaceVariant,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                fontWeight = if (selected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SelectionField(
    label: String,
    value: String,
    placeholder: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(7.dp)) {
        FieldLabel(label)
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                .background(colors.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.34f))
                .border(1.dp, colors.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.58f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                .clickable(
                    interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                    indication = null,
                    onClick = onClick
                )
                .padding(horizontal = 15.dp, vertical = 16.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Text(
                text = value.ifBlank { placeholder },
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                color = if (value.isBlank()) colors.onSurfaceVariant else colors.onSurface,
                fontSize = 13.sp,
                fontWeight = if (value.isBlank()) FontWeight.Normal else FontWeight.Medium,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
            Icon(Icons.Filled.ChevronRight, null, tint = colors.onSurfaceVariant, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
private fun SettingsChoiceSheet(
    title: String,
    selected: String,
    options: List<Pair<String, String>>,
    onSelect: (String) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    ModalBottomSheet(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        containerColor = colors.surface,
        contentColor = colors.onSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 28.dp, topEnd = 28.dp),
        dragHandle = {
            Box(
                Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
                    .size(width = 36.dp, height = 4.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.24f))
            )
        }
    ) {
        Column(
            Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 22.dp).padding(bottom = 28.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
        ) {
            Text(title, fontSize = 21.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 10.dp, bottom = 10.dp))
            Column(
                Modifier.fillMaxWidth().heightIn(max = 440.dp).verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
            ) {
                options.forEach { (value, label) ->
                    SettingsChoiceOption(
                        label = label,
                        selected = selected == value,
                        onClick = { onSelect(value) }
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
private fun CountrySelectionSheet(
    arabic: Boolean,
    countries: List<CountryChoice>,
    selectedCode: String,
    search: String,
    onSearchChange: (String) -> Unit,
    onSelect: (String) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    val filtered = remember(countries, search) {
        if (search.isBlank()) countries else countries.filter {
            it.name.contains(search.trim(), ignoreCase = true) ||
                it.englishName.contains(search.trim(), ignoreCase = true) ||
                it.code.contains(search.trim(), ignoreCase = true)
        }
    }
    ModalBottomSheet(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        containerColor = colors.surface,
        contentColor = colors.onSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 28.dp, topEnd = 28.dp),
        dragHandle = {
            Box(
                Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
                    .size(width = 36.dp, height = 4.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.24f))
            )
        }
    ) {
        Column(
            Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 22.dp).padding(bottom = 22.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
        ) {
            Text(
                if (arabic) "اختيار الدولة" else "Select a country",
                fontSize = 21.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 10.dp)
            )
            OutlinedTextField(
                value = search,
                onValueChange = onSearchChange,
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                singleLine = true,
                leadingIcon = { Icon(Icons.Filled.Search, contentDescription = null) },
                placeholder = { Text(if (arabic) "ابحث عن دولة أو رمزها" else "Search country or code") }
            )
            Column(
                Modifier.fillMaxWidth().heightIn(max = 390.dp).verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
            ) {
                SettingsChoiceOption(
                    label = if (arabic) "بدون تحديد" else "Not specified",
                    selected = selectedCode.isBlank(),
                    onClick = { onSelect("") }
                )
                filtered.forEach { country ->
                    SettingsChoiceOption(
                        label = "${country.name} (${country.code})",
                        selected = selectedCode == country.code,
                        onClick = { onSelect(country.code) }
                    )
                }
                if (filtered.isEmpty()) {
                    Text(
                        if (arabic) "لا توجد دولة تطابق بحثك" else "No countries match your search",
                        color = colors.onSurfaceVariant,
                        fontSize = 13.sp,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 20.dp),
                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsChoiceOption(
    label: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
            .background(if (selected) colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.09f) else colors.surface)
            .border(
                if (selected) 1.dp else 0.dp,
                if (selected) colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.55f) else colors.surface,
                RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)
            )
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = 15.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Text(
            text = label,
            fontSize = 14.sp,
            fontWeight = if (selected) FontWeight.SemiBold else FontWeight.Medium,
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
        )
        if (selected) {
            Box(Modifier.size(22.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(colors.primary), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                Icon(Icons.Filled.Check, contentDescription = null, tint = colors.onPrimary, modifier = Modifier.size(12.dp))
            }
        } else {
            Box(Modifier.size(22.dp).clip(CircleShape).border(1.5.dp, colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.24f), CircleShape))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun KeystoreItem(entry: KeyStoreRepository.Entry, arabic: Boolean, favorite: Boolean, onFavorite: () -> Unit, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(19.dp)
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().pressable(0.99f, onClick)
            .background(Brush.linearGradient(listOf(colors.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.52f), colors.surface)), shape)
            .border(1.dp, colors.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.58f), shape)
            .padding(14.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Box(Modifier.size(44.dp).background(colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.12f), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            Icon(Icons.Filled.Key, null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp))
        }
        Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(horizontal = 12.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(5.dp)) {
            Text(entry.displayName, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = colors.onSurface, fontSize = 13.sp, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
            if (entry.description.isNotBlank()) Text(entry.description, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 10.sp, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                Text("PKCS12", color = colors.primary, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 9.sp)
                Text("·", color = colors.onSurfaceVariant)
                Text("${(entry.size / 1024f).let { String.format("%.1f", it) }} KB", color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 10.sp)
                Text("·", color = colors.onSurfaceVariant)
                Text(if (arabic) "خاص" else "PRIVATE", color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 9.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
            }
        }
        androidx.compose.material3.IconButton(onClick = onFavorite, modifier = Modifier.size(36.dp)) {
            Icon(if (favorite) Icons.Filled.Star else Icons.Filled.StarBorder, null, tint = if (favorite) colors.primary else colors.onSurfaceVariant, modifier = Modifier.size(19.dp))
        }
        Box(Modifier.size(30.dp).background(colors.surfaceVariant, CircleShape), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            Icon(Icons.Filled.ChevronRight, null, tint = colors.onSurfaceVariant, modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun EmptyKeystores(arabic: Boolean) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().background(colors.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.35f), RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)).padding(vertical = 28.dp, horizontal = 20.dp), horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally, verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
        Icon(Icons.Filled.FolderOpen, null, tint = colors.onSurfaceVariant, modifier = Modifier.size(27.dp))
        Text(if (arabic) "لا توجد ملفات Keystore بعد" else "No keystores yet", color = colors.onSurface, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
        Text(if (arabic) "أنشئ ملفًا جديدًا أو استورد ملفًا موجودًا للبدء." else "Create a new keystore or import an existing one to get started.", color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 12.sp)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun FieldLabel(value: String) {
    Text(value, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface)
}

@Composable
private fun DetailLine(label: String, value: String) {
    val fingerprint = label == "SHA-1" || label == "SHA-256" || label == "MD5"
    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top) {
        Text(label, modifier = Modifier.weight(0.82f), color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 11.sp)
        Text(
            value,
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1.18f),
            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
            fontSize = if (fingerprint) 9.sp else 11.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
            fontFamily = if (fingerprint) FontFamily.Monospace else FontFamily.Default,
            textAlign = TextAlign.End,
            maxLines = 3,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
        )
    }
}


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/ui/locale/LocaleProvider.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.locale

import android.content.res.Configuration
import android.text.TextUtils
import android.view.View
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.CompositionLocalProvider
import androidx.compose.runtime.remember
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalConfiguration
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalLayoutDirection
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalResources
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.LayoutDirection
import com.forgehub.keyforge.data.AppPrefs
import java.util.Locale

@Composable
fun LocaleProvider(tag: String, content: @Composable () -> Unit) {
    val baseContext = LocalContext.current
