    val baseConfig = LocalConfiguration.current
    val override = tag != AppPrefs.LANGUAGE_SYSTEM

    val localized = remember(baseContext, baseConfig, tag) {
        if (override) {
            val locale = Locale.forLanguageTag(tag)
            val config = Configuration(baseConfig).apply {
                setLocale(locale)
                setLayoutDirection(locale)
            }
            baseContext.createConfigurationContext(config)
        } else {
            baseContext
        }
    }

    val config = if (override) localized.resources.configuration else baseConfig
    val rtl = TextUtils.getLayoutDirectionFromLocale(config.locales[0]) == View.LAYOUT_DIRECTION_RTL
    val direction = if (rtl) LayoutDirection.Rtl else LayoutDirection.Ltr

    CompositionLocalProvider(
        LocalContext provides localized,
        LocalConfiguration provides config,
        LocalResources provides localized.resources,
        LocalLayoutDirection provides direction,
        content = content
    )
}

@Composable
fun isArabicLocale(): Boolean = LocalConfiguration.current.locales[0].language == "ar"


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/ui/motion/Motion.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.motion

import android.provider.Settings
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.ProvidableCompositionLocal
import androidx.compose.runtime.remember
import androidx.compose.runtime.staticCompositionLocalOf
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext

val LocalReduceMotion: ProvidableCompositionLocal<Boolean> = staticCompositionLocalOf { false }

@Composable
fun rememberReduceMotion(): Boolean {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    return remember(context) {
        Settings.Global.getFloat(context.contentResolver, Settings.Global.ANIMATOR_DURATION_SCALE, 1f) == 0f
    }
}

@Composable
private fun FeatureDiagram(modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    val modules = listOf(
        OnboardingModule(
            stringResource(R.string.nav_keys),
            stringResource(R.string.ob_diagram_keys),
            Icons.Filled.VpnKey
        ),
        OnboardingModule(
            stringResource(R.string.nav_apk_tools),
            stringResource(R.string.ob_diagram_apk_tools),
            Icons.Filled.Build
        ),
        OnboardingModule(
            stringResource(R.string.nav_settings),
            stringResource(R.string.ob_diagram_settings),
            Icons.Filled.Settings
        )
    )

    Box(modifier = modifier) {
        Canvas(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            val center = Offset(size.width / 2f, size.height / 2f)
            val leftTop = Offset(size.width * 0.22f, size.height * 0.22f)
            val rightTop = Offset(size.width * 0.78f, size.height * 0.22f)
            val bottomCenter = Offset(size.width * 0.5f, size.height * 0.83f)
            val connector = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.28f)
            val connectorEffect = PathEffect.dashPathEffect(floatArrayOf(5.dp.toPx(), 6.dp.toPx()))

            listOf(leftTop, rightTop, bottomCenter).forEach { point ->
                drawLine(
                    color = connector,
                    start = center,
                    end = point,
                    strokeWidth = 1.25.dp.toPx(),
                    pathEffect = connectorEffect
                )
                drawCircle(
                    color = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.65f),
                    radius = 3.dp.toPx(),
                    center = point
                )
            }

            drawCircle(
                color = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.12f),
                radius = 37.dp.toPx(),
                center = center
            )
            drawCircle(
                color = colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.34f),
                radius = 31.dp.toPx(),
                center = center,
                style = Stroke(width = 1.dp.toPx())
            )
        }

        ModuleNode(
            module = modules[0],
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.TopStart)
                .fillMaxWidth(0.45f)
                .padding(top = 8.dp)
        )
        ModuleNode(
            module = modules[1],
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.TopEnd)
                .fillMaxWidth(0.45f)
                .padding(top = 8.dp)
        )
        ModuleNode(
            module = modules[2],
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                .fillMaxWidth(0.62f)
                .padding(bottom = 8.dp)
        )

        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.Center)
                .size(64.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(colors.surface)
                .border(1.dp, colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.28f), CircleShape),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
        ) {
            Text(
                text = "KF",
                color = colors.primary,
                fontSize = 21.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                letterSpacing = (-1).sp
            )
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.ob_diagram_core),
                color = colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.6f),
                fontSize = 7.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ModuleNode(
    module: OnboardingModule,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme

    Row(
        modifier = modifier,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(34.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.11f)),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Icon(
                imageVector = module.icon,
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = colors.primary,
                modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
            )
        }
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = module.title,
                color = colors.onBackground,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                lineHeight = 15.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                maxLines = 1
            )
            Text(
                text = module.subtitle,
                color = colors.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.57f),
                fontSize = 9.sp,
                lineHeight = 12.sp,
                maxLines = 2
            )
        }
    }
}


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/ui/settings/SettingsPanels.kt ==========

fun SecurityCenterSheet(onDismiss: () -> Unit) {

            SecurityInfo(
                icon = Icons.Filled.Lock,

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun BackupRestoreSheet(onDismiss: () -> Unit) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    var backupPassword by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var confirmPassword by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var restorePassword by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var pendingBackup by remember { mutableStateOf<ByteArray?>(null) }
    var pendingRestore by remember { mutableStateOf<ByteArray?>(null) }
    var busy by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var message by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var messageError by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val createLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.CreateDocument("application/octet-stream")) { uri ->
        val data = pendingBackup
        if (uri != null && data != null) {
            scope.launch {
                busy = true
                val result = runCatching {
                    withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        context.contentResolver.openOutputStream(uri, "w")?.use { it.write(data) }
                            ?: error("Unable to open the destination file")
                    }
                }
                result.onSuccess { message = context.getString(R.string.settings_backup_created); messageError = false }
                    .onFailure { message = it.message ?: context.getString(R.string.settings_backup_failed); messageError = true }
                data.fill(0)
                pendingBackup = null
                busy = false
            }
        } else {
            data?.fill(0)
            pendingBackup = null
        }
    }

    val restoreLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocument()) { uri ->
        if (uri != null) {
            scope.launch {
                busy = true
                val result = runCatching {
                    withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        context.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use { input ->
                            val output = java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream()
                            val buffer = ByteArray(8192)
                            var total = 0L
                            while (true) {
                                val count = input.read(buffer)
                                if (count < 0) break
                                total += count
                                require(total <= 256L * 1024L * 1024L + 64L) { "Backup file is too large" }
                                output.write(buffer, 0, count)
                            }
                            output.toByteArray()
                        } ?: error("Unable to read the selected backup")
                    }
                }
                result.onSuccess {
                    pendingRestore = it
                    message = context.getString(R.string.settings_backup_enter_password)
                    messageError = false
                }.onFailure {
                    message = it.message ?: context.getString(R.string.settings_backup_failed)
                    messageError = true
                }
                busy = false
            }
        }
    }

    ModalBottomSheet(
        onDismissRequest = {
            if (!busy) {
                pendingBackup?.fill(0)
                pendingRestore?.fill(0)
                onDismiss()
            }
        },
        containerColor = colors.surface,
        contentColor = colors.onSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 28.dp, topEnd = 28.dp)
    ) {
        Column(
            Modifier.fillMaxWidth().verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()).padding(horizontal = 22.dp).padding(bottom = 28.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
        ) {
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_backup), fontSize = 22.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp))
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_backup_description), color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 12.sp, lineHeight = 18.sp)
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)).background(colors.surfaceVariant).padding(13.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Icon(Icons.Filled.Lock, null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_backup_encryption_note), fontSize = 11.sp, lineHeight = 16.sp, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 10.dp))
            }
            if (pendingRestore == null) {
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = backupPassword,
                    onValueChange = { backupPassword = it.take(128) },
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    singleLine = true,
                    label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_backup_password)) },
                    supportingText = { Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_backup_password_hint)) },
                    visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation()
                )
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = confirmPassword,
                    onValueChange = { confirmPassword = it.take(128) },
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    singleLine = true,
                    label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_backup_confirm_password)) },
                    supportingText = {
                        if (backupPassword.isNotEmpty() && confirmPassword.isNotEmpty() && backupPassword != confirmPassword) {
                            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_backup_password_mismatch), color = colors.error)
                        }
                    },
                    visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation()
                )
                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        val secret = backupPassword.toCharArray()
                        backupPassword = ""
                        confirmPassword = ""
                        scope.launch {
                            busy = true
                            val result = runCatching { withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { EncryptedBackupManager.create(context, secret) } }
                            secret.fill('\u0000')
                            result.onSuccess {
                                pendingBackup = it
                                message = context.getString(R.string.settings_backup_choose_destination)
                                messageError = false
                                createLauncher.launch("KeyForge_Backup.kfb")
                            }.onFailure {
                                message = it.message ?: context.getString(R.string.settings_backup_failed)
                                messageError = true
                            }
                            busy = false
                        }
                    },
                    enabled = !busy && backupPassword.length >= 8 && backupPassword == confirmPassword,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
                ) {
                    if (busy) CircularProgressIndicator(Modifier.size(18.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp) else Icon(Icons.Filled.Backup, null, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                    Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_backup_create), modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp))
                }
                TextButton(onClick = { restoreLauncher.launch(arrayOf("*/*")) }, enabled = !busy, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.Restore, null, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                    Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_backup_select_restore), modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp))
                }
            } else {
                OutlinedTextField(
                    value = restorePassword,
                    onValueChange = { restorePassword = it.take(128) },
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    singleLine = true,
                    label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_backup_password)) },
                    visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation()
                )
                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        val source = pendingRestore ?: return@Button
                        val secret = restorePassword.toCharArray()
                        restorePassword = ""
                        scope.launch {
                            busy = true
                            val result = runCatching { withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { EncryptedBackupManager.restore(context, secret, source) } }
                            secret.fill('\u0000')
                            result.onSuccess {
                                source.fill(0)
                                pendingRestore = null
                                message = context.getString(R.string.settings_backup_restore_success, it.restoredKeystores, it.restoredReports, it.duplicateKeystores)
                                messageError = false
                            }.onFailure {
                                message = it.message ?: context.getString(R.string.settings_backup_failed)
                                messageError = true
                            }
                            busy = false
                        }
                    },
                    enabled = !busy && restorePassword.length >= 8,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
                ) {
                    if (busy) CircularProgressIndicator(Modifier.size(18.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp) else Icon(Icons.Filled.Restore, null, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                    Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_backup_restore), modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp))
                }
                TextButton(onClick = { pendingRestore?.fill(0); pendingRestore = null; restorePassword = "" }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                    Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_backup_choose_another))
                }
            }
            if (message.isNotBlank()) {
                Text(message, color = if (messageError) colors.error else colors.primary, fontSize = 11.sp, lineHeight = 17.sp)
            }
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun PrivacySheet(onDismiss: () -> Unit) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    var confirmClear by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var cleared by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var busy by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var cacheSizeBytes by remember { mutableStateOf(calculateCacheSize(context.cacheDir)) }
    ModalBottomSheet(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        containerColor = colors.surface,
        contentColor = colors.onSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 28.dp, topEnd = 28.dp)
    ) {
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()).padding(horizontal = 22.dp).padding(bottom = 28.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(13.dp)) {
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_privacy), fontSize = 22.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp))
            SecurityInfo(Icons.Filled.CloudOff, stringResource(R.string.settings_privacy_local_title), stringResource(R.string.settings_privacy_local_body))
            SecurityInfo(Icons.Filled.Shield, stringResource(R.string.settings_privacy_permissions_title), stringResource(R.string.settings_privacy_permissions_body))
            Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)).background(colors.surfaceVariant).padding(14.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_privacy_temp_title), fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_privacy_temp_body), fontSize = 11.sp, lineHeight = 17.sp, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant)
                Text(formatBytes(cacheSizeBytes), fontSize = 12.sp, color = colors.primary, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                Button(onClick = { confirmClear = true }, enabled = !busy && cacheSizeBytes > 0L, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)) {
                    if (busy) CircularProgressIndicator(Modifier.size(17.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp)
                    else Icon(Icons.Filled.DeleteOutline, null, modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp))
                    Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_privacy_clear_temp), modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp))
                }
                if (cleared) Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_privacy_cleared), color = colors.primary, fontSize = 11.sp)
            }
        }
    }
    if (confirmClear) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { confirmClear = false },
            title = { Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_privacy_confirm_title)) },
            text = { Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_privacy_confirm_body)) },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = {
                    confirmClear = false
                    scope.launch {
                        busy = true
                        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                            context.cacheDir.listFiles()?.forEach { it.deleteRecursively() }
                        }
                        cacheSizeBytes = calculateCacheSize(context.cacheDir)
                        cleared = true
                        busy = false
                    }
                }) { Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_privacy_clear_temp)) }
            },
            dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = { confirmClear = false }) { Text(stringResource(R.string.nav_info_done)) } }
        )
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun SigningSettingsSheet(onDismiss: () -> Unit) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val prefs = remember(context) { AppPrefs(context) }
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    val storedV1 by prefs.signingDefaultV1.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = true)
    val storedV2 by prefs.signingDefaultV2.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = true)
    val storedV3 by prefs.signingDefaultV3.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = true)
    var v1 by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }
    var v2 by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }
    var v3 by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }
    var saved by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    LaunchedEffect(storedV1, storedV2, storedV3) {
        v1 = storedV1
        v2 = storedV2
        v3 = storedV3
    }
    ModalBottomSheet(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        containerColor = colors.surface,
        contentColor = colors.onSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 28.dp, topEnd = 28.dp)
    ) {
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 22.dp).padding(bottom = 28.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_signing), fontSize = 22.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp))
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_signing_intro), fontSize = 12.sp, lineHeight = 18.sp, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant)
            SigningSchemePreference("V1", stringResource(R.string.settings_signing_v1_desc), v1) { v1 = it; saved = false }
            SigningSchemePreference("V2", stringResource(R.string.settings_signing_v2_desc), v2) { v2 = it; saved = false }
            SigningSchemePreference("V3", stringResource(R.string.settings_signing_v3_desc), v3) { v3 = it; saved = false }
            if (!v2 && !v3) Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_signing_required), color = colors.error, fontSize = 11.sp)
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    scope.launch {
                        prefs.setSigningDefaults(v1, v2, v3)
                        saved = true
                    }
                },
                enabled = v2 || v3,
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
            ) { Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_signing_save)) }
            if (saved) Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_signing_saved), color = colors.primary, fontSize = 11.sp)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SigningSchemePreference(code: String, description: String, checked: Boolean, onCheckedChange: (Boolean) -> Unit) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    Row(
        Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(15.dp)).background(colors.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.7f)).clickable { onCheckedChange(!checked) }.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Column(Modifier.weight(1f), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp)) {
            Text(code, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            Text(description, fontSize = 11.sp, lineHeight = 16.sp, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant)
        }
        Checkbox(checked = checked, onCheckedChange = onCheckedChange)
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun ActivityLogSheet(onDismiss: () -> Unit) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    var activityItems by remember { mutableStateOf<List<ActivityLogItem>>(emptyList()) }
    var activityLoading by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }
    LaunchedEffect(context) {
        activityItems = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val reports = ApkReportHistoryStore(context).getReports()
                .filterNot { it.operationType.equals("SIGNING", true) }
                .map {
                    ActivityLogItem(
                        title = it.appName.ifBlank { it.fileName },
                        subtitle = it.packageName.ifBlank { it.fileName },
                        timestamp = it.inspectedAt,
                        signing = false,
                        success = it.verified
                    )
                }
            val signatures = KeyStoreRepository(context).allSigningActivity().map {
                ActivityLogItem(
                    title = it.record.apkName,
                    subtitle = "SHA-256 · ${it.certificateFingerprint.take(12).uppercase(Locale.ROOT)}",
                    timestamp = it.record.timestamp,
                    signing = true,
                    success = it.record.success
                )
            }
            (reports + signatures).sortedByDescending { it.timestamp }.take(50)
        }
        activityLoading = false
    }
    ModalBottomSheet(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        containerColor = colors.surface,
        contentColor = colors.onSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 28.dp, topEnd = 28.dp)
    ) {
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()).padding(horizontal = 22.dp).padding(bottom = 28.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(9.dp)) {
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_activity), fontSize = 22.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp))
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_activity_intro), color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 12.sp, lineHeight = 18.sp)
            if (activityLoading) {
                Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 28.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                    CircularProgressIndicator(Modifier.size(24.dp), strokeWidth = 2.dp)
                }
            } else if (activityItems.isEmpty()) {
                Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 28.dp), horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally, verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.History, null, tint = colors.onSurfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.55f), modifier = Modifier.size(34.dp))
                    Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_activity_empty), color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 12.sp)
                }
            } else {
                activityItems.forEach { item ->
                    ActivityItem(item)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

private data class ActivityLogItem(
    val title: String,
    val subtitle: String,
    val timestamp: Long,
    val signing: Boolean,
    val success: Boolean
)

@Composable
private fun ActivityItem(item: ActivityLogItem) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    val arabic = androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalConfiguration.current.locales[0].language == "ar"
    val date = remember(item.timestamp, arabic) {
        SimpleDateFormat(if (arabic) "yyyy/MM/dd · HH:mm" else "MMM d, yyyy · HH:mm", if (arabic) Locale("ar") else Locale.US).format(Date(item.timestamp))
    }
    val signing = item.signing
    Row(
        Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(15.dp)).background(colors.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.7f)).padding(13.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Box(Modifier.size(36.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(11.dp)).background(colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.12f)), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            Icon(if (signing) Icons.Filled.FactCheck else Icons.Filled.History, null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(19.dp))
        }
        Column(Modifier.weight(1f).padding(start = 11.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp)) {
            Text(item.title, fontSize = 13.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
            Text(item.subtitle, fontSize = 10.sp, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
            Text(date, fontSize = 10.sp, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant)
        }
        Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.End, verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
            Text(
                text = stringResource(if (signing) R.string.settings_activity_sign else R.string.settings_activity_inspect),
                color = colors.primary,
                fontSize = 10.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
            )
            Text(
                text = stringResource(
                    when {
                        signing && item.success -> R.string.settings_activity_success
                        signing && !item.success -> R.string.settings_activity_failed
                        item.success -> R.string.settings_activity_verified
                        else -> R.string.settings_activity_not_verified
                    }
                ),
                color = if (item.success) colors.primary else colors.error,
                fontSize = 9.sp
            )
        }
    }
}

private fun calculateCacheSize(directory: File): Long = runCatching {
    directory.walkTopDown().filter { it.isFile }.sumOf { it.length() }
}.getOrDefault(0L)

private fun formatBytes(bytes: Long): String = when {
    bytes < 1024L -> "$bytes B"
    bytes < 1024L * 1024L -> "%.1f KB".format(Locale.US, bytes / 1024.0)
    else -> "%.1f MB".format(Locale.US, bytes / (1024.0 * 1024.0))
}

@Composable
fun SettingsScreen(
    themeMode: String,
    language: String,

    onAccentColorChange: (String) -> Unit,

) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme

        "ar" -> stringResource(R.string.settings_language_arabic)

    }

            color = colors.onBackground,
            fontSize = 32.sp,

            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 0.dp)
        )
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.settings_intro),
            color = colors.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.58f),
            fontSize = 13.sp,
            lineHeight = 20.sp,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 7.dp, bottom = 30.dp)
        )

                title = stringResource(R.string.settings_theme),

                onClick = { activeDialog = "accent" }
            )
            SettingsDivider()
            SettingsRow(
                title = stringResource(R.string.settings_language),
                subtitle = stringResource(R.string.settings_language_subtitle),
                value = languageLabel,
                icon = Icons.Filled.Language,
                onClick = { activeDialog = "language" }
            )
        }

        SettingsGroup(
            title = stringResource(R.string.settings_group_protection),
            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 30.dp)

@Composable
private fun SettingsGroup(
    title: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    Column(modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
        Text(
            text = title.uppercase(),
            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary,
            fontSize = 10.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            letterSpacing = 1.35.sp,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 9.dp)
        )
        content()
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsRow(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    icon: ImageVector,
    value: String? = null,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(vertical = 14.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = icon,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = colors.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.76f),
            modifier = Modifier.size(21.dp)
        )
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .padding(start = 15.dp, end = 8.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
        ) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                color = colors.onBackground,
                fontSize = 14.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
            )
            Text(
                text = subtitle,
                color = colors.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.52f),
                fontSize = 11.sp,
                lineHeight = 16.sp,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 3.dp)
            )
        }
        if (value != null) {
            Text(
                text = value,
                color = colors.primary,
                fontSize = 11.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
        }
        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Filled.ChevronRight,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = colors.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.35f),
            modifier = Modifier.size(19.dp)
        )
    }
}

@Composable

    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(start = 36.dp)
            .height(0.7.dp)
            .background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.09f))
    )
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
private fun ChoiceSheet(
    title: String,
    selected: String,
    options: List<Pair<String, String>>,
    onSelect: (String) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    ModalBottomSheet(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        containerColor = colors.surface,
        contentColor = colors.onSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 28.dp, topEnd = 28.dp),
        dragHandle = {
            Box(
                Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
                    .size(width = 36.dp, height = 4.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.24f))
            )
        }
    ) {
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 22.dp).padding(bottom = 28.dp)) {
            Text(title, fontSize = 21.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 10.dp, bottom = 18.dp))
            options.forEachIndexed { index, (value, label) ->
                val isSelected = selected == value
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                        .background(if (isSelected) colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.09f) else Color.Transparent)
                        .border(if (isSelected) 1.dp else 0.dp, if (isSelected) colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.55f) else Color.Transparent, RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                        .clickable { onSelect(value) }
                        .padding(horizontal = 15.dp, vertical = 15.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    if (title == stringResource(R.string.settings_theme)) {
                        ThemePreview(value = value, selected = isSelected)
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
                    }
                    Text(label, fontSize = 15.sp, fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.SemiBold else FontWeight.Medium, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                    if (isSelected) {
                        Box(Modifier.size(22.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(colors.primary), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.Check, contentDescription = null, tint = colors.onPrimary, modifier = Modifier.size(12.dp))
                        }
                    } else {
                        Box(Modifier.size(22.dp).clip(CircleShape).border(1.5.dp, colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.24f), CircleShape))
                    }
                }
                if (index != options.lastIndex) Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
            }
        }
    }
}


@Composable
private fun ThemePreview(value: String, selected: Boolean) {
    val isLight = when (value) {
        AppPrefs.THEME_LIGHT -> true
        AppPrefs.THEME_DARK -> false
        else -> !androidx.compose.foundation.isSystemInDarkTheme()
    }
    val base = if (isLight) Color(0xFFF2F2F4) else Color(0xFF24242A)
    val foreground = if (isLight) Color(0xFF24242A) else Color(0xFFE9E9ED)
    val accent = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary
    Box(
        Modifier.size(width = 54.dp, height = 42.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)).background(base)
            .border(1.dp, foreground.copy(alpha = 0.10f), RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)).padding(7.dp)
    ) {
        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
            Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(0.6f).height(4.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(foreground.copy(alpha = 0.75f)))
            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp)) {
                Box(Modifier.weight(1f).height(12.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp)).background(foreground.copy(alpha = 0.10f)))
                Box(Modifier.weight(1f).height(12.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp)).background(if (selected) accent.copy(alpha = 0.55f) else foreground.copy(alpha = 0.14f)))
            }
        }
        if (value == AppPrefs.THEME_SYSTEM) {
            Box(Modifier.align(Alignment.BottomEnd).size(11.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(Color(0xFF77777F)))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun accentLabel(id: String): String = when (id) {
    AppPrefs.ACCENT_PURPLE -> stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_purple)
    AppPrefs.ACCENT_BLUE -> stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_blue)
    AppPrefs.ACCENT_MINT -> stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_mint)
    AppPrefs.ACCENT_TEAL -> stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_teal)
    AppPrefs.ACCENT_ORANGE -> stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_orange)
    AppPrefs.ACCENT_ROSE -> stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_rose)
    AppPrefs.ACCENT_RED -> stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_red)
    else -> stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_default)
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
private fun AccentColorSheet(
    selected: String,
    themeMode: String,
    onSelect: (String) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    val dark = when (themeMode) {
        AppPrefs.THEME_DARK -> true
        AppPrefs.THEME_LIGHT -> false
        else -> androidx.compose.foundation.isSystemInDarkTheme()
    }
    val options = listOf(
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_DEFAULT to stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_default),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_PURPLE to stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_purple),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_BLUE to stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_blue),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_MINT to stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_mint),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_TEAL to stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_teal),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_ORANGE to stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_orange),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_ROSE to stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_rose),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_RED to stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_red)
    )
    ModalBottomSheet(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        containerColor = colors.surface,
        contentColor = colors.onSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 28.dp, topEnd = 28.dp),
        dragHandle = {
            Box(Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp, bottom = 8.dp).size(width = 36.dp, height = 4.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.24f)))
        }
    ) {
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 22.dp).padding(bottom = 28.dp)) {
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_accent), fontSize = 21.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 10.dp))
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_subtitle), color = colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.58f), fontSize = 12.sp, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 5.dp, bottom = 20.dp))
            options.chunked(4).forEach { rowOptions ->
                Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    rowOptions.forEach { (id, label) ->
                        val isSelected = selected == id
                        val tint = KeyForgeColors.accent(id, dark)
                        Column(
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)).clickable { onSelect(id) }.padding(vertical = 9.dp),
                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                        ) {
                            Box(
                                Modifier.size(44.dp)
                                    .border(if (isSelected) 2.dp else 1.dp, if (isSelected) colors.primary else colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.12f), CircleShape)
                                    .padding(4.dp)
                                    .clip(CircleShape)
                                    .background(tint),
                                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                            ) {
                                if (isSelected) Icon(Icons.Filled.Check, contentDescription = null, tint = KeyForgeColors.onAccent(id, dark), modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                            }
                            Text(label, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.SemiBold else FontWeight.Normal, color = if (isSelected) colors.onSurface else colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.62f), maxLines = 1)
                        }
                    }
                    repeat(4 - rowOptions.size) { Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f)) }
                }
            }
            Box(
                Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 18.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)).background(colors.surfaceVariant).padding(14.dp)
            ) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                    Box(Modifier.size(9.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(KeyForgeColors.accent(selected, dark)))
                    Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_preview), color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 12.sp)
                    Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                    Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_accent_preview_action), color = colors.primary, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                }
            }
            TextButton(onClick = onDismiss, modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.End).padding(top = 8.dp)) {
                Text(stringResource(R.string.nav_info_done))
            }
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
private fun FilePreferencesSheet(onDismiss: () -> Unit) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val prefs = remember(context) { AppPrefs(context) }
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    val folderValue by prefs.exportFolderUri.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = null)
    val storedPattern by prefs.exportNamePattern.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = AppPrefs.EXPORT_NAME_PATTERN_DEFAULT)
    val saveMode by prefs.exportSaveMode.collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = AppPrefs.EXPORT_SAVE_ASK)
    var patternDraft by remember(storedPattern) { mutableStateOf(storedPattern) }
    val folderUri = folderValue?.let { Uri.parse(it) }
    val folderPicker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocumentTree()) { uri ->
        if (uri != null) {
            FileExportManager.persistFolderPermission(context, uri)
            scope.launch { prefs.setExportFolderUri(uri.toString()) }
        }
    }
    val folderName = folderUri?.let { FileExportManager.folderLabel(it) }
    val previewName = FileExportManager.buildFileName(
        pattern = patternDraft,
        name = "MyApp",
        version = "1.0.0",
        packageName = "com.example.app",
        extension = "pdf"
    )
    ModalBottomSheet(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        containerColor = colors.surface,
        contentColor = colors.onSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 28.dp, topEnd = 28.dp),
        dragHandle = {
            Box(Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp, bottom = 8.dp).size(width = 36.dp, height = 4.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.24f)))
        }
    ) {
        Column(
            Modifier.fillMaxWidth().verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()).padding(horizontal = 22.dp).padding(bottom = 28.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(13.dp)
        ) {
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_file_preferences), fontSize = 21.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 10.dp))
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_file_preferences_subtitle), color = colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.58f), fontSize = 12.sp)

            Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)).background(colors.surfaceVariant).padding(15.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.FolderOpen, contentDescription = null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                    Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_export_folder), fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, fontSize = 14.sp, modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 9.dp).weight(1f))
                    if (folderUri != null) {
                        TextButton(onClick = {
                            runCatching {
                                context.contentResolver.releasePersistableUriPermission(
                                    folderUri,
                                    android.content.Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or android.content.Intent.FLAG_GRANT_WRITE_URI_PERMISSION
                                )
                            }
                            scope.launch { prefs.setExportFolderUri(null) }
                        }) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.DeleteOutline, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp))
                        }
                    }
                }
                Text(
                    text = folderName?.let { stringResource(R.string.settings_export_folder_selected, it) } ?: stringResource(R.string.settings_export_folder_none),
                    color = colors.onSurfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.75f),
                    fontSize = 12.sp
                )
                Button(
                    onClick = { folderPicker.launch(folderUri) },
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(13.dp)
                ) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.FolderOpen, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp))
                    Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_choose_folder), modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp))
                }
            }

            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_save_behavior), fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, fontSize = 14.sp)
            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(9.dp)) {
                FileModeOption(
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_save_ask),
                    selected = saveMode == AppPrefs.EXPORT_SAVE_ASK,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                ) { scope.launch { prefs.setExportSaveMode(AppPrefs.EXPORT_SAVE_ASK) } }
                FileModeOption(
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_save_direct),
                    selected = saveMode == AppPrefs.EXPORT_SAVE_DIRECT,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                ) { scope.launch { prefs.setExportSaveMode(AppPrefs.EXPORT_SAVE_DIRECT) } }
            }
            if (saveMode == AppPrefs.EXPORT_SAVE_DIRECT && folderUri == null) {
                Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_direct_folder_required), color = colors.error, fontSize = 11.sp, lineHeight = 16.sp)
            }

            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_file_pattern), fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, fontSize = 14.sp)
            Column(
                Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)).background(colors.surfaceVariant).padding(13.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
            ) {
                Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_file_pattern_explainer), color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 11.sp, lineHeight = 17.sp)
                PatternTokenHelp("{name}", stringResource(R.string.settings_pattern_name_desc))
                PatternTokenHelp("{app}", stringResource(R.string.settings_pattern_app_alias))
                PatternTokenHelp("{version}", stringResource(R.string.settings_pattern_version_desc))
                PatternTokenHelp("{date}", stringResource(R.string.settings_pattern_date_desc))
                PatternTokenHelp("{time}", stringResource(R.string.settings_pattern_time_desc))
                PatternTokenHelp("{package}", stringResource(R.string.settings_pattern_package_desc))
                Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_pattern_example), color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_pattern_example_result), color = colors.primary, fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
            }
            OutlinedTextField(
                value = patternDraft,
                onValueChange = { patternDraft = it.take(100) },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                singleLine = true,
                label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_file_pattern_label)) },
                supportingText = { Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_file_pattern_help)) },
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
            )
            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(7.dp), modifier = Modifier.horizontalScroll(rememberScrollState())) {
                listOf("{name}", "{app}", "{version}", "{date}", "{time}", "{package}").forEach { token ->
                    Box(
                        Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)).background(colors.surfaceVariant).clickable {
                            patternDraft = appendPatternToken(patternDraft, token)
                        }.padding(horizontal = 9.dp, vertical = 7.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text(token, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 10.sp)
                    }
                }
            }
            Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)).background(colors.surfaceVariant).padding(13.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_file_pattern_preview), color = colors.onSurfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.65f), fontSize = 10.sp)
                Text(previewName, color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
            }
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    scope.launch {
                        prefs.setExportNamePattern(patternDraft.trim().ifBlank { AppPrefs.EXPORT_NAME_PATTERN_DEFAULT })
                        onDismiss()
                    }
                },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
            ) {
                Text(stringResource(R.string.nav_info_done))
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun PatternTokenHelp(token: String, description: String) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
        Box(
            Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(7.dp)).background(colors.surface).padding(horizontal = 7.dp, vertical = 5.dp)
        ) {
            Text(token, color = colors.primary, fontSize = 10.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
        }
        Text(description.substringAfter(" — ", description), color = colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 10.sp, modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 9.dp))
    }
}

private fun appendPatternToken(pattern: String, token: String): String {
    val current = pattern.trim()
    if (current.isBlank()) return token
    if (current.contains(token)) return current
    val separator = if (current.lastOrNull() in listOf('_', '-', '.')) "" else "_"
    return (current + separator + token).take(100)
}

@Composable
private fun FileModeOption(
    title: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    Box(
        modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(13.dp))
            .background(if (selected) colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.12f) else colors.surfaceVariant)
            .border(if (selected) 1.dp else 0.dp, if (selected) colors.primary.copy(alpha = 0.55f) else Color.Transparent, RoundedCornerShape(13.dp))
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = 9.dp, vertical = 13.dp),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Text(title, color = if (selected) colors.primary else colors.onSurfaceVariant, fontSize = 11.sp, fontWeight = if (selected) FontWeight.SemiBold else FontWeight.Medium, textAlign = androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign.Center)
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
private fun AboutDialog(onDismiss: () -> Unit) {
    val uriHandler = LocalUriHandler.current
    val colors = MaterialTheme.colorScheme
    ModalBottomSheet(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        containerColor = colors.surface,
        contentColor = colors.onSurface,
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 28.dp, topEnd = 28.dp),
        dragHandle = {
            Box(Modifier.padding(top = 12.dp, bottom = 8.dp).size(width = 36.dp, height = 4.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.22f)))
        }
    ) {
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 24.dp).padding(bottom = 28.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(13.dp)) {
            Text("KeyForge", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 24.sp, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 10.dp))
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_about_tagline), color = colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.58f), fontSize = 12.sp)
            AboutValue(stringResource(R.string.settings_version), BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME)
            AboutValue(stringResource(R.string.settings_developer), "ShueibHub")
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clickable { uriHandler.openUri("https://www.youtube.com/@ShueibHub") }, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Icon(Icons.Filled.PlayCircleFilled, contentDescription = null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(19.dp))
                Text("YouTube · @ShueibHub", color = colors.primary, fontSize = 13.sp, modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 10.dp))
            }
            Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clickable { uriHandler.openUri("mailto:ShueibHub@gmail.com") }, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Icon(Icons.Filled.Email, contentDescription = null, tint = colors.primary, modifier = Modifier.size(19.dp))
                Text("ShueibHub@gmail.com", color = colors.primary, fontSize = 13.sp, modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 10.dp))
            }
            Text(stringResource(R.string.settings_about_technology), color = colors.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.55f), fontSize = 11.sp, lineHeight = 17.sp)

                Text(stringResource(R.string.nav_info_done), color = colors.onPrimary, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
            }
        }
    }
}

    }
}


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/ui/theme/Color.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.theme

import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import com.forgehub.keyforge.data.AppPrefs

data class AccentPalette(
    val dark: Color,
    val light: Color,
    val onDark: Color = Color(0xFF101114),
    val onLight: Color = Color(0xFFFFFFFF)
)

object KeyForgeColors {
    val DarkBackground = Color(0xFF000000)
    val DarkText = Color(0xFFFFFFFF)
    val LightBackground = Color(0xFFFFFFFF)
    val LightText = Color(0xFF000000)

    val Error = Color(0xFFEF4444)
    val Warning = Color(0xFFFFCD19)
    val Success = Color(0xFF03FF35)

    private val accents = mapOf(
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_DEFAULT to AccentPalette(Color(0xFF02FA8B), Color(0xFF9E29F2), Color(0xFF001B10), Color(0xFFFFFFFF)),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_PURPLE to AccentPalette(Color(0xFFB79CFF), Color(0xFF7542C8), Color(0xFF1C1230), Color(0xFFFFFFFF)),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_BLUE to AccentPalette(Color(0xFF78B9FF), Color(0xFF256BC2), Color(0xFF07182B), Color(0xFFFFFFFF)),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_MINT to AccentPalette(Color(0xFF58D9AE), Color(0xFF087D5B), Color(0xFF06241A), Color(0xFFFFFFFF)),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_TEAL to AccentPalette(Color(0xFF58D4D4), Color(0xFF087F83), Color(0xFF082526), Color(0xFFFFFFFF)),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_ORANGE to AccentPalette(Color(0xFFFFB66E), Color(0xFFC26714), Color(0xFF2B1808), Color(0xFFFFFFFF)),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_ROSE to AccentPalette(Color(0xFFFF91AE), Color(0xFFC43D68), Color(0xFF2B0D18), Color(0xFFFFFFFF)),
        AppPrefs.ACCENT_RED to AccentPalette(Color(0xFFFF7777), Color(0xFFC43737), Color(0xFF2B0909), Color(0xFFFFFFFF))
    )

    fun accent(id: String, darkTheme: Boolean): Color {
        val palette = accents[id] ?: accents.getValue(AppPrefs.ACCENT_DEFAULT)
        return if (darkTheme) palette.dark else palette.light
    }

    fun onAccent(id: String, darkTheme: Boolean): Color {
        val palette = accents[id] ?: accents.getValue(AppPrefs.ACCENT_DEFAULT)
        return if (darkTheme) palette.onDark else palette.onLight
    }
}


========== FILE: ./app/src/main/java/com/forgehub/keyforge/ui/theme/Theme.kt ==========
package com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.theme

import androidx.compose.foundation.isSystemInDarkTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.darkColorScheme
import androidx.compose.material3.lightColorScheme
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import com.forgehub.keyforge.data.AppPrefs

@Composable
fun KeyForgeTheme(
    themeMode: String = AppPrefs.THEME_SYSTEM,
    accentColor: String = AppPrefs.ACCENT_DEFAULT,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val darkTheme = when (themeMode) {
        AppPrefs.THEME_DARK -> true
        AppPrefs.THEME_LIGHT -> false
        else -> isSystemInDarkTheme()
    }
    val accent = KeyForgeColors.accent(accentColor, darkTheme)
    val onAccent = KeyForgeColors.onAccent(accentColor, darkTheme)
    val scheme = if (darkTheme) {
        darkColorScheme(
            primary = accent,
            onPrimary = onAccent,
            secondary = accent,
            onSecondary = onAccent,
            tertiary = accent,
            onTertiary = onAccent,
            background = KeyForgeColors.DarkBackground,
            onBackground = KeyForgeColors.DarkText,
            surface = KeyForgeColors.DarkBackground,
            onSurface = KeyForgeColors.DarkText,
            surfaceVariant = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFF17191D),
            onSurfaceVariant = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFFE0E2E8),
            error = KeyForgeColors.Error,
            onError = KeyForgeColors.DarkText
        )
    } else {
        lightColorScheme(
            primary = accent,
            onPrimary = onAccent,
            secondary = accent,
            onSecondary = onAccent,
            tertiary = accent,
            onTertiary = onAccent,
            background = KeyForgeColors.LightBackground,
            onBackground = KeyForgeColors.LightText,
            surface = KeyForgeColors.LightBackground,
            onSurface = KeyForgeColors.LightText,
            surfaceVariant = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFFF1F2F5),
            onSurfaceVariant = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color(0xFF393B43),
            error = KeyForgeColors.Error,
            onError = KeyForgeColors.LightText
        )
    }
    MaterialTheme(
        colorScheme = scheme,
        typography = KeyForgeTypography,
        content = content
    )
}

package com.forgehub.keyforge.ui.theme

import androidx.compose.material3.Typography
import androidx.compose.ui.text.TextStyle
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.Font
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontFamily
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import com.forgehub.keyforge.R

val KeyForgeFont = FontFamily(
    Font(
        resId = R.font.keyforgefont,
        weight = FontWeight.Normal
    )
)

val KeyForgeTypography = Typography(
    displayLarge = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),
    displayMedium = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),
    displaySmall = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),

    headlineLarge = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),
    headlineMedium = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),
    headlineSmall = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),

    titleLarge = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),
    titleMedium = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),
    titleSmall = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),

    bodyLarge = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),
    bodyMedium = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),
    bodySmall = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),

    labelLarge = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),
    labelMedium = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont),
    labelSmall = TextStyle(fontFamily = KeyForgeFont)
)
